00066Fr 2               
00066Fr 2               @bad:
00066Fr 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
000671r 2  38                       sec
000672r 2  60                       rts
000673r 2               
000673r 2               SER_READ:
000673r 2  C0 00                    cpy         #SER_FID_CONS
000675r 2  D0 04                    bne         :+                                  ; /dev/ser: any task
000677r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_SER_CAPTURE
000679r 2  D0 50                    bne         @wait                               ; /dev/cons: only the foreground task
00067Br 2               :
00067Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
00067Er 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000680r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000682r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes wanted (1-256; 256 = 0)
000684r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
000686r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Bytes done (the ring holds 255 at most)
000688r 2               
000688r 2               @next:
000688r 2  A4 FE                    ldy         SER_RX_TAIL
00068Ar 2  C4 FF                    cpy         SER_RX_HEAD
00068Cr 2  F0 10                    beq         @empty
00068Er 2  B9 00 02                 lda         SER_RX_BUF,Y
000691r 2  E6 FE                    inc         SER_RX_TAIL
000693r 2  48                       pha
000694r 2  8A                       txa
000695r 2  A8                       tay
000696r 2  68                       pla
000697r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000699r 2  E8                       inx
00069Ar 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_IO_TMP
00069Cr 2  D0 EA                    bne         @next
00069Er 2               
00069Er 2               @empty:
00069Er 2  8A                       txa
00069Fr 2  D0 1A                    bne         @done
0006A1r 2  08                       php                                             ; Nothing: check again with IRQs off, so a
0006A2r 2  78                       sei                                             ;   byte can't slip in before we're waiting
0006A3r 2  A5 FF                    lda         SER_RX_HEAD
0006A5r 2  C5 FE                    cmp         SER_RX_TAIL
0006A7r 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
0006A9r 2  28                       plp                                             ; It just came in
0006AAr 2  80 DC                    bra         @next
0006ACr 2               :
0006ACr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0006AEr 2  A0 FA                    ldy         #SER_RD_WAIT
0006B0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ADD_WAIT
0006B3r 2  28                       plp
0006B4r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
0006B6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0006B9r 2  80 18                    bra         SER_WOULD_BLOCK
0006BBr 2               
0006BBr 2               @done:
0006BBr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
0006BDr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; The count read
0006BFr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0006C1r 2  C8                       iny
0006C2r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0006C4r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0006C6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0006C9r 2  80 84                    bra         SER_OK
0006CBr 2               
0006CBr 2               @wait:                                                      ; Not in the foreground: wait until it is
0006CBr 2  08                       php
0006CCr 2  78                       sei
0006CDr 2  A0 FA                    ldy         #SER_RD_WAIT
0006CFr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ADD_WAIT
0006D2r 2  28                       plp
0006D3r 2               
0006D3r 2               SER_WOULD_BLOCK:
0006D3r 2  A9 73                    lda         #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
0006D5r 2  38                       sec
0006D6r 2  60                       rts
0006D7r 2               
0006D7r 2               SER_WRITE:
0006D7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
0006DAr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
0006DCr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0006DEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes offered (1-256; 256 = 0)
0006E0r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
0006E2r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Bytes taken
0006E4r 2               
0006E4r 2               @next:
0006E4r 2  8A                       txa
0006E5r 2  A8                       tay
0006E6r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0006E8r 2  08                       php
0006E9r 2  78                       sei
0006EAr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0006ECr 2  D0 07                    bne         @queue                              ; Busy: the TX IRQ sends it
0006EEr 2  8D rr rr                 IO_PORT_WRITE   ACIA_R_DATA                     ; Idle (so the ring is empty): send it now
0006F1r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS                  ; SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
0006F3r 2  80 0C                    bra         @taken
0006F5r 2               
0006F5r 2               @queue:
0006F5r 2  A4 FD                    ldy         SER_TX_HEAD
0006F7r 2  99 00 03                 sta         SER_TX_BUF,Y
0006FAr 2  C8                       iny
0006FBr 2  C4 FC                    cpy         SER_TX_TAIL
0006FDr 2  F0 10                    beq         @full                               ; (The byte stored isn't counted: head stays)
0006FFr 2  84 FD                    sty         SER_TX_HEAD
000701r 2               
000701r 2               @taken:
000701r 2  28                       plp
000702r 2  E8                       inx
000703r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_IO_TMP
000705r 2  D0 DD                    bne         @next
000707r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; All of it: the count stays
000709r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00070Cr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SER_OK
00070Fr 2               
00070Fr 2               @full:
00070Fr 2  8A                       txa
000710r 2  D0 0F                    bne         @short
000712r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK                         ; Nothing fits: wait for room (IRQs are
000714r 2  A0 F8                    ldy         #SER_WR_WAIT                        ;   still off, so the TX IRQ can't slip in)
000716r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ADD_WAIT
000719r 2  28                       plp
00071Ar 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
00071Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00071Fr 2  80 B2                    bra         SER_WOULD_BLOCK
000721r 2               
000721r 2               @short:
000721r 2  28                       plp
000722r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000724r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; The count taken
000726r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000728r 2  C8                       iny
000729r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
00072Br 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
00072Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000730r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SER_OK
000733r 2               
000733r 2               ; Add a task to a wait mask (SER_RD_WAIT or SER_WR_WAIT).  IRQs must be off.
000733r 2               ; IN: .X = task, .Y = the mask's ZP address.  Modifies: .A, .Y
000733r 2               SER_ADD_WAIT:
000733r 2  DA                       phx
000734r 2  8A                       txa
000735r 2  29 07                    and         #7
000737r 2  AA                       tax
000738r 2  BD rr rr                 lda         P2_BIT_MASKS,X
00073Br 2  FA                       plx
00073Cr 2  E0 08                    cpx         #8
00073Er 2  90 01                    bcc         :+
000740r 2  C8                       iny                                             ; Tasks 8-15: the mask's high byte
000741r 2               :
000741r 2  19 00 00                 ora         a:$0000,Y
000744r 2  99 00 00                 sta         a:$0000,Y
000747r 2  60                       rts
000748r 2               
000748r 1               .include "snd_srv.s"    ; The sound driver's file server (/dev/snd)
000748r 2               .debuginfo
000748r 2               
000748r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000748r 2               ; The sound driver's file server: /dev/snd.  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see
000748r 2               ; all.s); the driver itself is sound.s.  The serve routine runs in the sound task (its page 0 gate is in
000748r 2               ; sound.s).
000748r 2               ;   Write: YM2151 register/value byte pairs (an odd last byte is left over); if the chip doesn't
000748r 2               ;          respond, the pairs written so far count, or ERR_IO_DEVICE if none.
000748r 2               ;   Read: end of file.  Ctl: SND_CTL_INIT, SND_CTL_TEST.  Stat: all zero.
000748r 2               ; Server ZP (in the sound task): ZP_IO_TMP = pair bytes offered.
000748r 2               
000748r 2               .segment "IO_P2"
000748r 2               
000748r 2               SND_SERVE:
000748r 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
00074Ar 2  F0 44                    beq         @write
00074Cr 2  C9 02                    cmp         #H9_READ
00074Er 2  F0 0F                    beq         @read
000750r 2  C9 06                    cmp         #H9_CTL
000752r 2  F0 1C                    beq         @ctl
000754r 2  C9 05                    cmp         #H9_STAT
000756r 2  D0 03                    bne         @ok                                 ; H9_OPEN (fid 0), H9_CLUNK, H9_DUP
000758r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STAT_ZERO
00075Br 2               
00075Br 2               @ok:
00075Br 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
00075Dr 2  18                       clc
00075Er 2  60                       rts
00075Fr 2               
00075Fr 2               @read:                                                      ; Nothing to read: end of file
00075Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000762r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000764r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000766r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000768r 2  C8                       iny
000769r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
00076Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00076Er 2  80 EB                    bra         @ok
000770r 2               
000770r 2               @ctl:
000770r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000773r 2  A0 0B                    ldy         #IO_BLK_CTL_CODE
000775r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000777r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00077Ar 2  C9 01                    cmp         #SND_CTL_INIT
00077Cr 2  D0 05                    bne         :+
00077Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SOUND_INIT
000781r 2  80 D8                    bra         @ok
000783r 2               :
000783r 2  C9 02                    cmp         #SND_CTL_TEST
000785r 2  D0 05                    bne         @bad
000787r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SOUND_TEST
00078Ar 2  80 CF                    bra         @ok
00078Cr 2               
00078Cr 2               @bad:
00078Cr 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
00078Er 2  38                       sec
00078Fr 2  60                       rts
000790r 2               
000790r 2               @write:
000790r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000793r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000795r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y                       ; Bytes offered (1-256: 256 = low 0, high 1)
000797r 2  29 FE                    and         #$FE                                ; The count taken: whole pairs (an odd
000799r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y                       ;   last byte is left over)
00079Br 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP
00079Dr 2  C8                       iny
00079Er 2  11 rr                    ora         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0007A0r 2  F0 16                    beq         @done                               ; Just 1 byte: nothing taken
0007A2r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
0007A4r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0007A6r 2               
0007A6r 2               @pair:
0007A6r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0007A8r 2  AA                       tax                                             ; .X = register
0007A9r 2  C8                       iny
0007AAr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y                       ; .A = value
0007ACr 2  C8                       iny
0007ADr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         YM_WRITE
0007B0r 2  B0 0B                    bcs         @timeout
0007B2r 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_TMP
0007B4r 2  D0 F0                    bne         @pair
0007B6r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
0007B8r 2               
0007B8r 2               @done:
0007B8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0007BBr 2  80 9E                    bra         @ok
0007BDr 2               
0007BDr 2               @timeout:
0007BDr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
0007BFr 2  88                       dey                                             ; This pair didn't go
0007C0r 2  88                       dey
0007C1r 2  D0 07                    bne         @short
0007C3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0007C6r 2  A9 79                    lda         #ERR_IO_DEVICE
0007C8r 2  38                       sec
0007C9r 2  60                       rts
0007CAr 2               
0007CAr 2               @short:
0007CAr 2  98                       tya
0007CBr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
0007CDr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0007CFr 2  C8                       iny
0007D0r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0007D2r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0007D4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0007D7r 2  80 82                    bra         @ok
0007D9r 2               
0007D9r 1               .include "pipe_srv.s"   ; The pipe server (/dev/pipe)
0007D9r 2               .debuginfo
0007D9r 2               
0007D9r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0007D9r 2               ; The pipe server: /dev/pipe.  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see all.s); it runs in
0007D9r 2               ; its own Resident task (PIPE_TASK_NUM; PIPE_INIT and the gate are in io_p0.s), which holds the pipe
0007D9r 2               ; table and the rings (PIPE_TABLE, PIPE_BUFS).  Requests run one at a time (TASK_CALL: no task switch
0007D9r 2               ; while the server runs), and no IRQ handler touches the pipes, so no locking is needed.
0007D9r 2               ;
0007D9r 2               ;   Opening /dev/pipe makes a new pipe; the fid is its index.  IO_PIPE opens the read end and adds a
0007D9r 2               ;   write end.  Each request carries the fd's mode (IO_BLK_MODE), so the server counts the fds on each
0007D9r 2               ;   end: H9_OPEN and H9_DUP add one, H9_CLUNK takes one away; the pipe is free when both counts are 0.
0007D9r 2               ;   Read: what's in the ring, up to the count; if it's empty, the reader waits, or gets end of file if
0007D9r 2               ;         there are no writers left.
0007D9r 2               ;   Write: as much as fits; if nothing fits, the writer waits; ERR_IO_BROKEN if there are no readers.
0007D9r 2               ; Server ZP (in the pipe task): ZP_IO_CHUNK = client, ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1 = the pipe's table offset,
0007D9r 2               ; ZP_IO_OFS = its ring, ZP_IO_TMP = bytes asked for, ZP_IO_BYTE = bytes done, ZP_IO_LEFT = a wait mask.
0007D9r 2               
0007D9r 2               .segment "IO_P2"
0007D9r 2               
0007D9r 2               ; IN: .A = request, .X = client, .Y = fid
0007D9r 2               PIPE_SERVE:
0007D9r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0007DBr 2  C9 01                    cmp         #H9_OPEN
0007DDr 2  F0 3A                    beq         PIPE_OPEN
0007DFr 2  48                       pha
0007E0r 2  98                       tya
0007E1r 2  29 07                    and         #PIPE_MAX - 1
0007E3r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS + 1                       ; (Its ring: set up below)
0007E5r 2  0A                       asl
0007E6r 2  0A                       asl
0007E7r 2  0A                       asl
0007E8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1                     ; The pipe's table offset
0007EAr 2                           .assert     PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE = 8, error, "PIPE_SERVE: entry offset = fid * 8"
0007EAr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
0007ECr 2  18                       clc
0007EDr 2  69 03                    adc         #>PIPE_BUFS
0007EFr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
0007F1r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_OFS
0007F3r 2  68                       pla
0007F4r 2  C9 02                    cmp         #H9_READ
0007F6r 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
0007F8r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_READ
0007FBr 2               :
0007FBr 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
0007FDr 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
0007FFr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_WRITE
000802r 2               :
000802r 2  C9 07                    cmp         #H9_DUP
000804r 2  F0 3F                    beq         PIPE_DUP
000806r 2  C9 04                    cmp         #H9_CLUNK
000808r 2  F0 40                    beq         PIPE_CLUNK
00080Ar 2  C9 05                    cmp         #H9_STAT
00080Cr 2  D0 07                    bne         PIPE_BAD
00080Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STAT_ZERO
000811r 2               
000811r 2               PIPE_OK:
000811r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000813r 2  18                       clc
000814r 2  60                       rts
000815r 2               
000815r 2               PIPE_BAD:
000815r 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
000817r 2  38                       sec
000818r 2  60                       rts
000819r 2               
000819r 2               PIPE_OPEN:
000819r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; A free pipe
00081Br 2               
00081Br 2               @find:
00081Br 2  BD 00 02                 lda         PIPE_READERS,X
00081Er 2  1D 01 02                 ora         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000821r 2  F0 0D                    beq         @free
000823r 2  8A                       txa
000824r 2  18                       clc
000825r 2  69 08                    adc         #PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
000827r 2  AA                       tax
000828r 2  E0 40                    cpx         #PIPE_MAX * PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
00082Ar 2  D0 EF                    bne         @find
00082Cr 2  A9 7B                    lda         #ERR_IO_NO_PIPES
00082Er 2  38                       sec
00082Fr 2  60                       rts
000830r 2               
000830r 2               @free:
000830r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000832r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
000834r 2               
000834r 2               @clear:
000834r 2  9E 00 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE,X                        ; Empty, nobody waiting
000837r 2  E8                       inx
000838r 2  88                       dey
000839r 2  D0 F9                    bne         @clear
00083Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_ADD_FD                         ; The opener's end(s)
00083Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1                     ; fid = the pipe's index
000840r 2  4A                       lsr
000841r 2  4A                       lsr
000842r 2  4A                       lsr
000843r 2  18                       clc
000844r 2  60                       rts
000845r 2               
000845r 2               PIPE_DUP:
000845r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_ADD_FD
000848r 2  80 C7                    bra         PIPE_OK
00084Ar 2               
00084Ar 2               PIPE_CLUNK:
00084Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_MODE
00084Dr 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = read end
00084Er 2  90 0C                    bcc         @write_end
000850r 2  48                       pha
000851r 2  DE 00 02                 dec         PIPE_READERS,X
000854r 2  D0 05                    bne         :+
000856r 2  A0 06                    ldy         #PIPE_WR_WAIT                       ; No readers left: the writers find out
000858r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
00085Br 2               :
00085Br 2  68                       pla
00085Cr 2               
00085Cr 2               @write_end:
00085Cr 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = write end
00085Dr 2  90 B2                    bcc         PIPE_OK
00085Fr 2  DE 01 02                 dec         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000862r 2  D0 AD                    bne         PIPE_OK
000864r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #PIPE_RD_WAIT                       ; No writers left: the readers get end of file
000866r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
000869r 2  80 A6                    bra         PIPE_OK
00086Br 2               
00086Br 2               PIPE_READ:
00086Br 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
00086Dr 2  BD 02 02                 lda         PIPE_HEAD,X
000870r 2  DD 03 02                 cmp         PIPE_TAIL,X
000873r 2  D0 1B                    bne         @data
000875r 2  BD 01 02                 lda         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000878r 2  F0 05                    beq         @eof
00087Ar 2  A0 04                    ldy         #PIPE_RD_WAIT                       ; Empty: wait for a writer
00087Cr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_WAIT
00087Fr 2               
00087Fr 2               @eof:
00087Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000882r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000884r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000886r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000888r 2  C8                       iny
000889r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
00088Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00088Er 2  80 81                    bra         PIPE_OK
000890r 2               
000890r 2               @data:
000890r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000893r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000895r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000897r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes wanted (1-256; 256 = 0)
000899r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
00089Br 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_BYTE
00089Dr 2               
00089Dr 2               @next:
00089Dr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
00089Fr 2  BC 03 02                 ldy         PIPE_TAIL,X
0008A2r 2  98                       tya
0008A3r 2  DD 02 02                 cmp         PIPE_HEAD,X
0008A6r 2  F0 10                    beq         @end
0008A8r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y
0008AAr 2  FE 03 02                 inc         PIPE_TAIL,X
0008ADr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_BYTE
0008AFr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0008B1r 2  C8                       iny
0008B2r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_BYTE
0008B4r 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_TMP
0008B6r 2  D0 E5                    bne         @next
0008B8r 2               
0008B8r 2               @end:
0008B8r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
0008BAr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BYTE                          ; The count read (the ring holds 255 at most)
0008BCr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
0008BEr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0008C0r 2  C8                       iny
0008C1r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0008C3r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0008C5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0008C8r 2  A0 06                    ldy         #PIPE_WR_WAIT                       ; There's room now
0008CAr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
0008CDr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_OK
0008D0r 2               
0008D0r 2               PIPE_WRITE:
0008D0r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0008D2r 2  BD 00 02                 lda         PIPE_READERS,X
0008D5r 2  D0 04                    bne         :+
0008D7r 2  A9 7A                    lda         #ERR_IO_BROKEN                      ; Nobody will read it
0008D9r 2  38                       sec
0008DAr 2  60                       rts
0008DBr 2               :
0008DBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
0008DEr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
0008E0r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0008E2r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes offered (1-256; 256 = 0)
0008E4r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
0008E6r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_BYTE
0008E8r 2               
0008E8r 2               @next:
0008E8r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_BYTE
0008EAr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0008ECr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0008EEr 2  BC 02 02                 ldy         PIPE_HEAD,X
0008F1r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y                       ; (The slot at head is always free)
0008F3r 2  C8                       iny
0008F4r 2  98                       tya
0008F5r 2  DD 03 02                 cmp         PIPE_TAIL,X
0008F8r 2  F0 0F                    beq         @full
0008FAr 2  9D 02 02                 sta         PIPE_HEAD,X
0008FDr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_BYTE
0008FFr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BYTE
000901r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IO_TMP
000903r 2  D0 E3                    bne         @next
000905r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; All of it: the count stays
000907r 2  80 16                    bra         @taken
000909r 2               
000909r 2               @full:
000909r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
00090Br 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BYTE
00090Dr 2  D0 07                    bne         @short
00090Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP                        ; Nothing fits: wait for a reader
000912r 2  A0 06                    ldy         #PIPE_WR_WAIT
000914r 2  80 14                    bra         PIPE_WAIT
000916r 2               
000916r 2               @short:
000916r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; The count taken
000918r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
00091Ar 2  C8                       iny
00091Br 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
00091Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
00091Fr 2               
00091Fr 2               @taken:
00091Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000922r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #PIPE_RD_WAIT                       ; There's data now
000924r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
000927r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_OK
00092Ar 2               
00092Ar 2               ; The client waits: add it to a wait mask of the pipe, and tell the IO layer.  IN: .Y = PIPE_RD_WAIT or
00092Ar 2               ; PIPE_WR_WAIT
00092Ar 2               PIPE_WAIT:
00092Ar 2  98                       tya
00092Br 2  18                       clc
00092Cr 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
00092Er 2  AA                       tax                                             ; PIPE_TABLE,X = the mask
00092Fr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000931r 2  29 07                    and         #7
000933r 2  A8                       tay
000934r 2  B9 rr rr                 lda         P2_BIT_MASKS,Y
000937r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000939r 2  C0 08                    cpy         #8
00093Br 2  90 01                    bcc         :+
00093Dr 2  E8                       inx                                             ; Tasks 8-15: the mask's high byte
00093Er 2               :
00093Er 2  1D 00 02                 ora         PIPE_TABLE,X
000941r 2  9D 00 02                 sta         PIPE_TABLE,X
000944r 2  A9 73                    lda         #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
000946r 2  38                       sec
000947r 2  60                       rts
000948r 2               
000948r 2               ; Wake the tasks in a wait mask of the pipe, and clear it.  IN: .Y = PIPE_RD_WAIT or PIPE_WR_WAIT
000948r 2               ; OUT: .X = the pipe's table offset.  Modifies: .A, .Y
000948r 2               PIPE_WAKE:
000948r 2  98                       tya
000949r 2  18                       clc
00094Ar 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
00094Cr 2  AA                       tax
00094Dr 2  BD 00 02                 lda         PIPE_TABLE,X
000950r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
000952r 2  BD 01 02                 lda         PIPE_TABLE + 1,X
000955r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000957r 2  9E 00 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE,X
00095Ar 2  9E 01 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE + 1,X
00095Dr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
00095Fr 2               
00095Fr 2               @loop:
00095Fr 2  46 rr                    lsr         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000961r 2  66 rr                    ror         ZP_IO_LEFT
000963r 2  90 04                    bcc         :+
000965r 2  98                       tya
000966r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_WAKE
000969r 2               :
000969r 2  C8                       iny
00096Ar 2  C0 10                    cpy         #16
00096Cr 2  D0 F1                    bne         @loop
00096Er 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000970r 2  60                       rts
000971r 2               
000971r 2               ; Count a new fd on the pipe's end(s), from the request's mode.  OUT: .X = the pipe's table offset
000971r 2               PIPE_ADD_FD:
000971r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_MODE
000974r 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = read end
000975r 2  90 03                    bcc         :+
000977r 2  FE 00 02                 inc         PIPE_READERS,X
00097Ar 2               :
00097Ar 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = write end
00097Br 2  90 03                    bcc         :+
00097Dr 2  FE 01 02                 inc         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000980r 2               :
000980r 2  60                       rts
000981r 2               
000981r 2               ; The request's mode (the fd's).  OUT: .A = mode, .X = the pipe's table offset.  Modifies: .Y
000981r 2               PIPE_MODE:
000981r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000984r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #IO_BLK_MODE
000986r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000988r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00098Br 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
00098Dr 2  60                       rts
00098Er 2               
00098Er 2               ; IO_SRV_MAP for the client in ZP_IO_CHUNK.  Modifies: .X
00098Er 2               IO_SRV_MAP_C:
00098Er 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000990r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         IO_SRV_MAP
000993r 2               
000993r 1               .include "io_test.s"
000993r 2               .debuginfo
000993r 2               
000993r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000993r 2               ; IO self test (TH_IO_TEST, $F88A; from WOZMON: F88AR).  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2`
000993r 2               ; (see all.s).  Runs in the current task and prints "IO test: ok", or "IO test: FAIL x ee" (x = failing
000993r 2               ; step, ee = error code or value).  Uses /dev/null, /dev/zero and /dev/cons (it writes "cons "), and a
000993r 2               ; 300-byte buffer from the MMU.  The task's other fds (e.g. stdin, stdout, stderr) are left alone.
000993r 2               
000993r 2               .segment "IO_P2"
000993r 2               
000993r 2  09 49 4F 20  NamedHString    S_IO_TEST, "IO test: "
000997r 2  74 65 73 74  
00099Br 2  3A 20        
00099Dr 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_NULL:     .byte "/dev/null", 0
0009A1r 2  2F 6E 75 6C  
0009A5r 2  6C 00        
0009A7r 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_ZERO:     .byte "/dev/zero", 0
0009ABr 2  2F 7A 65 72  
0009AFr 2  6F 00        
0009B1r 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_ZERO_SUB: .byte "/dev/zero/sub", 0
0009B5r 2  2F 7A 65 72  
0009B9r 2  6F 2F 73 75  
0009BFr 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_NOTHERE:  .byte "/dev/nothere", 0
0009C3r 2  2F 6E 6F 74  
0009C7r 2  68 65 72 65  
0009CCr 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_ZEROO:    .byte "/dev/zeroo", 0
0009D0r 2  2F 7A 65 72  
0009D4r 2  6F 6F 00     
0009D7r 2  2F 66 6F 6F  S_NOT_DEV:      .byte "/foo", 0
0009DBr 2  00           
0009DCr 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_CONS_T:   .byte "/dev/cons", 0
0009E0r 2  2F 63 6F 6E  
0009E4r 2  73 00        
0009E6r 2  63 6F 6E 73  S_CONS_MSG:     .byte "cons "
0009EAr 2  20           
0009EBr 2               CONS_MSG_LEN    = * - S_CONS_MSG
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               IT_BUF_SIZE     = 300
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               .macro _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C  step                                ; Fail if the call returned an error
0009EBr 2                           bcc         :+
0009EBr 2                           ldx         #step
0009EBr 2                           jmp         @fail
0009EBr 2               :
0009EBr 2               .endmacro
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               .macro _M_IT_FAIL_IF_NC step, error                         ; Fail unless the call failed with this error
0009EBr 2                           bcs         :+
0009EBr 2                           lda         #0
0009EBr 2                           ldx         #step
0009EBr 2                           jmp         @fail
0009EBr 2               :
0009EBr 2                           cmp         #error
0009EBr 2                           beq         :+
0009EBr 2                           ldx         #step
0009EBr 2                           jmp         @fail
0009EBr 2               :
0009EBr 2               .endmacro
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               .macro _M_IT_OPEN       name, mode                          ; .A = fd
0009EBr 2                           lda         #<name
0009EBr 2                           ldy         #>name
0009EBr 2                           ldx         #mode
0009EBr 2                           jsr         IO_OPEN
0009EBr 2               .endmacro
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               .macro _M_IT_COUNT      fd, count                           ; Set up a transfer of count bytes to/from the buffer
0009EBr 2                           lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
0009EBr 2                           sta         ZP_IO_BUF
0009EBr 2                           lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
0009EBr 2                           sta         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
0009EBr 2                           lda         #<count
0009EBr 2                           sta         ZP_IO_CNT
0009EBr 2                           lda         #>count
0009EBr 2                           sta         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
0009EBr 2                           lda         fd
0009EBr 2               .endmacro
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               .macro _M_IT_EXPECT_CNT step, count                         ; Fail unless ZP_IO_CNT = count
0009EBr 2                           lda         ZP_IO_CNT
0009EBr 2                           cmp         #<count
0009EBr 2                           bne         :+
0009EBr 2                           lda         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
0009EBr 2                           cmp         #>count
0009EBr 2                           beq         :++
0009EBr 2               :
0009EBr 2                           lda         ZP_IO_CNT
0009EBr 2                           ldx         #step
0009EBr 2                           jmp         @fail
0009EBr 2               :
0009EBr 2               .endmacro
0009EBr 2               
0009EBr 2               IO_TEST:
0009EBr 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0009EEr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_IO_TEST
0009F2r 2  20 rr rr     
0009F5r 2  A9 2C                    lda         #<IT_BUF_SIZE                       ; The buffer (task RAM pages)
0009F7r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #>IT_BUF_SIZE
0009F9r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0009FBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ALLOC
0009FEr 2  90 05 A2 30              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     '0'
000A02r 2  4C rr rr     
000A05r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC4                        ; Its handle
000A07r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_LOCK                             ; .A.Y = address (page blocks don't move)
000A0Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000A0Cr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
000A0Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC4
000A10r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_UNLOCK
000A13r 2               
000A13r 2               ; /dev/null: writes take everything (split over two transfers), reads are empty
000A13r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_IT_OPEN  S_DEV_NULL, IO_MODE_RDWR
000A17r 2  A2 03 20 rr  
000A1Br 2  rr           
000A1Cr 2  90 05 A2 61              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'a'
000A20r 2  4C rr rr     
000A23r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP                             ; (The first free fd)
000A25r 2  A5 rr 85 rr              _M_IT_COUNT ZP_TEMP, IT_BUF_SIZE
000A29r 2  A5 rr 85 rr  
000A2Dr 2  A9 2C 85 rr  
000A37r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_WRITE
000A3Ar 2  90 05 A2 62              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'b'
000A3Er 2  4C rr rr     
000A41r 2  A5 rr C9 2C              _M_IT_EXPECT_CNT    'b', IT_BUF_SIZE
000A45r 2  D0 06 A5 rr  
000A49r 2  C9 01 F0 07  
000A54r 2  A5 rr 85 rr              _M_IT_COUNT ZP_TEMP, 10
000A58r 2  A5 rr 85 rr  
000A5Cr 2  A9 0A 85 rr  
000A66r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_READ
000A69r 2  90 05 A2 63              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'c'

000A78r 2  C9 00 F0 07  
000A83r 2               
000A83r 2               ; /dev/zero: reads fill the buffer with zeros; it's read-only here
000A83r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0                                  ; Fill the buffer with $FF first
000A85r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF

000A90r 2  C8                       iny
000A91r 2  C0 2C                    cpy         #<IT_BUF_SIZE
000A93r 2  D0 F9                    bne         :-
000A95r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1

000A9Br 2  A2 01 20 rr  
000A9Fr 2  rr           
000AA0r 2  90 05 A2 64              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'd'
000AA4r 2  4C rr rr     
000AA7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_2                           ; (The next one)
000AA9r 2  A5 rr 85 rr              _M_IT_COUNT ZP_TEMP_2, IT_BUF_SIZE
000AADr 2  A5 rr 85 rr  
000AB1r 2  A9 2C 85 rr  
000ABBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_READ
000ABEr 2  90 05 A2 65              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'e'
000AC2r 2  4C rr rr

000AC9r 2  D0 06 A5 rr  
000ACDr 2  C9 01 F0 07  
000AD8r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0                                  ; All zeros?
000ADAr 2               :
000ADAr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_TEMP_VEC3),Y
000ADCr 2  D0 39                    bne         @not_zero
000ADEr 2  C8                       iny
000ADFr 2  D0 F9                    bne         :-
000AE1r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
000AE3r 2               :

000AE5r 2  D0 30                    bne         @not_zero
000AE7r 2  C8                       iny
000AE8r 2  C0 2C                    cpy         #<IT_BUF_SIZE
000AEAr 2  D0 F7                    bne         :-
000AECr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
000AEEr 2  A5 rr 85 rr              _M_IT_COUNT ZP_TEMP_2, 5
000AF2r 2  A5 rr 85 rr  
000AF6r 2  A9 05 85 rr  
000B00r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_WRITE                            ; Read-only: must fail
000B03r 2  B0 07 A9 00              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_NC    'f', ERR_IO_MODE
000B07r 2  A2 66 4C rr  
000B0Br 2  rr C9 72 F0  
000B15r 2  80 05                    bra         @getc
000B17r 2               
000B17r 2               @not_zero:
000B17r 2  A2 65                    ldx         #'e'
000B19r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @fail
000B1Cr 2               
000B1Cr 2               ; One byte at a time
000B1Cr 2               @getc:

000B33r 2  A9 78                    lda         #'x'
000B35r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_PUTC
000B38r 2  90 05 A2 68              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'h'
000B3Cr 2  4C rr rr     
000B3Fr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TEMP
000B41r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_GETC                             ; /dev/null: end of file
000B44r 2  B0 07 A9 00              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_NC    'h', ERR_IO_EOF

000B5Fr 2  4C rr rr

000BB5r 2  A2 6A 4C rr

000BC3r 2

000C2Er 2               
000C2Er 2               ; /dev/cons: writes show up; an unknown control code is refused
000C2Er 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_IT_OPEN  S_DEV_CONS_T, IO_MODE_WRITE
000C32r 2  A2 02 20 rr

000C42r 2               :

000C45r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC3),Y
000C47r 2  88                       dey
000C48r 2  10 F8                    bpl         :-

000C4Er 2  A5 rr 85 rr

000C94r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_CLOSE
000C97r 2  90 05 A2 73              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     's'
000C9Br 2  4C rr rr     
000C9Er 2               
000C9Er 2               ; A pipe between two tasks: a child writes IT_PIPE_SIZE bytes (more than the ring holds, so both sides
000C9Er 2               ; wait for each other) and ends, which closes its write end; we read to end of file.  The child gets the
000C9Er 2               ; write end as fd IO_MAX_FDS - 1 (IO_DUP2), and inherits it.
000C9Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_PIPE                             ; .A = read fd, .X = write fd
000CA1r 2  90 05 A2 74              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     't'
000CA5r 2  4C rr rr     
000CA8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC2                        ; (TASK_RUN uses ZP_TEMP and ZP_TEMP_VEC)
000CAAr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_TEMP_2
000CACr 2  8A                       txa
000CADr 2  A2 0B                    ldx         #IO_MAX_FDS - 1
000CAFr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_DUP2
000CB2r 2  90 05 A2 74              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     't'
000CB6r 2  4C rr rr     
000CB9r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_2
000CBBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_CLOSE
000CBEr 2  90 05 A2 74              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     't'
000CC2r 2  4C rr rr     
000CC5r 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<IT_CHILD
000CC7r 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>IT_CHILD
000CC9r 2  A2 02                    ldx         #2                                  ; (ROM page 2)
000CCBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_RUN
000CCEr 2  90 05 A2 75              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'u'
000CD2r 2  4C rr rr     
000CD5r 2  A9 0B                    lda         #IO_MAX_FDS - 1                     ; Only the child's write end is left
000CD7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_CLOSE
000CDAr 2  90 05 A2 75              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'u'
000CDEr 2  4C rr rr     
000CE1r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Bytes read
000CE3r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000CE5r 2               
000CE5r 2               @pipe_read:
000CE5r 2  A5 rr 85 rr              _M_IT_COUNT ZP_TEMP_VEC2, IT_BUF_SIZE
000CE9r 2  A5 rr 85 rr  
000CEDr 2  A9 2C 85 rr  
000CF7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_READ
000CFAr 2  90 05 A2 76              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'v'
000CFEr 2  4C rr rr     
000D01r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CNT
000D03r 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
000D05r 2  F0 0F                    beq         @pipe_eof
000D07r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D09r 2  18                       clc
000D0Ar 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CNT
000D0Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D0Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000D10r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
000D12r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000D14r 2  80 CF                    bra         @pipe_read
000D16r 2               
000D16r 2               @pipe_eof:
000D16r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D18r 2  C9 58                    cmp         #<IT_PIPE_SIZE
000D1Ar 2  D0 06                    bne         :+
000D1Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000D1Er 2  C9 02                    cmp         #>IT_PIPE_SIZE
000D20r 2  F0 07                    beq         :++
000D22r 2               :
000D22r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D24r 2  A2 77                    ldx         #'w'
000D26r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @fail
000D29r 2               :
000D29r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000D2Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_CLOSE
000D2Er 2  90 05 A2 77              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'w'
000D32r 2  4C rr rr     
000D35r 2               
000D35r 2               ; Running out of fds: fill the free ones (ZP_TEMP_VEC: bit n = fd n was free), then close them again
000D35r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D37r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000D39r 2  A0 58                    ldy         #(IO_MAX_FDS - 1) * IO_FD_SIZE
000D3Br 2               
000D3Br 2               @scan:
000D3Br 2  B9 A0 7D                 lda         IO_FD_SERVER,Y
000D3Er 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IO_FD_CLOSED                       ; C = 1: free
000D40r 2  26 rr                    rol         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D42r 2  26 rr                    rol         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000D44r 2  98                       tya
000D45r 2  38                       sec
000D46r 2  E9 08                    sbc         #IO_FD_SIZE
000D48r 2  A8                       tay
000D49r 2  B0 F0                    bcs         @scan
000D4Br 2               
000D4Br 2               @open_all:
000D4Br 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_IT_OPEN  S_DEV_NULL, IO_MODE_RDWR
000D4Fr 2  A2 03 20 rr  
000D53r 2  rr           
000D54r 2  90 F5                    bcc         @open_all
000D56r 2  C9 75                    cmp         #ERR_IO_NO_FDS
000D58r 2  F0 05                    beq         :+
000D5Ar 2  A2 6E                    ldx         #'n'
000D5Cr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @fail
000D5Fr 2               :
000D5Fr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
000D61r 2               
000D61r 2               @close_all:
000D61r 2  46 rr                    lsr         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000D63r 2  66 rr                    ror         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000D65r 2  90 0B                    bcc         @next_fd
000D67r 2  8A                       txa
000D68r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_CLOSE
000D6Br 2  90 05 A2 6F              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'o'
000D6Fr 2  4C rr rr     
000D72r 2               
000D72r 2               @next_fd:
000D72r 2  E8                       inx
000D73r 2  E0 0C                    cpx         #IO_MAX_FDS
000D75r 2  D0 EA                    bne         @close_all
000D77r 2               
000D77r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC4
000D79r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_FREE
000D7Cr 2  90 05 A2 70              _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C     'p'
000D80r 2  4C rr rr     
000D83r 2  A9 6F 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'o', #'k'
000D87r 2  rr A9 6B 20  
000D8Br 2  rr rr        
000D8Dr 2  80 28                    bra         @end
000D8Fr 2               
000D8Fr 2               @fail:                                                      ; .X = step, .A = error / value
000D8Fr 2  48                       pha
000D90r 2  DA                       phx
000D91r 2  A9 46 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'F', #'A', #'I', #'L', #' '
000D95r 2  rr A9 41 20  
000D99r 2  rr rr A9 49  
000DAAr 2  68                       pla
000DABr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
000DAEr 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_SPACE
000DB2r 2  rr           
000DB3r 2  68                       pla
000DB4r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
000DB7r 2               
000DB7r 2               @end:
000DB7r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000DBAr 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
000DBDr 2  60                       rts
000DBEr 2               
000DBEr 2               ; The pipe test's child task: write IT_PIPE_SIZE bytes (any: this ROM page) to fd IO_MAX_FDS - 1, and end
000DBEr 2               IT_PIPE_SIZE    = 600
000DBEr 2               
000DBEr 2               IT_CHILD:
000DBEr 2  A9 00                    lda         #<RESET_ENTRY
000DC0r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF
000DC2r 2  A9 E0                    lda         #>RESET_ENTRY
000DC4r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
000DC6r 2  A9 58                    lda         #<IT_PIPE_SIZE
000DC8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CNT
000DCAr 2  A9 02                    lda         #>IT_PIPE_SIZE
000DCCr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
000DCEr 2  A9 0B                    lda         #IO_MAX_FDS - 1
000DD0r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         IO_WRITE
000DD3r 2               
000DD3r 1               .include "post_ram.s"   ; POST paged RAM line tests
000DD3r 2               .debuginfo
000DD3r 2               
000DD3r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000DD3r 2               ; POST paged RAM line tests (see POST in os_main.s).  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2`
000DD3r 2               ; (see all.s); POST calls it through a gate, at boot (task 0, IRQs off, polled serial output).
000DD3r 2               ; Prints the second POST line:
000DD3r 2               ;       RAM U:x bb:x/dd/aaaa bb:x/dd/aaaa ...
000DD3r 2               ;   Each is a hex mask of bad lines (bit set = bad; all 0 when good):
000DD3r 2               ;   U:x      U lines U0-U3 (bit n = Un), tested on shared bank $F0
000DD3r 2               ;   bb:x/dd/aaaa  bank bb at $8000-$9FFF: bank register lines 0-3 (bb ^ 1/2/4/8), data lines D0-D7 and
000DD3r 2               ;            address lines A0-A12.  Tested: the first bank of each shared RAM chip ($F0, $F4, $F8, $FC, with
000DD3r 2               ;            U = 0; a missing chip shows as bad lines) and of each installed task RAM module.
000DD3r 2               ;   Destructive: run before anything is kept in paged RAM.  Leaves RAM_BANK_REG and U at 0.
000DD3r 2               ;   Records the chips that failed, for the MMU to leave unused (in task 0): ZP_M_BAD_SH (shared RAM chips,
000DD3r 2               ;   used by SHARED_RAM_INIT) and ZP_M_BAD_MODS (task RAM modules, used by TASK_RAM_INIT).
000DD3r 2               
000DD3r 2               .segment "POST_P2"
000000r 2               
000000r 2  0D 0A 52 41  POST_S_RAM: .byte ASCII_CR, ASCII_LF, "RAM U:", 0
000004r 2  4D 20 55 3A  
000008r 2  00           
000009r 2               
000009r 2               POST_RAM_TEST:
000009r 2  A2 00                    ldx                 #0
00000Br 2               :
00000Br 2  BD rr rr                 lda                 POST_S_RAM,X
00000Er 2  F0 06                    beq                 :+
000010r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000013r 2  E8                       inx
000014r 2  80 F5                    bra                 :-
000016r 2               :
000016r 2  A9 FF 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR           U_REGISTER, ZP_TEMP_VEC2
00001Ar 2  A9 F1 85 rr  
00001Er 2  A9 F0                    lda                 #$F0
000020r 2  85 rr                    sta                 RAM_BANK_REG
000022r 2  A9 00                    lda                 #0                                  ; U lines, on shared bank $F0
000024r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_LINE_TEST
000027r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
00002Ar 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR           RAM_BANK_REG, ZP_TEMP_VEC2
00002Er 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000032r 2  A9 F0                    lda                 #$F0                                ; Shared RAM (U = 0): each chip's first bank
000034r 2               
000034r 2               @shared:
000034r 2  A2 03                    ldx                 #$03                                ; Bank lines 2-3 pick the chip: only lines
000036r 2  86 rr                    stx                 ZP_M_TEMP                           ;   0-1 count against this one
000038r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_BANK_TEST                      ; Z = 0: bad
00003Br 2  18                       clc
00003Cr 2  F0 01                    beq                 :+
00003Er 2  38                       sec
00003Fr 2               :
00003Fr 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_BAD_SH                         ; Chip c -> bit 4 + c
000041r 2  18                       clc
000042r 2  69 04                    adc                 #4
000044r 2  90 EE                    bcc                 @shared
000046r 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH                         ; Chip c -> bit c
000048r 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH
00004Ar 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH
00004Cr 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH
00004Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 MMU_PROBE_MODULES                   ; Task RAM: each installed module's first bank
000051r 2  A9 00                    lda                 #0
000053r 2               
000053r 2               @module:
000053r 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_MODS + 1
000055r 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_MODS
000057r 2  90 0B                    bcc                 :+                                  ; Not installed (C = 0: not bad)
000059r 2  A2 0F                    ldx                 #$0F                                ; All 4 bank lines are the module's
00005Br 2  86 rr                    stx                 ZP_M_TEMP
00005Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_BANK_TEST                      ; Z = 0: bad
000060r 2  18                       clc
000061r 2  F0 01                    beq                 :+
000063r 2  38                       sec
000064r 2               :
000064r 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_BAD_MODS + 1                   ; Module m -> bit m (after all 16)
000066r 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_BAD_MODS
000068r 2  18                       clc
000069r 2  69 10                    adc                 #$10
00006Br 2  90 E6                    bcc                 @module                             ; (Module 15 is the shared banks: never
00006Dr 2  64 rr                    stz                 RAM_BANK_REG                        ;   installed, so never tested)
00006Fr 2  9C F1 FF                 stz                 U_REGISTER
000072r 2  60                       rts
000073r 2               
000073r 2               ; Test paged RAM bank .A ($8000-$9FFF) and print " bb:x/dd/aaaa": the bad bank lines (x, see
000073r 2               ; POST_LINE_TEST), data lines (dd: D0-D7) and address lines (aaaa: A0-A12); bit set = bad line.
000073r 2               ; ZP_TEMP_VEC2 must point at RAM_BANK_REG.  Leaves the bank selected.
000073r 2               ; IN: ZP_M_TEMP = the bank lines that count against this bank.  OUT: Z = 0 if any line is bad.  Preserves .A
000073r 2               POST_BANK_TEST:
000073r 2  48                       pha
000074r 2  48                       pha
000075r 2  A9 20                    lda                 #' '
000077r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
00007Ar 2  68                       pla
00007Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
00007Er 2  A9 3A                    lda                 #':'
000080r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000083r 2  68                       pla
000084r 2  48                       pha
000085r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_LINE_TEST
000088r 2  48                       pha
000089r 2  25 rr                    and                 ZP_M_TEMP
00008Br 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_M_TEMP                           ; ZP_M_TEMP = all of this bank's bad lines
00008Dr 2  68                       pla
00008Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
000091r 2  A9 2F                    lda                 #'/'
000093r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000096r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP                             ; Data lines: walking one at $8000
000098r 2  A2 01                    ldx                 #1
00009Ar 2               :
00009Ar 2  8E 00 80                 stx                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
00009Dr 2  8A                       txa
00009Er 2  4D 00 80                 eor                 PAGED_RAM_BASE                      ; Bits that read back wrong
0000A1r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP
0000A3r 2  8A                       txa
0000A4r 2  0A                       asl
0000A5r 2  AA                       tax
0000A6r 2  D0 F2                    bne                 :-
0000A8r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP
0000AAr 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_M_TEMP
0000ACr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
0000AFr 2  A9 2F                    lda                 #'/'
0000B1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
0000B4r 2               
0000B4r 2               ; Address lines: $8000 = 0, $8000 + 2^n = n + 1 (n = 0-12).  Line n is bad if $8000 + 2^n reads back
0000B4r 2               ; wrong, or its write landed on $8000.
0000B4r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Bad lines
0000B6r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000B8r 2  9C 00 80                 stz                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
0000BBr 2  A2 00                    ldx                 #0                                  ; Pass 0 writes, pass 1 checks
0000BDr 2               
0000BDr 2               @pass:
0000BDr 2  A9 01                    lda                 #1
0000BFr 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4                        ; 2^n
0000C1r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000C3r 2  A0 01                    ldy                 #1                                  ; n + 1
0000C5r 2               
0000C5r 2               @line:
0000C5r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4
0000C7r 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_VEC3
0000C9r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000CBr 2  09 80                    ora                 #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
0000CDr 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
0000CFr 2  98                       tya
0000D0r 2  E0 00                    cpx                 #0
0000D2r 2  D0 04                    bne                 :+
0000D4r 2  92 rr                    sta                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC3)
0000D6r 2  80 11                    bra                 @next
0000D8r 2               :
0000D8r 2  CD 00 80                 cmp                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
0000DBr 2  F0 04                    beq                 @bad
0000DDr 2  D2 rr                    cmp                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC3)
0000DFr 2  F0 08                    beq                 @next
0000E1r 2               
0000E1r 2               @bad:
0000E1r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4
0000E3r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP_VEC
0000E5r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000E7r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000E9r 2               
0000E9r 2               @next:
0000E9r 2  C8                       iny
0000EAr 2  06 rr                    asl                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4
0000ECr 2  26 rr                    rol                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000EEr 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000F0r 2  C9 20                    cmp                 #$20                                ; Past A12
0000F2r 2  D0 D1                    bne                 @line
0000F4r 2  E8                       inx
0000F5r 2  E0 02                    cpx                 #2
0000F7r 2  D0 C4                    bne                 @pass
0000F9r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000FBr 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_M_TEMP
0000FDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
000100r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC
000102r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_M_TEMP
000104r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
000107r 2  68                       pla
000108r 2  A6 rr                    ldx                 ZP_M_TEMP                           ; Z = 0: bad
00010Ar 2  60                       rts
00010Br 2               
00010Br 2               ; Bank line test: the byte at $8000, with the register at (ZP_TEMP_VEC2) (RAM_BANK_REG or U) set to
00010Br 2               ; .A ^ 8, .A ^ 4, .A ^ 2, .A ^ 1 and .A.  OUT: .A = bad lines (bit n = register bit n, n = 0-3); the
00010Br 2               ; register is left at .A.  Modifies: .X, .Y
00010Br 2               POST_LINE_TEST:
00010Br 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_2
00010Dr 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP
00010Fr 2  A0 00                    ldy                 #0                                  ; Pass 0 writes, pass 1 checks
000111r 2               
000111r 2               @pass:
000111r 2  A2 10                    ldx                 #$10
000113r 2               
000113r 2               @line:
000113r 2  8A                       txa
000114r 2  4A                       lsr
000115r 2  AA                       tax                                                     ; 8, 4, 2, 1, 0 (the marker)
000116r 2  45 rr                    eor                 ZP_TEMP_2
000118r 2  92 rr                    sta                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC2)
00011Ar 2  8A                       txa
00011Br 2  C0 00                    cpy                 #0
00011Dr 2  D0 05                    bne                 :+
00011Fr 2  8D 00 80                 sta                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
000122r 2  80 07                    bra                 @next
000124r 2               :
000124r 2  CD 00 80                 cmp                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
000127r 2  F0 02                    beq                 @next
000129r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP
00012Br 2               
00012Br 2               @next:
00012Br 2  8A                       txa
00012Cr 2  D0 E5                    bne                 @line
00012Er 2  C8                       iny
00012Fr 2  C0 02                    cpy                 #2
000131r 2  D0 DE                    bne                 @pass
000133r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP
000135r 2  60                       rts
000136r 2               
000136r 2               ; Print .A as 2 hex digits (POST_PUTBYTE) or its low nibble as 1 (POST_PUTHEX).  Modifies: .Y
000136r 2               POST_PUTBYTE:
000136r 2  48                       pha
000137r 2  4A                       lsr
000138r 2  4A                       lsr
000139r 2  4A                       lsr
00013Ar 2  4A                       lsr
00013Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
00013Er 2  68                       pla
00013Fr 2               
00013Fr 2               POST_PUTHEX:
00013Fr 2  29 0F                    and                 #$0F
000141r 2  C9 0A                    cmp                 #10
000143r 2  90 02                    bcc                 :+
000145r 2  69 06                    adc                 #'A' - '0' - 10 - 1                 ; (C = 1)
000147r 2               :
000147r 2  69 30                    adc                 #'0'
000149r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp                 POST_PUTC
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 1               .include "spi.s"        ; SPI (for the SD card server, later)
00014Cr 2               .debuginfo
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00014Cr 2               ; SPI (bit-banged on the VIA).  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see all.s); moved here
00014Cr 2               ; from page 0 (bios.s) to make room there.  Not called yet (SPI_INIT and SPI_TEST are commented out in
00014Cr 2               ; os_main.s); the SD card server (IO plan, Phase 3) will use it.
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 2               .segment "SPI_P2"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Set up the SPI interface registers on the VIA
000000r 2               SPI_INIT:
000000r 2  48                           pha
000001r 2  A9 00 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_AUX_CTRL, , 0
000005r 2  rr           
000006r 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000009r 2  A9 FF 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_PER_CTRL, , $FF
00000Dr 2  rr           
00000Er 2  A9 7F 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DDR,  , SPI_DDR_BITS
000012r 2  rr           
000013r 2  A9 02 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DATA, , SPI_BIT_CSB   ; de-select all SPI devices
000017r 2  rr           
000018r 2  68                           pla
000019r 2  60                           rts
00001Ar 2               
00001Ar 2               ; Macro to remove essentially duplicate code
00001Ar 2               .macro          SPI_SEND_SETUP  mode
00001Ar 2                               sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
00001Ar 2                               phy
00001Ar 2                               txa
00001Ar 2                               ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI
00001Ar 2                               tay
00001Ar 2                               lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
00001Ar 2                               sei
00001Ar 2               .ifblank        mode
00001Ar 2                               asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
00001Ar 2               .endif
00001Ar 2                               sec
00001Ar 2                               rol
00001Ar 2               .endmacro
00001Ar 2               
00001Ar 2               ; Write and Read SPI data
00001Ar 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
00001Ar 2               ; A: data to send
00001Ar 2               ; X: device ID to send to/receive from
00001Ar 2               ; Returns input data in A
00001Ar 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_IN, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
00001Ar 2               SPI_TRANSCEIVE:
00001Ar 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP
00001Er 2  09 04 A8 A5  
000022r 2  rr 78 06 rr  
000028r 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
00002Ar 2               @spi_send_0:
00002Ar 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
00002Dr 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
00002Fr 2               @spi_send_1:
00002Fr 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
000032r 2               @spi_send:
000032r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
000035r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
000038r 2  10 02                        bpl             @spi_recv
00003Ar 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; incoming bit was a 1 (set LSb = 1)
00003Cr 2               @spi_recv:
00003Cr 2  0A                           asl
00003Dr 2  F0 0A                        beq             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
00003Fr 2  B0 04                        bcs             @had_1
000041r 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000043r 2  80 E5                        bra             @spi_send_0
000045r 2               @had_1:
000045r 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000047r 2  80 E6                        bra             @spi_send_1
000049r 2               
000049r 2               SPI_OPERATION_DONE:
000049r 2  A9 02                        lda             #SPI_BIT_CSB        ; de-select all SPI devices
00004Br 2  0C rr rr                     tsb             IOR_SPI_DATA
00004Er 2  7A                           ply
00004Fr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; load the input for return in A
000051r 2  58                           cli
000052r 2  60                           rts
000053r 2               
000053r 2               ; Write SPI data
000053r 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
000053r 2               ; A: data to send
000053r 2               ; X: device ID to send to
000053r 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000053r 2               SPI_SEND:
000053r 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP  1
000057r 2  09 04 A8 A5  
00005Br 2  rr 78 38 2A  
00005Fr 2               @send_loop:
00005Fr 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
000061r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
000064r 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
000066r 2               @spi_send_1:
000066r 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
000069r 2               @spi_send:
000069r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
00006Cr 2  0A                           asl
00006Dr 2  D0 F0                        bne             @send_loop
00006Fr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000072r 2               
000072r 2               ; Read from the SPI device
000072r 2               ; X: device to read from
000072r 2               ; Result returned in A
000072r 2               SPI_RECV:
000072r 2  5A                           phy
000073r 2  A0 08                        ldy             #8
000075r 2  8A                           txa
000076r 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_BIT_CSB
000078r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; Select the device to receive from
00007Br 2  78                           sei
00007Cr 2               @recv_loop:
00007Cr 2  0A                           asl                                 ; Shift in 0 to LSb of result
00007Dr 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA
000080r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
000083r 2  10 01                        bpl             @spi_recv_2
000085r 2                                                                   ; Set LSb = 1
000085r 2  1A                           inc
000086r 2               @spi_recv_2:
000086r 2  88                           dey
000087r 2  D0 F3                        bne             @recv_loop
000089r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
00008Br 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
00008Er 2               
00008Er 2               ; Delay for some number of cycles to ensure SPI device is ready to start working
00008Er 2               SPI_INIT_DELAY:
00008Er 2  48 DA 5A                     PUSH_AXY
000091r 2  8A                           txa                                         ; set SPI device
000092r 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI ; de-select all devices
000094r 2  AA                           tax
000095r 2  09 01                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CLK
000097r 2  A0 50                        ldy             #SPI_INIT_DELAY_CYCLES
000099r 2               @loop:
000099r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA
00009Cr 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
00009Fr 2  88                           dey
0000A0r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @loop
0000A2r 2  7A FA 68                     PULL_YXA
0000A5r 2  60                           rts
0000A6r 2               
0000A6r 2               SPI_TEST:
0000A6r 2  A2 00                    ldx                 #SPI_DEV_0
0000A8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 SPI_INIT_DELAY
0000ABr 2  A9 40 20 rr              SPI_SEND_CMD        0, 0, 0, 0,   0, $4A                ; CMD0
0000AFr 2  rr A9 00 20  
0000B3r 2  rr rr 85 rr  
0000D0r 2  A9 48 20 rr              SPI_SEND_CMD        8, 0, 0, 1, $AA, $43                ; CMD8
0000D4r 2  rr A9 00 20  
0000D8r 2  rr rr 85 rr  
0000F5r 2               @loop:

000096r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000096r 2               ; BIOS ROM page 1: included inside `.scope PAGE1` (see all.s), so MM_* / SH_* / TASK_CALL calls go through
000096r 2               ; the page 1 gates (page1.s).  Routines passed to TASK_CALL are named with :: (their page 0 addresses),
000096r 2               ; because TASK_CALL runs them on page 0.

000096r 2               .segment "TESTS_P1"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; MMU self test (TH_MMU_TEST, $F833; from WOZMON: F833R; runs on ROM page 1).  Runs the handle calls in the current task and
000000r 2               ; prints "MMU test: ok", or "MMU test: FAIL x ee" (x = failing step, ee = error code or value read).
000000r 2               ; Allocates and frees task RAM pages (at the top of the free area), chunks, 2 RAM banks and shared memory,
000000r 2               ; and borrows (and resets) task MMU_TEST_TASK.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C  step                                ; Fail if the call returned an error
000000r 2                           bcc         :+
000000r 2                           ldx         #step
000000r 2                           jmp         @fail
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro _M_MT_FAIL_IF_NC step                                ; Fail if the call should have failed but didn't
000000r 2                           bcs         :+
000000r 2                           ldx         #step
000000r 2                           jmp         @fail
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro _M_MT_EXPECT     step, value                         ; Fail if .A <> value
000000r 2                           cmp         #value
000000r 2                           beq         :+
000000r 2                           ldx         #step
000000r 2                           jmp         @fail
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2  0A 4D 4D 55  NamedHString    S_MMU_TEST, "MMU test: "
000004r 2  20 74 65 73  
000008r 2  74 3A 20     
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               MMU_TEST_TASK   = 2                                         ; An idle task the test borrows (and resets)
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               MMU_TEST:

000012r 2  20 rr rr

000018r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1                    ; (e.g. HyForth's arena)
00001Ar 2               
00001Ar 2               ; Small (in-entry) allocation
00001Ar 2  A9 02                    lda         #2                                  ; 2 bytes
00001Cr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
00001Er 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
000020r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ALLOC
000023r 2  90 05 A2 61              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C     'a'
000027r 2  4C rr rr     
00002Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP                             ; h1
00002Cr 2  A2 5A                    ldx         #$5A
00002Er 2  A0 01                    ldy         #1
000030r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_WRITE
000033r 2  90 05 A2 62              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C     'b'

00004Cr 2  A2 63 4C rr  
000050r 2  rr

000053r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #2                                  ; Past the end
000055r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_READ
000058r 2  B0 05 A2 64              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_NC    'd'
00005Cr 2  4C rr rr     
00005Fr 2               
00005Fr 2               ; Task RAM pages
00005Fr 2  A9 2C                    lda         #<300                               ; 2 pages

000065r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ALLOC

00007Dr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
00007Fr 2  C9 00 F0 05              _M_MT_EXPECT        'f', 0                      ; Page aligned
000083r 2  A2 66 4C rr  
000087r 2  rr           
000088r 2  98                       tya

00008Cr 2  F0 05                    beq         :+

00029Br 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK

00029Fr 2  A0 05                    ldy         #5
0002A1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                           ; The other task can still read it
0002A4r 2  90 05 A2 31              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C     '1'
