000364r 1               .include "sched_test.s"
000364r 2               .debuginfo
000364r 2               
000364r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000364r 2               ; Scheduler self test (TH_SCHED_TEST, $F869; from WOZMON: F869R).  BIOS ROM page 1: included inside
000364r 2               ; `.scope PAGE1` (see all.s), so calls go through the page 1 gates.
000364r 2               ;
000364r 2               ; Prints three lines:
000364r 2               ;   1. Two background tasks print "a" and "b" while this task prints "m": the letters interleave
000364r 2               ;      (preemption on the timer tick)
000364r 2               ;   2. A background task prints "[cccccccccc]" holding NO_PREEMPT while this task prints "m": the
000364r 2               ;      bracketed run is never split
000364r 2               ;   3. A background task waits (TASK_WAIT); this task prints "w", then wakes it (IO_WAKE) and it prints
000364r 2               ;      "W": "wW"
000364r 2               ; then "done".  Uses two free tasks.
000364r 2               
000364r 2               .segment "TESTS_P1"
000364r 2               
000364r 2  0B 53 63 68  NamedHString    S_SCHED_TEST, "Sched test:"
000368r 2  65 64 20 74  
00036Cr 2  65 73 74 3A  
000370r 2  04 64 6F 6E  NamedHString    S_SCHED_DONE, "done"
000374r 2  65           
000375r 2  12 46 41 49  NamedHString    S_SCHED_FAIL, "FAIL: no free task"
000379r 2  4C 3A 20 6E  
00037Dr 2  6F 20 66 72  
000388r 2               
000388r 2               ST_COUNT        = 12                                        ; Letters per task
000388r 2               
000388r 2               ; About 10,000 cycles (~2.8 ms, a little over half a scheduler tick).  Modifies: .X, .Y
000388r 2               ST_DELAY:
000388r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #8 * CPU_CLOCK_MULT
00038Ar 2               :
00038Ar 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
00038Cr 2               :
00038Cr 2  CA                       dex
00038Dr 2  D0 FD                    bne         :-
00038Fr 2  88                       dey
000390r 2  D0 F8                    bne         :--
000392r 2  60                       rts
000393r 2               
000393r 2               ; Print a letter ST_COUNT times, with a delay after each.  IN: .A = letter.  Uses ZP_TEMP, ZP_TEMP_2
000393r 2               ST_PRINT_RUN:
000393r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_2
000395r 2  A9 0C                    lda         #ST_COUNT
000397r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP
000399r 2               :
000399r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_2
00039Br 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
00039Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
0003A1r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_TEMP
0003A3r 2  D0 F4                    bne         :-
0003A5r 2  60                       rts
0003A6r 2               
0003A6r 2               ; Background tasks (each has its own ZP, so they can all use ZP_TEMP)
0003A6r 2               ST_WORK_A:
0003A6r 2  A9 61                    lda         #'a'
0003A8r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ST_PRINT_RUN
0003ABr 2               
0003ABr 2               ST_WORK_B:
0003ABr 2  A9 62                    lda         #'b'
0003ADr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ST_PRINT_RUN
0003B0r 2               
0003B0r 2               ST_WORK_C:
0003B0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NO_PREEMPT
0003B3r 2  A9 5B 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'['
0003B7r 2  rr           
0003B8r 2  A9 0A                    lda         #10
0003BAr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP
0003BCr 2               :
0003BCr 2  A9 63 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'c'
0003C0r 2  rr           
0003C1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
0003C4r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_TEMP
0003C6r 2  D0 F4                    bne         :-
0003C8r 2  A9 5D 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #']'
0003CCr 2  rr           
0003CDr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PREEMPT
0003D0r 2               
0003D0r 2               ST_WORK_D:
0003D0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_WAIT
0003D3r 2  A9 57 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'W'
0003D7r 2  rr           
0003D8r 2  60                       rts
0003D9r 2               
0003D9r 2               ; Start a background task on ROM page 1.  IN: .A.Y = entry point.  OUT: .A = task (C = 1: none free)
0003D9r 2               .macro _M_ST_RUN    entry
0003D9r 2                           lda         #<entry
0003D9r 2                           ldy         #>entry
0003D9r 2                           ldx         #1
0003D9r 2                           jsr         TASK_RUN
0003D9r 2               .endmacro
0003D9r 2               
0003D9r 2               ; Wait (yielding) until a task has finished.  IN: .A = task.  Uses ZP_TEMP_VEC
0003D9r 2               ST_WAIT_DONE:
0003D9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC
0003DBr 2               :
0003DBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
0003DDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_STATUS
0003E0r 2  29 01                    and         #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
0003E2r 2  F0 05                    beq         :+
0003E4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         YIELD
0003E7r 2  80 F2                    bra         :-
0003E9r 2               :
0003E9r 2  60                       rts
0003EAr 2               
0003EAr 2               SCHED_TEST:
0003EAr 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0003EDr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_SCHED_TEST
0003F1r 2  20 rr rr     
0003F4r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0003F7r 2               
0003F7r 2               ; 1. Preemption: three tasks printing at once
0003F7r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_A
0003FBr 2  A2 01 20 rr  
0003FFr 2  rr           
000400r 2  B0 6D                    bcs         @fail
000402r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000404r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_B
000408r 2  A2 01 20 rr  
00040Cr 2  rr           
00040Dr 2  B0 60                    bcs         @fail
00040Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
000411r 2  A9 6D                    lda         #'m'
000413r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_PRINT_RUN
000416r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000418r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
00041Br 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
00041Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
000420r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000423r 2               
000423r 2               ; 2. NO_PREEMPT keeps "[cccccccccc]" together
000423r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_C
000427r 2  A2 01 20 rr  
00042Br 2  rr           
00042Cr 2  B0 41                    bcs         @fail
00042Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000430r 2  A9 6D                    lda         #'m'
000432r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_PRINT_RUN
000435r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000437r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
00043Ar 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
00043Dr 2               
00043Dr 2               ; 3. TASK_WAIT / IO_WAKE: "wW"
00043Dr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_D
000441r 2  A2 01 20 rr  
000445r 2  rr           
000446r 2  B0 27                    bcs         @fail
000448r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
00044Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY                            ; Let it get to TASK_WAIT
00044Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
000450r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
000453r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
000456r 2  A9 77 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'w'
00045Ar 2  rr           
00045Br 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
00045Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_WAKE
000460r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
000463r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000466r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_SCHED_DONE
00046Ar 2  20 rr rr     
00046Dr 2  80 07                    bra         @end
00046Fr 2               
00046Fr 2               @fail:
00046Fr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_SCHED_FAIL
000473r 2  20 rr rr     
000476r 2               
000476r 2               @end:
000476r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000479r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
00047Cr 2  60                       rts
00047Dr 2

000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; 0..3 = even indexes, 4..7 = odd
000100r 2               ; x000: one byte
000100r 2               ; xxx1: two byte
000100r 2               ; xxx0 (!x000): three byte
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ;x1xx = Indexed
000100r 2               ;   x10x = Indexed by X
000100r 2               ;   x11x (!1110) = Indexed by Y
000100r 2               ;1xxx = Indirect (!100x && !1110)
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; INDEXED SUFFIXES
000100r 2               ; 010x == ,X
000100r 2               ; 110x == ,X)
000100r 2               ; 011x == ,Y
000100r 2               ; 1111 == ),Y
000100r 2               
000100r 2               AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
000100r 2               AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
000100r 2               AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
000100r 2               AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
000100r 2               AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
000100r 2               AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
000100r 2               AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
000100r 2               AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
000100r 2               AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
000100r 2               AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
000100r 2               AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
000100r 2               AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
000100r 2               AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
000100r 2               AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
000100r 2               AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
000100r 2               AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; ADDRESSING MODES
000100r 2               ; Even Opcode Modes (%xxxxyyz0), xxxx = row, yy = column, z = 0 (low nibble), z = 1 (hi nibble)
000100r 2               MN_AMODE_EVEN:

000104r 2  B1 53 08 4E      .byte $B1,$53,$08,$4E	; %0001yyz0

000168r 2  F0 04            beq         :+
00016Ar 2  49 02            eor         #2
00016Cr 2  F0 0F            beq         :+++
00016Er 2               
00016Er 2               :
00016Er 2  AA               tax
00016Fr 2               
00016Fr 2               :
00016Fr 2  A9 20 20 rr      PRINT_SPACE
000173r 2  rr           
000174r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000177r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
00017Ar 2  CA               dex
00017Br 2  D0 F2            bne         :-
00017Dr 2               
00017Dr 2               :
00017Dr 2  60               rts
00017Er 2               
00017Er 2               ; Carry: 1 = Y, Carry: 0 = X
00017Er 2               _disasm_commaXY:
00017Er 2  A9 2C 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COMMA
000182r 2  rr           
000183r 2  A9 58            lda         #ASCII_X
000185r 2  90 01            bcc         :+
000187r 2  1A               inc
000188r 2               
000188r 2               :
000188r 2  4C rr rr         PRINT_CHAR_JMP
00018Br 2               
00018Br 2               _disasm_absolute:
00018Br 2  C8               iny
00018Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
00018Fr 2  88               dey
000190r 2  80 0B            bra         _disasm_zp_byte
000192r 2               
000192r 2               _disasm_immediate:
000192r 2  A9 23 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_HASH
000196r 2  rr           
000197r 2                   ; fall through
000197r 2               
000197r 2               _disasm_zeropage:
000197r 2  C8               iny
000198r 2  A9 24 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_DOLLAR
00019Cr 2  rr           
00019Dr 2               
00019Dr 2               _disasm_zp_byte:
00019Dr 2  B9 rr rr 4C      PRINT_BYTE_JMP  {ZP_D_INST, y}
0001A1r 2  rr rr        
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 1               .include "hyforth/hyforth.s"
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;  Patrick Struthers - March 2026
0001A3r 2               ;      - THANKS to AGSB for the starting point here....
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;  The HyForth project starts here with AGSB's core Forth engine;
0001A3r 2               ;  the engine will be moved to ROM and the heap will be copied to RAM
0001A3r 2               ;  on cold start.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;  The ulitmate purpose of working this port through is to develop a
0001A3r 2               ;  flexible and powerful operating system for the Hydra-16 of reasonable
0001A3r 2               ;  efficiency and minimal memory footprint.  HyForth will grow and
0001A3r 2               ;  shrink in RAM footprint according to context.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .debuginfo
0001A3r 2               .setcpu "65C02"
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ; macros for dictionary, creates code as follows:
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;   h_name:
0001A3r 2               ;   .word  link_to_previous_entry
0001A3r 2               ;   .byte  strlen(name) + flags
0001A3r 2               ;   .byte  name
0001A3r 2               ;   name:
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; label for primitives
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro makelabel arg1, arg2
0001A3r 2               .ident (.concat (arg1, arg2)):
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; header for primitives
0001A3r 2               ; the entry point for dictionary is h_~name~
0001A3r 2               ; the entry point for code is ~name~
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro def_word name, label, flag
0001A3r 2               makelabel "h_", label
0001A3r 2               .ident(.sprintf("H%04X", hcount + 1)):
0001A3r 2                 .word .ident (.sprintf ("H%04X", hcount))
0001A3r 2               hcount .set hcount + 1
0001A3r 2                 .byte .strlen(name) + flag + 0 ; nice trick !
0001A3r 2                 .byte name
0001A3r 2               makelabel "", label
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;  macros for PGS stuff
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro WCRLF_np                ; no push of A
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CRLF
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro WCRLF
0001A3r 2                   pha
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   pla
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro  WSEQ_np  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   phy
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   lda strlbl, y
0001A3r 2                   beq :+
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
0001A3r 2                   bra :-
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   ply
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro  WSEQ_raw  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   phy
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   lda strlbl, y
0001A3r 2                   beq :+
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
0001A3r 2                   bra :-
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   ply
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro WSEQ  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   pha
0001A3r 2                   WSEQ_np  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   pla
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;   ANSI screen stuff
0001A3r 2               ; <rr>;<cc>f move cursor to rr,cc
0001A3r 2               ; <cc>m for color/attributes
0001A3r 2               ; 2J for clear screen
0001A3r 2               ; H for 'home'
0001A3r 2               ; <cc>[ABCD] screen moves
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro ANSI b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6,b7
0001A3r 2                   pha
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_ANSI_ESC_SEQ b1, b2, b3, b4, b5, b6, b7
0001A3r 2                   pla
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;  for error messages
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro ERR_entry errmsg
0001A3r 2                   .byte <errmsg
0001A3r 2                   .byte >errmsg
0001A3r 2                   emcount .set emcount + 1
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro WERR errptr
0001A3r 2               ;.macro WERR
0001A3r 2                   .local werrloop, werrend
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2                   lda (errptr),y
0001A3r 2                   sta TEMP0
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   lda (errptr),y
0001A3r 2                   sta TEMP0+1
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2               werrloop:
0001A3r 2                   lda (TEMP0),y
0001A3r 2                   beq werrend
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
0001A3r 2                   bra werrloop
0001A3r 2               werrend:
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   rts
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ; variables for macros
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               hcount .set 0
0001A3r 2               emcount .set 0             ; # of error messages set up
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               H0000 = 0
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;               CONFIGURATION OPTIONS
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; number conversion
0001A3r 2               numbers := 1      ; include DEC/BIN/HEX conversion
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               SINGLE := 1     ; single digits hard coded?
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               HYWORDS := 1      ; add in additional hardcoded words / logic
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ANSIOK := 1         ; add ANSI screen stuff
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               YSOUND := 1        ; add sound support
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;TXT2STACK := 1     ; use old TXTGET instead of new
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;              for PGS hyforth stuff
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; error codes
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ERR_RPTR := $01    ; return stack pointer error
0001A3r 2               ERR_SPTR := $02    ; data stack pointer error
0001A3r 2               ERR_DIV0 := $03    ; divide by zero
0001A3r 2               ERR_MEM := $04     ; memory not-avail
0001A3r 2               ERR_UKW := $05     ; unknown word
0001A3r 2               ERR_SEC := $06     ; security error, ie dangerous address write
0001A3r 2               ERR_SYS := $07     ; return from system call error
0001A3r 2               ERR_IO := $08      ; IO error (the IO layer's error code: ioerr)
0001A3r 2                 ; warnings
0001A3r 2               WRN_SEC := $86     ; security
0001A3r 2               WRN_MEM := $84     ; memory alloc
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;                 CORE ENGINE CONSTANTS
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               CELL = 2         ; cell size, two bytes, 16-bit
0001A3r 2               FLAG_IMM = 1<<7  ; immediate flag
0001A3r 2               FLAG_COM = 1<<6  ; compiled flag
0001A3r 2               MAXSTR = 100
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; terminal input buffer, forward
0001A3r 2               ; getline, token, skip, scan, depends on page boundary
0001A3r 2               ; INBUF = $0400  (see segment STACKS below)
0001A3r 2               ; moves forwards
0001A3r 2               INBUF_end = $FD
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; data stacks
0001A3r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
0001A3r 2               DSEND = $7E
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; return stack
0001A3r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
0001A3r 2               RTEND = $FE
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; malloc stack
0001A3r 2               ; moves backwards
0001A3r 2               MEMEND = $01FE
0001A3r 2               MEM_SZ = $04
0001A3r 2               MEM_MMU = $40             ; record type flag: the record is its own MMU block (large records)
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; malloc records (see MALLOC): small ones go in an arena the MMU allocates at 'cold'; records of
0001A3r 2               ; FORTH_LARGE_MIN bytes or more get their own MMU block
0001A3r 2               FORTH_ARENA_SIZE = $0800  ; 2K arena for small records
0001A3r 2               FORTH_LARGE_MIN = $0100   ; records this big or bigger get their own MMU block
0001A3r 2               FORTH_DICT_MARGIN = 2     ; pages above 'here' the MMU must keep free (see DICTCHK)
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; spi
0001A3r 2               ;F9E8 SPI_INIT_DELAY
0001A3r 2               ;F9CC SPI_RECV
0001A3r 2               ;F9AD SPI_SEND
0001A3r 2               ;F9A3 SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001A3r 2               ;F974 SPI_TRANSCEIVE
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ALTBUF = $6000
0001A3r 2               ALTBUF_end = $6FFF
0001A3r 2               ; malloc allocates DOWN from the top of the arena (MEMTOPV), set up at 'cold'
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;       Look closely at hyforth.cfg and the output of ca65/ld65 after
0001A3r 2               ;  a build; the RAM and ROM code is carefully arranged when the binary
0001A3r 2               ;  is created, to make initialization easier and optimize RAM use.
0001A3r 2               ;  The main program engine starts at $A000 in ROM and is only about 500 bytes
0001A3r 2               ;  long.  Additional functions, then initialization and debug code,
0001A3r 2               ;  then the dictionary follow in the binary, and these stay in ROM.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;       The dictionary has a particular structure, with 'bye' and 'abort'
0001A3r 2               ;  at the beginning; there are some core words,
0001A3r 2               ;  then contents of 'primitives.s', then 'hywords.s', followed by
0001A3r 2               ;  the end of the dictionary with critical items such as 'fetch', 'store',
0001A3r 2               ;  'immediate', 'compile, 'semis', 'exit, and ancillary stuff.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;       When $A003 is run from WozMon, a jump instruction to 'main' is
0001A3r 2               ;  copied to $0600, followed by the dictionary, with 'exit' at the end.
0001A3r 2               ;  HyForth is start by running '600R' or 'A000R'.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;                   ZERO PAGE USAGE
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;   Task ZP (see TASK_ZP in defines.s): allocated top-down from $FF, so this
0001A3r 2               ;   list runs from the top of ZP down.  The addresses are unchanged.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP_BEGIN
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;          NXTTOK, BACKHEAP, TEMP5 - 7
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP7, 2       ;    AUTOLOAD                           $FE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP6, 1       ;    WORDS, DIGCONT, TEXTGET            $FD
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP5, 1       ;    WORDS, WFIND                       $FC
0001A3r 2               FFLAG = TEMP5          ; 'find flag' for wfind entry point
0001A3r 2               supprint = TEMP5       ; suppress printing in MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP BACKHEAP, 2    ; hold 'here while compile              $FA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP NXTTOK, 2      ; next token in tib (INBUF)             $F8
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                    TEMP1 - 4
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP4, 2       ; fourth  (two bytes)                   $F6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP3, 2       ; third  (two bytes)                    $F4
0001A3r 2               ramstart = TEMP3       ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP2, 2       ; second                                $F2
0001A3r 2               endsoff = TEMP2        ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP1, 2       ; first                                 $F0
0001A3r 2               mainoff = TEMP1        ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   pointer registers
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP WORKREG, 2     ; working register                      $EE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP INSTPTR, 2     ; instruction pointer                   $EC
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP RTPTR, 2       ; return stack pointer                  $EA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DSPTR, 2       ; data stack pointer                    $E8
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   internal Forth
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP NEXTHEAP, 2    ; next free cell in heap dictionary     $E6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP LASTHEAP, 2    ; last link cell                        $E4
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP CURBUF, 2      ; CURBUF next free byte in TIB          $E2
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP STATUS, 2      ; state at lsb, last size+flag at msb   $E0
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   HyForth setup stuff
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP ALFLAG, 1      ; autoload flag                         $DF
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP RSEED, 4       ; random # seed                         $DB
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DIGBASE, 1     ; base for number conversion            $DA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP ERRPTR, 2      ; ptr to mitigation/message             $D8
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP ERRFLAG, 1     ; error type, 0 = none                  $D7
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DFLAG, 1       ; debug flag                            $D6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TIBEND, 2      ; pointer to end of TIB                 $D4
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TIB, 2         ; pointer to input buffer               $D2
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMLAST, 2     ;  malloc                               $D0
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMPTR, 2      ;   malloc                              $CE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP9, 2       ;   Imm                                 $CC
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP8, 2       ;  Hstring macro                        $CA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP0, 2       ;  DUMPREG                              $C8
0001A3r 2               ZPSTART = TEMP0        ; CLEAR zeroes ZPSTART-$FF; the MMU variables below are set up by 'cold'
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   MMU (not cleared by CLEAR)
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMBOT, 2      ; malloc arena bottom                   $C6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMTOPV, 2     ; malloc arena top (MEMLAST starts here) $C4
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMHND, 1      ; MMU handle of the arena               $C3
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DICTLIM, 1     ; MMU page floor: 'here' stays below it $C2
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP HLBANK, 1      ; RAM bank saved by hlock               $C1
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP IOERR, 1       ; the last IO error (ioerr)             $C0
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP_END
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; *** $DO-$FF total usage in ZP, including TEMP vars ***
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;   HYDRA-16 serial/read buffers at $200 and $300 so skip those
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;                   FORTH STACKS
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000000r 2               INBUF:
000000r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 256
000004r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000008r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000100r 2               DS:                          ; data stack (S)
000100r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000104r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000108r 2  xx xx xx xx  
00017Er 2  xx xx              .res 2
000180r 2               RT:                          ; return stack (R)
000180r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000184r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000188r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0001FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000200r 2               MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
000200r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 510
000204r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000208r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0003FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000400r 2               ;
000400r 2               ;
000400r 2               .segment "FORTH_ROM"              ; regular core
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; ************ the real deal...
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               
000000r 2               forth_main:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp cold
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp COPYTORAM
000006r 2               
000006r 2               HYPROMPT:
000006r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
000008r 2  48 46 3E         .byte "HF>"
00000Br 2  00               .byte 0
00000Cr 2               ;
00000Cr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00000Cr 2               WDISP:
00000Cr 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00000Er 2  57 3D            .byte "W="
000010r 2  00               .byte 0
000011r 2               WATDISP:
000011r 2  20 20 5B 57      .byte "  [W]="
000015r 2  5D 3D        
000017r 2  00               .byte 0
000018r 2               .endif
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               cold:
000018r 2  D8               cld
000019r 2  20 rr rr         jsr CLEAR          ; zero out zero page, INBUF, DS, and RT
00001Cr 2               
00001Cr 2               ; Forth owns this task's MMU memory: free anything left from before (only the MMU area: typed-ahead
00001Cr 2               ; input in the message rings is kept), then get the malloc arena
00001Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_TASK_INIT
00001Fr 2  64 C6            stz MEMBOT                 ; no arena (every small malloc fails) unless MM_ALLOC works
000021r 2  64 C7            stz MEMBOT+1
000023r 2  64 C4            stz MEMTOPV
000025r 2  64 C5            stz MEMTOPV+1
000027r 2  64 C2            stz DICTLIM
000029r 2  A9 00            lda #<FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
00002Br 2  A0 08            ldy #>FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
00002Dr 2  A2 00            ldx #0
00002Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_ALLOC               ; whole pages at the top of task RAM
000032r 2  B0 1B            bcs NOARENA
000034r 2  85 C3            sta MEMHND
000036r 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_LOCK                ; .A.Y = address (page blocks don't move), .X = RAM bank
000039r 2  85 C6            sta MEMBOT
00003Br 2  84 C7            sty MEMBOT+1
00003Dr 2  A5 C3            lda MEMHND
00003Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_UNLOCK
000042r 2  A5 C6            lda MEMBOT
000044r 2  18               clc
000045r 2  69 00            adc #<FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
000047r 2  85 C4            sta MEMTOPV
000049r 2  A5 C7            lda MEMBOT+1
00004Br 2  69 08            adc #>FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
00004Dr 2  85 C5            sta MEMTOPV+1
00004Fr 2               NOARENA:
00004Fr 2               
00004Fr 2               warm:
00004Fr 2               ; link list of headers
00004Fr 2  A9 rr            lda #>h_exit               ; initialize HEAP pointers
000051r 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
000053r 2  A9 rr            lda #<h_exit
000055r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP
000057r 2               
000057r 2               ; next heap free cell
000057r 2  A9 rr            lda #>ends + 1
000059r 2  85 E7            sta NEXTHEAP + 1
00005Br 2  64 E6            stz NEXTHEAP
00005Dr 2  64 D7            stz ERRFLAG                ; clear ERROR flag
00005Fr 2               
00005Fr 2               
00005Fr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>(MEMSTK+MEMEND)       ; initialize memory manager area
000061r 2  84 CF            sty MEMPTR + 1
000063r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<(MEMSTK+MEMEND)
000065r 2  84 CE            sty MEMPTR
000067r 2  A4 C4            ldy MEMTOPV                 ; malloc records grow down from the top of the arena
000069r 2  84 D0            sty MEMLAST
00006Br 2  A4 C5            ldy MEMTOPV+1
00006Dr 2  84 D1            sty MEMLAST+1
00006Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK                 ; keep the MMU out of the pages above 'here'
000072r 2               
000072r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000072r 2               ; various reinitialization points
000072r 2               ;
000072r 2               reset:
000072r 2  A0 FD            ldy #INBUF_end
000074r 2  84 D4            sty TIBEND
000076r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<INBUF
000078r 2  84 D2            sty TIB
00007Ar 2  A0 rr            ldy #>INBUF
00007Cr 2  84 D3            sty TIB+1
00007Er 2  84 D5            sty TIBEND+1
000080r 2  84 E3            sty CURBUF+1
000082r 2  84 F9            sty NXTTOK+1
000084r 2               
000084r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>DS                     ; DS and RT are now half page each
000086r 2  84 E9            sty DSPTR + 1
000088r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>RT
00008Ar 2  84 EB            sty RTPTR + 1
00008Cr 2               
00008Cr 2  A9 01            lda #1                       ; DEBUG OFF by default
00008Er 2  85 D6            sta DFLAG
000090r 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG                   ; autoload flag OFF
000092r 2               
000092r 2               abort:                            ; clear DS
000092r 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
000094r 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
000096r 2               
000096r 2               errrtn:                          ; return from error
000096r 2               quit:                             ; clear RT
000096r 2  A0 FE            ldy #<RTEND
000098r 2  84 EA            sty RTPTR
00009Ar 2               
00009Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr wrterror                 ; print any error messages
00009Dr 2  A0 00            ldy #0          ; reset INBUF
00009Fr 2  A9 00            lda #0
0000A1r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB),y     ; clear INBUF stuff
0000A3r 2  64 E2            stz CURBUF    ; clear cursor  (pointer into INBUF)
0000A5r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS    ; status is 'interpret' == \0
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2  2C               .byte $2c       ; mask next two bytes, nice trick !
0000A8r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000A8r 2               ; the outer loop
0000A8r 2               
0000A8r 2               resolvept:
0000A8r 2  rr rr            .word okey
0000AAr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000AAr 2               okey:               ; well shit, I hope this is easy....
0000AAr 2               resolve:           ; get a token
0000AAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr token      ; then just process the regular way
0000ADr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000ADr 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG                 ; DEBUG
0000AFr 2  D0 08            bne RVPSKIP
0000B1r 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
0000B4r 2  A9 50            lda #'P'
0000B6r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR            ; DEBUG
0000B9r 2               .endif
0000B9r 2               
0000B9r 2               RVPSKIP:
0000B9r 2               
0000B9r 2               RESFIND:                ; load last or 'latest' word on heap
0000B9r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
0000BBr 2  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0000BDr 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
0000BFr 2  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0000C1r 2               
0000C1r 2               RESLOOP:              ; lsb linked list
0000C1r 2  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0000C3r 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG             ; so 'last' -> W
0000C5r 2  05 F3            ora TEMP2+1             ; only zero if both are zero
0000C7r 2  D0 12            bne RESEACH              ; PGS - did he forget this?
0000C9r 2               
0000C9r 2               WORDNOTFOUND:
0000C9r 2  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG            ; keep an error already raised (e.g. out of memory for a q^...^ string)
0000CBr 2  D0 04            bne WNFERR
0000CDr 2  A9 05            lda #ERR_UKW           ; UNKNOWN WORD error
0000CFr 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000D1r 2               WNFERR:
0000D1r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn ; end of dictionary, no more words to search, abort
0000D4r 2               ;
0000D4r 2               DICTFULL:                  ; the dictionary would grow into memory the MMU has allocated
0000D4r 2  A9 04            lda #ERR_MEM
0000D6r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000D8r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
0000DBr 2               
0000DBr 2               RESEACH:                        ; msb linked list
0000DBr 2  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
0000DDr 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1           ; update next link
0000DFr 2               
0000DFr 2  A2 EE            ldx #WORKREG
0000E1r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000E3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                  ; W += 2, now pointing at size/flag byte from 'here'
0000E6r 2  A0 00            ldy #0              ; compare words
0000E8r 2  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG), y    ; save the flag, first byte is (size and flag)
0000EAr 2  85 E1            sta STATUS + 1
0000ECr 2                           ;; *** start of mod for bit check
0000ECr 2  29 3F            and #$3F            ; mask off flags
0000EEr 2  38               sec
0000EFr 2  F1 F8            sbc (NXTTOK), y    ; compare lengths
0000F1r 2  D0 CE            bne RESLOOP
0000F3r 2  C8               iny
0000F4r 2               ; compare chars
0000F4r 2               RESEQUAL:
0000F4r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0000F6r 2  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE            ; space ends
0000F8r 2  F0 09            beq RESDONE
0000FAr 2  38               sec                 ; verify
0000FBr 2  F1 EE            sbc (WORKREG), y
0000FDr 2  0A               asl                 ; clean 7-bit ascii
0000FEr 2  D0 C1            bne RESLOOP
000100r 2  C8               iny                 ; get next char
000101r 2  D0 F1            bne RESEQUAL
000103r 2               
000103r 2               RESDONE:
000103r 2  98               tya                ; increment W by y, W will point at CFA?
000104r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000107r 2               
000107r 2               eval:
000107r 2               ; executing ? if status = 0
000107r 2  A5 E0            lda STATUS
000109r 2  F0 20            beq execute
00010Br 2               ;
00010Br 2               ; falls thru on compile, but...
00010Br 2               ; immediate ? if status+1 < 0 (bit seven set)
00010Br 2  A5 E1            lda STATUS + 1
00010Dr 2  30 1C            bmi immediate
00010Fr 2               
00010Fr 2               compile:          ; otherwise compile
00010Fr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00010Fr 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG          ; DEBUG, print C if here
000111r 2  D0 08            bne CMPSKIP
000113r 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
000116r 2  A9 43            lda #'C'
000118r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
00011Br 2               CMPSKIP:
00011Br 2               .endif
00011Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK         ; room for the dictionary to grow?
00011Er 2  B0 03            bcs CMPROOM
000120r 2  4C rr rr         jmp DICTFULL
000123r 2               CMPROOM:
000123r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma          ; copy W into NEXTHEAP ('here'), increment NEXTHEAP
000126r 2  B0 03            bcs immediate
000128r 2  4C rr rr         jmp resolve         ; if not 'immediate' go on to next token
00012Br 2               ;
00012Br 2               immediate:
00012Br 2               execute:
00012Br 2               
00012Br 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00012Br 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG         ; DEBUG, print E if here
00012Dr 2  D0 08            bne EXESKIP
00012Fr 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
000132r 2  A9 45            lda #'E'
000134r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000137r 2               EXESKIP:
000137r 2               .endif
000137r 2  A9 rr            lda #>resolvept     ; set up INSTPTR to run,
000139r 2  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1     ; or return to interpreter.
00013Br 2  A9 rr            lda #<resolvept
00013Dr 2  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
00013Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp pick             ; almost done, 'next' and either ENTER or EXEC
000142r 2               
000142r 2               ;-----------------------START PROCESSING INPUT-----------------------
000142r 2               try:
000142r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y                   ; index is in y
000144r 2  F0 04            beq getline    ; if \0  - get a line if pointing at 0
000146r 2  C8               iny
000147r 2  49 20            eor #ASCII_SPACE    ; return 0 in  A if a space
000149r 2  60               rts
00014Ar 2               
00014Ar 2               ;--------------------GET AN INPUT LINE ENDING WITH CR/LF ------------
00014Ar 2               getline:   ; drop rts of try, fall through to 'token'
00014Ar 2  68               pla
00014Br 2  68               pla
00014Cr 2               ;
00014Cr 2               ;   DO AUTOLOAD HERE
00014Cr 2               ;      load a space, then copy next line to buffer
00014Cr 2               ;      calc y (length + 1) jump to GETLNEND
00014Cr 2               ;
00014Cr 2  A5 DF            lda ALFLAG
00014Er 2  F0 06            beq GLNORMAL
000150r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADTIB
000153r 2  4C rr rr         jmp GETLNSKIPCRLF
000156r 2               
000156r 2               GLNORMAL:
000156r 2  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw HYPROMPT    ; print prompt
00015Ar 2  rr rr F0 06  
00015Er 2  C8 20 rr rr  
000165r 2               ;
000165r 2  A0 00            ldy #0   ; leave the first
000167r 2               GETLOOP:
000167r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; dummy store on first pass, overwritten
000169r 2  C8               iny
00016Ar 2  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
00016Cr 2  F0 19            beq GETLNEND
00016Er 2  C0 FF            cpy #$FF
000170r 2  D0 02            bne GETREADLOOP
000172r 2  A0 01            ldy #1
000174r 2               GETREADLOOP:
000174r 2  20 rr rr         jsr GET_CHAR      ; (sleeps until a key comes in)
000177r 2  90 FB            bcc GETREADLOOP
000179r 2  C9 0D            cmp #ASCII_CR
00017Br 2  F0 0A            beq GETLNEND
00017Dr 2  C9 08            cmp #ASCII_BACKSPACE         ; handle backspace
00017Fr 2  D0 E6            bne GETLOOP
000181r 2  88               dey
000182r 2  88               dey
000183r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y      ; make sure prev char not overwritten
000185r 2  80 E0            bra GETLOOP
000187r 2               GETLNEND:                ; clear all if y eq \0
000187r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CRLF
00018Ar 2               GETLNSKIPCRLF:          ; SKIP to here if don't want CRLF
00018Ar 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
00018Cr 2  5A               phy
00018Dr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
00018Fr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y       ; start with space
000191r 2  7A               ply
000192r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y        ; ends with space
000194r 2  A9 00            lda #0            ; mark eol with 0
000196r 2  C8               iny
000197r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y
000199r 2  88               dey
00019Ar 2               ; start it
00019Ar 2  85 E2            sta CURBUF
00019Cr 2               
00019Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Cr 2               ; in place every token,
00019Cr 2               ; the counter is placed at last space before word
00019Cr 2               ; no rewinds
00019Cr 2               token:
00019Cr 2  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF   ; last position on INBUF
00019Er 2               
00019Er 2               TOKENSKIP:   ; skip spaces
00019Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001A1r 2  F0 FB            beq TOKENSKIP
0001A3r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <start of input word> + 1
0001A4r 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001A6r 2               
0001A6r 2               TOKENSCAN:  ; scan spaces
0001A6r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001A9r 2  D0 FB            bne TOKENSCAN
0001ABr 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <end of input word> + 1
0001ACr 2  84 E2            sty CURBUF
0001AEr 2               
0001AEr 2               TOKENDONE:  ; find size and store it;
0001AEr 2  98               tya
0001AFr 2  38               sec
0001B0r 2  E5 F8            sbc NXTTOK
0001B2r 2  A4 F8            ldy NXTTOK    ; keep it
0001B4r 2  88               dey
0001B5r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; store size for counted string
0001B7r 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001B9r 2                   ;
0001B9r 2                   ;  During interpretive mode at least...do number and string capture here, before
0001B9r 2                   ;     looking at word list; will be pushed on stack.
0001B9r 2                   ;  This SHOULD have a general digit converter (any base up to 16); return C = 1 if no conversion
0001B9r 2                   ;   if SINGLE is defined, this will skip single digit numbers and use hardcoded ones.
0001B9r 2                   ;
0001B9r 2                   ;  Following check for #'s, check for quoted inline txt a la 'q^....^'
0001B9r 2                   ;
0001B9r 2               .ifdef numbers
0001B9r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001BBr 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0001BDr 2  AA               tax                ; store size in X, pass to conversion
0001BEr 2  20 rr rr         jsr DIGCONVT
0001C1r 2  B0 02            bcs CHKFERTXT       ; if some error in conversion, skip and continue processing
0001C3r 2  80 0C            bra TOKCLR0
0001C5r 2               .endif  ; 'numbers'
0001C5r 2               CHKFERTXT:
0001C5r 2  20 rr rr         jsr TEXTGET         ; returns length +4 in X
0001C8r 2  B0 18            bcs TOKENEND
0001CAr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001CCr 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
0001CEr 2  4C rr rr         jmp TOKCLR
0001D1r 2               TOKCLR0:
0001D1r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001D3r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y     ; load length again
0001D5r 2  AA               tax
0001D6r 2  E8               inx
0001D7r 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE          ; copy spaces over entire converted string
0001D9r 2               TOKCLR:
0001D9r 2  91 F8            sta (NXTTOK),y
0001DBr 2  C8               iny
0001DCr 2  CA               dex
0001DDr 2  D0 FA            bne TOKCLR
0001DFr 2               TOKNEXT:
0001DFr 2  4C rr rr         jmp token               ; and use 'token' to rebuild input buffer w/o converted #
0001E2r 2               
0001E2r 2               
0001E2r 2               TOKENEND:
0001E2r 2  18               clc     ; clean - setup token
0001E3r 2  60               rts
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               ;--------------------UTILITIES----------------------------------------
0001E4r 2               ;    A whole bunch of helper functions;
0001E4r 2               ;  wcomma / comma increment WORKREG (or another reg) and NEXTHEAP ('here')
0001E4r 2               ;  while copying into NEXTHEAP, uses incwx.
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               ;  copyinto / copyfrom copy AND increment/decrement
0001E4r 2               ;  spush/rpush - pushes something onto stacks indexed on ZP by Y (increment using addwx/incwx)
0001E4r 2               ;  spull/rpull - pulls from stacks, copies into ZP indexed by Y (decrement builtin)
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               ;  addwx/incwx/decwx do some of the inc/dec-rementing duties
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               ;   COMMA allocates memory at top of heap for 'other stuff'
0001E4r 2               ;   and makes sure heap and working reg pointers are updated.
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               ; heap linked list (moves forward)
0001E4r 2               ;
0001E4r 2               wcomma:
0001E4r 2  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG                  ; copy addr at WORKREG, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
0001E6r 2               comma:
0001E6r 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                 ; Y has source of address, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
0001E8r 2                   ; FALL THROUGH - copyinto, then rts after second incwx
0001E8r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001E8r 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
0001E8r 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
0001E8r 2               ;
0001E8r 2               copyinto:
0001E8r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
0001EBr 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
0001EDr 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx
0001F0r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
0001F3r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
0001F5r 2  4C rr rr         jmp incwx                        ; incwx ends with rts!
0001F8r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001F8r 2               ;
0001F8r 2               ; generics - PUSH, PULL, incwx/addwx, copyfrom
0001F8r 2               ;
0001F8r 2               ;------------------------PUSH a cell--------------------------------
0001F8r 2               spush_2:
0001F8r 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3       ; push TEMP3 on top
0001FAr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
0001FDr 2               spush_1:
0001FDr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2       ; push TEMP2 on top
0001FFr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000202r 2               spush_0:             ; push TEMP1 to stack, probably top of stack
000202r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1
000204r 2                    ; FALL THROUGH
000204r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000204r 2               ; PUSH a cell
000204r 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
000204r 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000204r 2               spush:
000204r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000206r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
000208r 2  C9 rr            cmp #<DS                ; ditto
00020Ar 2  F0 20            beq ptrerr_s              ; ditto
00020Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp push
00020Fr 2               rpush:
00020Fr 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000211r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR               ; ditto
000213r 2  C9 rr            cmp #<RT                ; ditto
000215r 2  F0 11            beq ptrerr_r
000217r 2               
000217r 2                   ; FALL THROUGH
000217r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000217r 2               ; classic stack backwards
000217r 2               push:
000217r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
00021Ar 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
00021Dr 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00021Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
000222r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
000225r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000227r 2  60               rts
000228r 2               ;
000228r 2               ;                      pointer error (DS or RT)
000228r 2               ptrerr_r:                      ; pop jsr off stack, throw error
000228r 2  A9 01            lda #ERR_RPTR
00022Ar 2  80 02            bra ptrerr_cont
00022Cr 2               ptrerr_s:
00022Cr 2  A9 02            lda #ERR_SPTR
00022Er 2               ptrerr_cont:
00022Er 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000230r 2  68               pla
000231r 2  68               pla
000232r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
000235r 2               ;
000235r 2               ;---------------- PULL a cell, with convenience for TEMP 1/2/3 -----
000235r 2               ;
000235r 2               spull_2:
000235r 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3              ; pull TEMP3 from top
000237r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
00023Ar 2               
00023Ar 2               spull_1:                    ; pull TEMP2 from top
00023Ar 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
00023Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
00023Fr 2               
00023Fr 2               spull_0:
00023Fr 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1             ; pull TEMP1 from top of DS
000241r 2               ;
000241r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000241r 2               ;
000241r 2               ; PULL a cell
000241r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000241r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000241r 2               ;
000241r 2               spull:
000241r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000243r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR         ; pointer bounds checking
000245r 2  C9 7E            cmp #DSEND        ; ditto
000247r 2  F0 E3            beq ptrerr_s      ; ditto
000249r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pull          ; pull includes rts from incwx so....
00024Cr 2               rpull:                ;
00024Cr 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
00024Er 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR        ; pointer bounds checking
000250r 2  C9 FE            cmp #RTEND        ; ditto
000252r 2  F0 D4            beq ptrerr_r        ; ditto
000254r 2               ;
000254r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000254r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000254r 2               ;
000254r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000254r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000254r 2               pull:
000254r 2               copyfrom:
000254r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000256r 2  99 00 00         sta 0, y
000259r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx      ; NOTE:  not a jmp, returns here.
00025Cr 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
00025Er 2  99 01 00         sta 1, y
000261r 2                ;
000261r 2                ;  FALL THROUGH
000261r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000261r 2               ; increment a word in page zero. offset by X
000261r 2               ;
000261r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr incwx/addwx
000261r 2               ;
000261r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000261r 2               ;   zeropage pointers, and nothing else.  Offsets indexed by
000261r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000261r 2               ;   pointer location.
000261r 2               ;
000261r 2               incwx:
000261r 2  A9 01            lda #01
000263r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000263r 2               ; add a byte in A to a word in page zero. offset by X
000263r 2               addwx:
000263r 2  18               clc
000264r 2  75 00            adc 0, x
000266r 2  95 00            sta 0, x
000268r 2  90 03            bcc addwx_end
00026Ar 2  F6 01            inc 1, x
00026Cr 2  18               clc      ; keep carry clean.
00026Dr 2                            ; our convention is that functions SET the carry for positive results,
00026Dr 2                            ; clear carry for negative or neutral ones.
00026Dr 2               addwx_end:
00026Dr 2  60               rts
00026Er 2               
00026Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00026Er 2               ;          decwx, heap moves
00026Er 2               ; decrement a word in page zero. offset by X
00026Er 2               ;
00026Er 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr decwx
00026Er 2               ;
00026Er 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
00026Er 2               ;   zeropage pointers, and nothing else.  Offsets indexed by
00026Er 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
00026Er 2               ;   pointer location.
00026Er 2               ;
00026Er 2               decwx:
00026Er 2  B5 00            lda 0, x
000270r 2  D0 02            bne decwx_end
000272r 2  D6 01            dec 1, x
000274r 2               decwx_end:
000274r 2  D6 00            dec 0, x
000276r 2  60               rts
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ENGINEEND:
000277r 2               ;                           END OF CORE ENGINE
000277r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ;                        DICTIONARY and ADDITIONS
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               ;    upper.s -- error messaging, CLEAR on start, debug, utilities
000277r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000277r 2               ;
000277r 2               upper_ram:
000277r 2                  .include "upper.s"
000277r 3               ;
000277r 3               ;   upper.s  - utility functions for HyForth - eventually ROM resident
000277r 3               ;
000277r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000277r 3               ;  error messaging
000277r 3               ;
000277r 3               wrterror:
000277r 3  A9 rr            lda #>err_jumptable
000279r 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
00027Br 3  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG
00027Dr 3  F0 28            beq ERREND
00027Fr 3  0A               asl a
000280r 3  18               clc
000281r 3  69 rr            adc #<err_jumptable
000283r 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
000285r 3  90 02            bcc ERRSKIP
000287r 3  E6 D9            inc ERRPTR+1
000289r 3               ERRSKIP:
000289r 3  20 rr rr A0      WERR ERRPTR
00028Dr 3  00 B1 D8 85  
000291r 3  C8 C8 B1 D8  
0002A7r 3               ERREND:
0002A7r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0002A9r 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0002ABr 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
0002ADr 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
0002AFr 3  60               rts
0002B0r 3               ;
0002B0r 3               ;
0002B0r 3               err_jumptable:
0002B0r 3  xx xx            .res 2
0002B2r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry RPTR_ERR              ; RT stack full/empty  - error $01
0002B4r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SPTR_ERR              ; DS stack full/empty  - error $02
0002B6r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry DIV_ERR               ; divide by zero - error $03
0002B8r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry OOM_ERR               ; out of memory  - error $04
0002BAr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry UKW_ERR               ; no existing word - error $05
0002BCr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SEC_ERR               ; writing to dangerous RAM areas - error $06
0002BEr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SYS_ERR               ; error on return from SYSCALL - error $07
0002C0r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry IO_ERR                ; IO error (see ioerr) - error $08
0002C2r 3               LASTERR = 8
0002C2r 3               ;
0002C2r 3               ;  error messages
0002C2r 3               RPTR_ERR:
0002C2r 3  20 21 52 54      .byte " !RT PTR ERROR!"
0002C6r 3  20 50 54 52  
0002CAr 3  20 45 52 52  
0002D1r 3  00               .byte 0
0002D2r 3               SPTR_ERR:
0002D2r 3  20 21 44 53      .byte " !DS PTR ERROR!"
0002D6r 3  20 50 54 52  
0002DAr 3  20 45 52 52  
0002E1r 3  00               .byte 0
0002E2r 3               DIV_ERR:
0002E2r 3  20 21 44 49      .byte " !DIV ZERO!"
0002E6r 3  56 20 5A 45  
0002EAr 3  52 4F 21     
0002EDr 3  00               .byte 0
0002EEr 3               OOM_ERR:

000326r 3               ;
000326r 3               ;           CODE called by engine AND heap
000326r 3               ;

000326r 3               ;

000326r 3

000328r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpull                       ; IP = [RTPTR], RTPTR += 2
00032Br 3

00032Br 3  A2 EC            ldx #INSTPTR
00032Dr 3  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG
00032Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                    ; W = [IP], IP += 2  ( and either ENTER or EXECUTE )
000332r 3

000365r 3               ;
000365r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1                 ; compare pages (MSBs)
000367r 3  C9 rr            cmp #>ends + 1                  ;  !! Compiled must be higher in memory than hardcoded !! (see below)
000369r 3  30 16            bmi jump                        ; jump over nest if native code
00036Br 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; ldy #0 if check extra byte
00036Dr 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
00036Fr 3  F0 10            beq jump                        ; yep, jump to native execution
000371r 3               
000371r 3               nest:                               ; ENTER in classic Forth lingo   ( COMPILED )
000371r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
000373r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpush                       ; RTPTR -=2, [RTPTR] = [IP]
000376r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG                     ; W = IP

00037Ar 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1
00037Cr 3  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1

000381r 3

000381r 3  6C EE 00         jmp (WORKREG)                   ; start running code at next word
000384r 3                                                   ;  JUMP [W]
000384r 3               ;
000384r 3               ;------- used by 'autoload'
000384r 3               ALOADTIB:
000384r 3  A0 00            ldy #0

000398r 3               
000398r 3               ALOADCHKDONE:
000398r 3  C8               iny
000399r 3  B1 FE            lda (TEMP7),y

0003A1r 3               ALNOTSKIP:
0003A1r 3  98               tya
0003A2r 3  18               clc

0003A5r 3  85 FE            sta TEMP7

0003A9r 3  E6 FF            inc TEMP7+1

0003ABr 3  88               dey
0003ACr 3  60               rts
0003ADr 3               ;
0003ADr 3               ;-------- malloc and mlen
0003ADr 3               
0003ADr 3               MALFAIL:
0003ADr 3  18               clc                   ; out of memory
0003AEr 3  60               rts
0003AFr 3               MALLOC:
0003AFr 3                   ;  TEMP1 and TEMP2 should have bytes / record type if 'jsr MALLOC'
0003AFr 3                   ;  OUT: C = 1 and TEMP1 = memory stack slot (holds the record address); C = 0 if out of memory
0003AFr 3                   ;  Records smaller than FORTH_LARGE_MIN go in the arena (MEMLAST grows down, not below MEMBOT);
0003AFr 3                   ;  bigger ones get their own MMU block, marked with MEM_MMU in the type byte.
0003AFr 3                   ;  uses TEMP3, TEMP4, y, x, a
0003AFr 3  A5 CE            lda MEMPTR           ; memory stack full?
0003B1r 3  C9 rr            cmp #<MEMSTK
0003B3r 3  A5 CF            lda MEMPTR+1
0003B5r 3  E9 rr            sbc #>MEMSTK
0003B7r 3  90 F4            bcc MALFAIL
0003B9r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0003BBr 3  D0 30            bne MALLARGE
0003BDr 3                                       ; arena: new record at MEMLAST - 3 - bytes, into TEMP4
0003BDr 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
0003BFr 3  38               sec
0003C0r 3  E9 03            sbc #3
0003C2r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
0003C4r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
0003C6r 3  E9 00            sbc #0
0003C8r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1
0003CAr 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
0003CCr 3  38               sec
0003CDr 3  E5 F0            sbc TEMP1
0003CFr 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
0003D1r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
0003D3r 3  E5 F1            sbc TEMP1+1
0003D5r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1
0003D7r 3  90 D4            bcc MALFAIL         ; wrapped
0003D9r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
0003DBr 3  C5 C6            cmp MEMBOT
0003DDr 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
0003DFr 3  E5 C7            sbc MEMBOT+1
0003E1r 3  90 CA            bcc MALFAIL         ; below the arena
0003E3r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
0003E5r 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
0003E7r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
0003E9r 3  85 D1            sta MEMLAST+1        ;MEMLAST updated to start of new record
0003EBr 3  80 27            bra MALHDR
0003EDr 3               MALLARGE:               ; its own MMU block: bytes + 3 for the header
0003EDr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0003EFr 3  18               clc
0003F0r 3  69 03            adc #3
0003F2r 3  48               pha
0003F3r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0003F5r 3  69 00            adc #0
0003F7r 3  A8               tay
0003F8r 3  68               pla
0003F9r 3  B0 B2            bcs MALFAIL          ; more than $FFFF
0003FBr 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0003FDr 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_ALLOC         ; .A = handle
000400r 3  B0 AB            bcs MALFAIL
000402r 3  48               pha
000403r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_LOCK          ; .A.Y = address (page blocks don't move), .X = RAM bank
000406r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
000408r 3  84 F7            sty TEMP4+1
00040Ar 3  68               pla
00040Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_UNLOCK
00040Er 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000410r 3  09 40            ora #MEM_MMU
000412r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000414r 3               MALHDR:
000414r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000416r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000418r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
00041Ar 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1         ; use TEMP3 to walk through clearing of memory
00041Cr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00041Er 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; write type first
000420r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000422r 3  C8               iny
000423r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1            ; LSB length
000425r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000427r 3  C8               iny
000428r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1          ; MSB length
00042Ar 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
00042Cr 3  A2 F4            ldx #TEMP3
00042Er 3  A9 03            lda #3
000430r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx            ; increment TEMP3 by 3
000433r 3               MALLOOP:                 ; clear TEMP1 bytes at TEMP3
000433r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000435r 3  05 F1            ora TEMP1+1
000437r 3  F0 14            beq MALCONT
000439r 3  A9 00            lda #0
00043Br 3  92 F4            sta (TEMP3)
00043Dr 3  E6 F4            inc TEMP3
00043Fr 3  D0 02            bne MALSK01
000441r 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
000443r 3               MALSK01:
000443r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000445r 3  D0 02            bne MALSK02
000447r 3  C6 F1            dec TEMP1+1
000449r 3               MALSK02:
000449r 3  C6 F0            dec TEMP1
00044Br 3  80 E6            bra MALLOOP
00044Dr 3               MALCONT:                   ; now store the record address at MEMPTR
00044Dr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00044Fr 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000451r 3  91 CE            sta (MEMPTR),y
000453r 3  C8               iny
000454r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000456r 3  91 CE            sta (MEMPTR),y
000458r 3  A5 CF            lda MEMPTR+1
00045Ar 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00045Cr 3  A5 CE            lda MEMPTR
00045Er 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1             ; copy to TEMP1 before incrementing
000460r 3  38               sec                   ; MEMPTR + 2
000461r 3  E9 02            sbc #2
000463r 3  85 CE            sta MEMPTR
000465r 3  B0 02            bcs MALLOCEND
000467r 3  C6 CF            dec MEMPTR+1
000469r 3               MALLOCEND:
000469r 3  38               sec                   ; OK
00046Ar 3  60               rts
00046Br 3               ;
00046Br 3               ;  Keep the MMU from handing out the pages the dictionary grows into: raise the MMU page floor
00046Br 3               ;  (DICTLIM) to FORTH_DICT_MARGIN pages above 'here' when needed.
00046Br 3               ;  OUT: C = 1 OK; C = 0 the dictionary is full (the MMU has allocated those pages)
00046Br 3               ;  uses a, x, y
00046Br 3               DICTCHK:
00046Br 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP+1
00046Dr 3  18               clc
00046Er 3  69 03            adc #FORTH_DICT_MARGIN + 1   ; floor wanted: page of 'here' + margin + 1
000470r 3  C5 C2            cmp DICTLIM
000472r 3  90 0B            bcc DICTOK                   ; already at or above it
000474r 3  F0 09            beq DICTOK
000476r 3  48               pha
000477r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_SET_FLOOR             ; MMU: C = 0 OK
00047Ar 3  68               pla
00047Br 3  B0 04            bcs DICTNO
00047Dr 3  85 C2            sta DICTLIM
00047Fr 3               DICTOK:
00047Fr 3  38               sec
000480r 3  60               rts
000481r 3               DICTNO:
000481r 3  18               clc
000482r 3  60               rts
000483r 3               ;
000483r 3               ;  Free a large (MEM_MMU) record's MMU block.  IN: TEMP3 = record address.  uses a, x, y
000483r 3               MMUFREE:
000483r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
000485r 3  A4 F5            ldy TEMP3+1
000487r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_FIND                  ; .A = handle
00048Ar 3  B0 03            bcs MMUFREEND
00048Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_FREE
00048Fr 3               MMUFREEND:
00048Fr 3  60               rts
000490r 3               ;
000490r 3               MEMLEN:              ; address in TEMP2
000490r 3  A0 00             ldy #0
000492r 3  B1 F2             lda (TEMP2),y
000494r 3  85 F4             sta TEMP3
000496r 3  C8                iny
000497r 3  B1 F2             lda (TEMP2),y   ; and deref once
000499r 3  85 F5             sta TEMP3+1
00049Br 3  A0 01             ldy #1
00049Dr 3  B1 F4             lda (TEMP3),y   ; skip over type, get length
00049Fr 3  85 F0             sta TEMP1
0004A1r 3  C8                iny
0004A2r 3  B1 F4             lda (TEMP3),y
0004A4r 3  85 F1             sta TEMP1+1
0004A6r 3  60                rts
0004A7r 3               ;
0004A7r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
0004A7r 3               ;                MATH routines
0004A7r 3               ;         with MULT16 / DIV16, signs handled by calling word.
0004A7r 3               ;         we just do the math here.
0004A7r 3               ;
0004A7r 3               ;
0004A7r 3               MULT16:                             ; 16 x 16 multiply; TEMP1 and TEMP2 are #'s, TEMP1 will be result
0004A7r 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3                       ; with TEMP3 as high bytes
0004A9r 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1
0004ABr 3  A2 11            ldx #17
0004ADr 3  18               clc
0004AEr 3               MULTLOOP:
0004AEr 3  66 F5            ror TEMP3+1                     ; RIGHT.  if you need to go backwards, go backwards stupid fuck.
0004B0r 3  66 F4            ror TEMP3
0004B2r 3  66 F1            ror TEMP1+1
0004B4r 3  66 F0            ror TEMP1
0004B6r 3  90 0D            bcc MULTDECCNT
0004B8r 3  18               clc
0004B9r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0004BBr 3  65 F4            adc TEMP3
0004BDr 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0004BFr 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2+1
0004C1r 3  65 F5            adc TEMP3+1
0004C3r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0004C5r 3               MULTDECCNT:
0004C5r 3  CA               dex
0004C6r 3  D0 E6            bne MULTLOOP
0004C8r 3  60               rts
0004C9r 3               
0004C9r 3               ;
0004C9r 3               ;
0004C9r 3                      ; 16 x 16 divide; TEMP1 and TEMP2 #'s - TEMP3 is 'overflow'
0004C9r 3                      ;  TEMP2 divisor, TEMP1 dividend, TEMP1 + 3 = result + remainder
0004C9r 3               DIV16:
0004C9r 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3
0004CBr 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1
0004CDr 3  A2 10            ldx #16
0004CFr 3               UDIVLP:
0004CFr 3  26 F0            rol TEMP1
0004D1r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
0004D3r 3  26 F4            rol TEMP3
0004D5r 3  26 F5            rol TEMP3+1
0004D7r 3               UDIVCHK:
0004D7r 3  38               sec
0004D8r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
0004DAr 3  E5 F2            sbc TEMP2
0004DCr 3  A8               tay
0004DDr 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
0004DFr 3  E5 F3            sbc TEMP2+1
0004E1r 3  90 04            bcc UDIVCNT
0004E3r 3  84 F4            sty TEMP3
0004E5r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0004E7r 3               UDIVCNT:
0004E7r 3  CA               dex
0004E8r 3  D0 E5            bne UDIVLP
0004EAr 3  26 F0            rol TEMP1
0004ECr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
0004EEr 3  60               rts
0004EFr 3               ;
0004EFr 3               ;     galois32o - LSFR psuedo-random # generator
0004EFr 3               ;
0004EFr 3               ;  -- boilerplate --
0004EFr 3               ; 6502 LFSR PRNG - 32-bit
0004EFr 3               ; Brad Smith, 2019
0004EFr 3               ; http://rainwarrior.ca
0004EFr 3               ;
0004EFr 3               ;
0004EFr 3               galois32o:
0004EFr 3                   ; rotate the middle bytes left
0004EFr 3  A4 DD            ldy RSEED+2                     ; will move to RSEED+3 at the end
0004F1r 3  A5 DC            lda RSEED+1
0004F3r 3  85 DD            sta RSEED+2
0004F5r 3                   ; compute RSEED+1 ($C5>>1 = %1100010)
0004F5r 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
0004F7r 3  4A               lsr
0004F8r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1                     ; reverse: 100011
0004FAr 3  4A               lsr
0004FBr 3  4A               lsr
0004FCr 3  4A               lsr
0004FDr 3  4A               lsr
0004FEr 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
000500r 3  4A               lsr
000501r 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
000503r 3  45 DB            eor RSEED+0                     ; combine with original low byte
000505r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1
000507r 3                   ; compute RSEED+0 ($C5 = %11000101)
000507r 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000509r 3  0A               asl
00050Ar 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
00050Cr 3  0A               asl
00050Dr 3  0A               asl
00050Er 3  0A               asl
00050Fr 3  0A               asl
000510r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000512r 3  0A               asl
000513r 3  0A               asl
000514r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000516r 3  84 DE            sty RSEED+3                     ; finish rotating byte 2 into 3
000518r 3  85 DB            sta RSEED+0
00051Ar 3  60               rts
00051Br 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
00051Br 3               ;              get delimited text from INBUF, store in string
00051Br 3               ;
00051Br 3               
00051Br 3               .ifndef TXT2STACK
00051Br 3               ;
00051Br 3               ;  uses TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP3, TEMP5, TEMP6, X, Y
00051Br 3               ;
00051Br 3               TEXTGET:
00051Br 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1                     ; will store length here
00051Dr 3  A9 04            lda #$04
00051Fr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; record type is 'sz'
000521r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
000523r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6                       ; to save y for later copy
000525r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip len of first token
000527r 3               TX2SKSPC:
000527r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000529r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE                ; skip leading spaces
00052Br 3  D0 03            bne TX2SK00
00052Dr 3  C8               iny
00052Er 3  80 F7            bra TX2SKSPC
000530r 3               TX2SK00:
000530r 3  C9 71            cmp #ASCII_q
000532r 3  D0 6C            bne TX2NOGOOD
000534r 3  C8               iny
000535r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000537r 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
000539r 3  D0 65            bne TX2NOGOOD
00053Br 3  C8               iny
00053Cr 3  84 FD            sty TEMP6                       ; temp6 stores pos of first char
00053Er 3               TX2SCAN:
00053Er 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
000540r 3  C8               iny
000541r 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
000543r 3  F0 0A            beq TX2FOUND
000545r 3  98               tya
000546r 3  18               clc
000547r 3  65 F8            adc NXTTOK
000549r 3  C9 64            cmp #MAXSTR
00054Br 3  B0 53            bcs TX2NOGOOD
