00054Fr 3               TX2FOUND:
00054Fr 3  88               dey
000550r 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                       ; temp5 pos+1 last letter
000552r 3  98               tya
000553r 3  38               sec
000554r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
000556r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                       ; and this should be the length
000558r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1                       ; and add one for zero at end
00055Ar 3               
00055Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr MALLOC                      ; TEMP1 now has address on mem stack
00055Dr 3  B0 07            bcs TX2ROOM
00055Fr 3  A9 04            lda #ERR_MEM                    ; out of memory: reported when the token isn't found
000561r 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000563r 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX2NOGOOD
000566r 3               TX2ROOM:
000566r 3               
000566r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000568r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
00056Ar 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
00056Cr 3  C8               iny
00056Dr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
00056Fr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; address in memory area
000571r 3               
000571r 3                   ; now for math.  NXTTOK ptr needs to be ref'd with same y
000571r 3                   ; as TEMP2, so we need to match them up...and we are hitting
000571r 3                   ; the record at +3, so....TEMP6 is start
000571r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000573r 3  38               sec
000574r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
000576r 3  B0 02            bcs TX2SK01
000578r 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
00057Ar 3               TX2SK01:
00057Ar 3  18               clc
00057Br 3  69 03            adc #3
00057Dr 3  90 02            bcc TX2SK02
00057Fr 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
000581r 3               TX2SK02:
000581r 3  A4 FD            ldy TEMP6
000583r 3               TX2CPYLOOP:
000583r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000585r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
000587r 3  C8               iny
000588r 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
00058Ar 3  D0 F7            bne TX2CPYLOOP
00058Cr 3               TX2SK99:
00058Cr 3  A9 00            lda #0
00058Er 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                   ; put zero on end
000590r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push address from mem stack on DS
000593r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5
000595r 3  38               sec
000596r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
000598r 3  18               clc
000599r 3  69 04            adc #4
00059Br 3  AA               tax
00059Cr 3  18               clc
00059Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX2END
0005A0r 3               TX2NOGOOD:
0005A0r 3  38               sec
0005A1r 3               TX2END:
0005A1r 3  60               rts
0005A2r 3               ;
0005A2r 3               ;  end of TXG2  (new text capture)
0005A2r 3               ;
0005A2r 3               
0005A2r 3               .else  ;  TXT2STACK
0005A2r 3               
0005A2r 3               TEXTGET:
0005A2r 3                   stz TEMP6
0005A2r 3                   stz TEMP1
0005A2r 3                   stz TEMP1+1
0005A2r 3                   ldy #1                          ; skip len
0005A2r 3               TXTSKIPSPC:
0005A2r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0005A2r 3                   cmp #ASCII_SPACE                ; skip leading spaces
0005A2r 3                   bne TXTSPCS
0005A2r 3                   iny
0005A2r 3                   bra TXTSKIPSPC
0005A2r 3               TXTSPCS:
0005A2r 3                   cmp #ASCII_q
0005A2r 3                   bne TEXTNOGOOD
0005A2r 3                   iny
0005A2r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0005A2r 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET
0005A2r 3                   bne TEXTNOGOOD
0005A2r 3                   sta TEMP3                       ; remember....
0005A2r 3                   iny
0005A2r 3               TXTSCAN:
0005A2r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
0005A2r 3                   iny
0005A2r 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET
0005A2r 3                   beq TXTFOUND
0005A2r 3                   tya
0005A2r 3                   clc
0005A2r 3                   adc NXTTOK
0005A2r 3                   cmp #MAXSTR
0005A2r 3                   bcs TEXTNOGOOD
0005A2r 3                   bra TXTSCAN
0005A2r 3               TXTFOUND:
0005A2r 3                   ldx #0
0005A2r 3                   dey
0005A2r 3                   dey                             ; now points at last letter
0005A2r 3                   tya
0005A2r 3                   sec
0005A2r 3                   sbc TEMP3                       ; and this should be the length
0005A2r 3                   stx TEMP3
0005A2r 3                   and #1
0005A2r 3                   beq TEXTGLOOP
0005A2r 3                   inc TEMP3
0005A2r 3                   inx
0005A2r 3               TEXTGLOOP:
0005A2r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0005A2r 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET                        ; when we hit the other end again...
0005A2r 3                   beq TEXTOK
0005A2r 3                   sta TEMP6
0005A2r 3               
0005A2r 3               .ifdef DEBUG
0005A2r 3                   jsr DUMPREG                     ; DEBUG
0005A2r 3               .endif
0005A2r 3               
0005A2r 3                   txa
0005A2r 3                   and #1
0005A2r 3                   bne TEXTODD
0005A2r 3                   lda TEMP6
0005A2r 3                   sta TEMP1
0005A2r 3                   stz TEMP1+1
0005A2r 3                   bra TEXTSKIP2
0005A2r 3               TEXTODD:
0005A2r 3                   lda TEMP6
0005A2r 3                   sta TEMP1+1
0005A2r 3                   phx
0005A2r 3                   phy
0005A2r 3                   jsr spush_0
0005A2r 3                   ply
0005A2r 3                   plx
0005A2r 3               TEXTSKIP2:
0005A2r 3                   inx
0005A2r 3                   dey
0005A2r 3               .ifdef DEBUG
0005A2r 3                   jsr DUMPREG                     ; DEBUG
0005A2r 3               .endif
0005A2r 3                   bra TEXTGLOOP
0005A2r 3               TEXTOK:
0005A2r 3                   phy
0005A2r 3                   phx
0005A2r 3                   txa
0005A2r 3                   and #1
0005A2r 3                   beq TEXTCONT
0005A2r 3                   jsr spush_0
0005A2r 3               TEXTCONT:
0005A2r 3                   plx
0005A2r 3                   txa
0005A2r 3                   sec
0005A2r 3                   sbc TEMP3
0005A2r 3                   sta TEMP3
0005A2r 3                   stz TEMP3+1
0005A2r 3                   jsr spush_2                     ; push length on top
0005A2r 3                   ply
0005A2r 3                   clc
0005A2r 3                   bra TEXTGEND
0005A2r 3               TEXTNOGOOD:
0005A2r 3                   sec
0005A2r 3               TEXTGEND:
0005A2r 3                   rts
0005A2r 3               
0005A2r 3               .endif  ; ---TXT2STACK
0005A2r 3               
0005A2r 3               ;-----------------------   NUMBER CONVERSIONS
0005A2r 3               ;
0005A2r 3               DEC2ASCII:       ;  X is # - return as two digits in TEMP3, TEMP3+1
0005A2r 3  A9 30            lda #ASCII_0
0005A4r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0005A6r 3  8A               txa
0005A7r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0005A9r 3               D2ASCLOOP:
0005A9r 3  38               sec
0005AAr 3  E9 0A            sbc #ASCII_LF
0005ACr 3  90 04            bcc D2ASCNEXT
0005AEr 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
0005B0r 3  80 F7            bra D2ASCLOOP
0005B2r 3               D2ASCNEXT:
0005B2r 3  18               clc
0005B3r 3  69 3A            adc #$3A
0005B5r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0005B7r 3  60               rts
0005B8r 3               
0005B8r 3               .ifdef numbers
0005B8r 3               ;------------------------
0005B8r 3               ;      CONVERT DIGITS, PUSH on DS
0005B8r 3               
0005B8r 3               DIGCONVT:    ;  Y is index into NXTPTR, X is length
0005B8r 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
0005BAr 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0005BCr 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2                       ; no necc for bin or hex, but
0005BEr 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1                     ; since is convenient....
0005C0r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0005C2r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip over length
0005C4r 3  A9 0A            lda #10
0005C6r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0005C8r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0005CAr 3  C9 2D            cmp #ASCII_MINUS
0005CCr 3  F0 0A            beq DIGCONV_MINUS
0005CEr 3  C9 24            cmp #ASCII_DOLLAR
0005D0r 3  F0 12            beq DIGHEX1
0005D2r 3  C9 25            cmp #ASCII_PERCENT
0005D4r 3  F0 07            beq DIGBIN1
0005D6r 3  80 12            bra DIG_SNG
0005D8r 3               DIGCONV_MINUS:
0005D8r 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
0005DAr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
0005DDr 3               DIGBIN1:
0005DDr 3  A9 02            lda #2
0005DFr 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0005E1r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
0005E4r 3               DIGHEX1:
0005E4r 3  A9 10            lda #16
0005E6r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0005E8r 3               DIG_XY:
0005E8r 3  C8               iny
0005E9r 3  CA               dex
0005EAr 3               DIG_SNG:
0005EAr 3                 .ifdef SINGLE
0005EAr 3  E0 01            cpx #1                          ; skip single digit, handle hard-wired or...
0005ECr 3  D0 03            bne DIGCONV_LOOP                ; only one digit left, we can handle that elsewhere
0005EEr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; not really an error, just return and let 'find' to it
0005F1r 3                 .endif
0005F1r 3               DIGCONV_LOOP:
0005F1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETDIG
0005F4r 3  90 03            bcc DIGCONT0
0005F6r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR
0005F9r 3               DIGCONT0:
0005F9r 3  48               pha                             ; else save it to add in a bit
0005FAr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; do first shift
0005FCr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
0005FEr 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE                     ; check base that was set above
000600r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000602r 3  F0 06            beq DIGDEC                      ; it's DEC, go there
000604r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000606r 3  F0 2F            beq DIGBIN                      ; it's BIN, go there
000608r 3  80 1D            bra DIGHEX                      ; Go to hex if others not true
00060Ar 3               DIGDEC:
00060Ar 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; 2nd shift; upper nybble doesn't end up right
00060Cr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00060Er 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1                       ; load results of two shifts
000610r 3  18               clc
000611r 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2                       ; add in previous total from last loop
000613r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000615r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1                     ; and same with second digit, and the carry
000617r 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1
000619r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00061Br 3               DIGDEC2:
00061Br 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; final shift
00061Dr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00061Fr 3  68               pla                             ; bring back read digit
000620r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10                         ; make sure it's not hex, mostly
000622r 3  90 18            bcc DIGCONT                     ; jump to continue
000624r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; else throw error
000627r 3               DIGHEX:
000627r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000629r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00062Br 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00062Dr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00062Fr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000631r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1                     ; shifted three more times
000633r 3  68               pla
000634r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONT
000637r 3               DIGBIN:                             ; already did the single shift
000637r 3  68               pla
000638r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
00063Ar 3  B0 3E            bcs DIGCONV_ERR                 ; fall through to the additon of new digit
00063Cr 3               DIGCONT:
00063Cr 3  18               clc                             ; add digit finally
00063Dr 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
00063Fr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000641r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; copy to intermediate result in case another dec digit
000643r 3  90 02            bcc DIGCONT2
000645r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1                     ; and inc 2nd byte if necc.
000647r 3               DIGCONT2:
000647r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
000649r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00064Br 3  D0 08            bne DIGCONT3
00064Dr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00064Fr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; save it for next round, regardless
000651r 3  C9 80            cmp #$80                        ; check to see if > $8000
000653r 3  B0 25            bcs DIGCONV_ERR
000655r 3               DIGCONT3:
000655r 3  C8               iny
000656r 3  CA               dex
000657r 3  D0 98            bne DIGCONV_LOOP
000659r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
00065Br 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00065Dr 3  D0 16            bne DIGCONT4
00065Fr 3               DECFINISH:
00065Fr 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6                       ; check for minus
000661r 3  F0 12            beq DIGCONT4
000663r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000665r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
000667r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000669r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00066Br 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
00066Dr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00066Fr 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
000671r 3  D0 02            bne DIGCONT4
000673r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
000675r 3               DIGCONT4:
000675r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push TEMP1 on to stack
000678r 3  18               clc
000679r 3  60               rts
00067Ar 3               DIGCONV_ERR:
00067Ar 3  38               sec
00067Br 3  60               rts
00067Cr 3               
00067Cr 3               ;--------------------------
00067Cr 3               GETDIG:                             ; y is index to next char in NXTTOK
00067Cr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
00067Er 3  38               sec
00067Fr 3  E9 30            sbc #ASCII_0
000681r 3  90 10            bcc GETDIG_ERR
000683r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000685r 3  90 0A            bcc GETDIG_RTN
000687r 3  E9 07            sbc #7
000689r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00068Br 3  90 06            bcc GETDIG_ERR
00068Dr 3  C9 10            cmp #16
00068Fr 3  B0 02            bcs GETDIG_ERR
000691r 3               GETDIG_RTN:                         ; pass carry clear for good digit
000691r 3  18               clc
000692r 3  60               rts
000693r 3               GETDIG_ERR:                         ; pass carry set for no digit
000693r 3  38               sec
000694r 3  60               rts
000695r 3               ;  end of new number conv
000695r 3               ;
000695r 3  27 10        H2NUM: .byte $27,$10
000697r 3  03 E8         .byte $03,$E8
000699r 3  00 64         .byte $00,$64
00069Br 3  00 0A         .byte $00,$0A
00069Dr 3               HEX2DEC:                            ; low/high in A,Y - use X, TEMP1, TEMP3, TEMP4, TEMP6
00069Dr 3  84 F5            sty TEMP3+1
00069Fr 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0006A1r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0006A3r 3               H2DDIV10:

0006A6r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1

0006ACr 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
0006AEr 3  E8               inx
0006AFr 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0006B1r 3               H2DLOOP:

0006B9r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
0006BBr 3  C5 F6            cmp TEMP4
0006BDr 3  90 11            bcc  H2DSK1
0006BFr 3               H2DSK0:
0006BFr 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
0006C1r 3  38               sec
0006C2r 3  E5 F6            sbc TEMP4
0006C4r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0006C6r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
0006C8r 3  E5 F7            sbc TEMP4+1
0006CAr 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0006CCr 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
0006CEr 3  80 E1            bra H2DLOOP
0006D0r 3               H2DSK1:
0006D0r 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6
0006D2r 3  18               clc
0006D3r 3  69 30            adc #ASCII_0
0006D5r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0006D7r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0006D9r 3  DA               phx
0006DAr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                      ; remember!  A/X both destroyed with push and pull!
0006DDr 3  FA               plx
0006DEr 3  E0 08            cpx #8
0006E0r 3  F0 03            beq H2DFIN

0006F1r 3  60               rts

0008C4r 3               ;
0008C4r 3               ;    load mainoff (TEMP1 $F0), ramstart (TEMP3 $F4), and endsoff (TEMP2 $F2)
0008C4r 3               ;     with current ROM offsets (main = start, ends = finish)
0008C4r 3               ;     and ramstart with destination corresponding to main.
0008C4r 3               ;     Set 'supprint' <> 0 (TEMP5 $FC) to suppress printing of progress bar.
0008C4r 3               ;
0008C4r 3               MEMCPY:
0008C4r 3               COPYLOOP:
0008C4r 3  B2 F0 92 F4      MOV (mainoff), (ramstart)
0008C8r 3  A5 F0 C5 F2      BNE16 mainoff, endsoff, SKIP1
0008CCr 3  D0 07 A5 F1  
0008D0r 3  C5 F3 D0 01  
0008D4r 3  60               rts
0008D5r 3               
0008D5r 3               SKIP1:
0008D5r 3  A5 FC            lda supprint
0008D7r 3  D0 05            bne SKIP01
0008D9r 3  A9 2E 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR #ASCII_PERIOD
0008DDr 3  rr           
0008DEr 3               
0008DEr 3               SKIP01:
0008DEr 3  E6 F0 D0 02      INC16_BARE mainoff
0008E2r 3  E6 F1        
0008E4r 3  E6 F4 D0 02      INC16_BARE ramstart
0008E8r 3  E6 F5        
0008EAr 3  80 D8            bra COPYLOOP

0008ECr 3               ;
0008ECr 3

000529r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000529r 2               ;
000529r 2               ; ( w a -- ) ; [a] = w    (w is word, a is an address)
000529r 2  rr rr 01 21  def_word "!", "store", 0
00052Dr 2               storew:
00052Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; get address, store in TEMP2
000530r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get data, store in TEMP1
000533r 2  A2 F2            ldx #TEMP2              ;  [a]
000535r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1              ;   w
000537r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyinto            ; copy TEMP2 stuff to addr in TEMP1 (opposite of @)..
00053Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                ;            ...(see below)
00053Dr 2               ;
00053Dr 2               ;--------------------------------------------FETCH--------------------
00053Dr 2               ; ( a -- w ) ; w = [a]
00053Dr 2  rr rr 01 40  def_word "@", "fetch", 0      ; replace addr of data on top of DS, with data pointed to
000541r 2               fetchw:
000541r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get addr from DS
000544r 2  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
000546r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
000548r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom            ; copies data from [TEMP1] => TEMP2
00054Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00054Br 2               ;            NEXT entry point for many AGSB primitives
00054Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00054Br 2               copys:                      ; copy from cell at y (zp) to TEMP1
00054Br 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00054Er 2  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000550r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000553r 2               keeps:                      ; saves bytes since have to get here anyway
000553r 2  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000555r 2               this:                       ; same as above
000555r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0             ; then push back on stack
000558r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00055Br 2               ;
00055Br 2               ;-----------------------IMMEDIATE, '[', ']', ','-----------------------
00055Br 2  rr rr 01 49  def_word "I", "Imm", 0
00055Fr 2               wimm:                        ; jmp here if proccessing a compiled
00055Fr 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP+1           ;   word that needs to run 'immediate'.
000561r 2  85 CD            sta TEMP9+1
000563r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP             ; get addr of 'last' compiled word, copy to TEMP4, add 2
000565r 2  18               clc
000566r 2  69 02            adc #2                   ; ..to find where length byte is...
000568r 2  85 CC            sta TEMP9
00056Ar 2  90 02            bcc IMMSKIP
00056Cr 2  E6 CD            inc TEMP9+1
00056Er 2               IMMSKIP:
00056Er 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000570r 2  B1 CC            lda (TEMP9),y
000572r 2  09 80            ora #$80                 ; ...set bit 7 and store
000574r 2  91 CC            sta (TEMP9),y
000576r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000579r 2               ;
000579r 2  rr rr 81 5B  def_word "[", "leftbrack", FLAG_IMM       ; switch to 'interpret'
00057Dr 2  64 E0            stz STATUS
00057Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000582r 2               ;
000582r 2  rr rr 01 5D  def_word "]", "rtbrack", 0               ; switch back to 'compile'
000586r 2  A9 01            lda #1
000588r 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
00058Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00058Dr 2               ;
00058Dr 2               ;
00058Dr 2                                                        ; NOTE: 'lit' or similar will have pushed a value
00058Dr 2                                                        ; or address onto stack; 'comma' stores it inline in word
00058Dr 2                                                        ;
00058Dr 2  rr rr 01 2C  def_word ",", "xcomma", 0                ; pull data from top of stack, store at 'here', adjust
000591r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0                          ; 'here' to point at next cell
000594r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000596r 2  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000598r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y                     ; POP DS TO TEMP1, [here] = TEMP1
00059Ar 2  C8               iny
00059Br 2  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00059Dr 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
00059Fr 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                        ; here += 2
0005A1r 2  A9 02            lda #2
0005A3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0005A6r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005A9r 2               
0005A9r 2               ;--------------------------------------------SEMIS-----------------
0005A9r 2  rr rr 81 3B  def_word ";", "semis",  FLAG_IMM
0005ADr 2  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP
0005AFr 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP                ; bring back BACKHEAP to LASTHEAP
0005B1r 2  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
0005B3r 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
0005B5r 2               
0005B5r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                  ; set status to 'interpret' (presumably from 'compile')
0005B7r 2               
0005B7r 2               finish:                         ; compiled words must end with exit
0005B7r 2  A9 rr            lda #<exit
0005B9r 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG
0005BBr 2  A9 rr            lda #>exit
0005BDr 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1
0005BFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma                  ; change NEXTHEAP to point to addr of 'exit',
0005C2r 2                                               ; and make sure is last entry in code table for word...
0005C2r 2                                               ; as all good Forth compiled words should do.
0005C2r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005C5r 2               ;
0005C5r 2               ;
0005C5r 2               ;------------------------------------------COMPILE------------------
0005C5r 2  rr rr 01 3A  def_word ":", "colon", 0
0005C9r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK                 ; room for the dictionary to grow?
0005CCr 2  B0 03            bcs COLONROOM
0005CEr 2  4C rr rr         jmp DICTFULL
0005D1r 2               COLONROOM:
0005D1r 2  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
0005D3r 2  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
0005D5r 2  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
0005D7r 2  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
0005D9r 2               
0005D9r 2  A9 01            lda #1                      ; set status to 'compile'
0005DBr 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
0005DDr 2               
0005DDr 2               COMPHEADER:
0005DDr 2               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
0005DDr 2  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
0005DFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
0005E2r 2  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
0005E5r 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
0005E7r 2                                                ; code field comes with later proc
0005E7r 2               COMPLOOP:
0005E7r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0005E9r 2  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE
0005EBr 2  F0 0B            beq COMPEND
0005EDr 2  C0 00            cpy #0                       ; if this is the length field...
0005EFr 2  D0 02            bne COMCOPY
0005F1r 2  09 40            ora #$40                     ; set bit six for 'compiled' word
0005F3r 2               COMCOPY:
0005F3r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
0005F5r 2  C8               iny
0005F6r 2  D0 EF            bne COMPLOOP
0005F8r 2               COMPEND:
0005F8r 2  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
0005F9r 2  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
0005FBr 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
0005FEr 2               
0005FEr 2               ;~~~~~~~~ all done....
0005FEr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                     ; and then see below; compiled word will continue until ';'
000601r 2               ;
000601r 2               ;---------------------ADD IN EXTRA HARDCODED LOGIC / NUMERALS / ETC--
000601r 2               .ifdef HYWORDS
000601r 2                  .include "hywords.s"

00110Dr 3               ;-------- IO: files (see os_rom IO_PLAN.md).  A failed call gives !IO ERR!, and 'ioerr' the IO layer's
00110Dr 3               ;         error code ($70 not found, $71 bad fd, $72 wrong mode, $73 would block, $75 no fds, ...).
00110Dr 3               ;         fds 0, 1, 2 are the console (key, emit); buffers must be in task RAM ($0000-$7FFF).
00110Dr 3               ;
00110Dr 3               ; ( sz mode -- fd )  open a file: sz = a q^...^ string, e.g. q^/dev/cons^; mode 1 = read, 2 = write,
00110Dr 3               ;                   3 = both, + $80 = don't wait (reads and writes give ioerr $73 instead)

001558r 3               ;
001558r 3               ;  BLOAD test code
001558r 3               ;
001558r 3               ;  ALL binary libraries loadable with 'bload' MUST be Forth words,
001558r 3               ;   (not jsr subroutines), fully relocatable.  That means NO jumps
001558r 3               ;   or branches to labels!  Calculate all jumps and branches accordingly
001558r 3               ;   and use numercal offsets.  jsr's and jmp's to core primitive labels
001558r 3               ;   (next, keeps, this, etc) or ROM locations will work fine.
001558r 3               ;
001558r 3               ;  Format below must be rigidly followed, with length of name followed
001558r 3               ;   by that many bytes for the name.  'bit #0' ($89 $00) must be
001558r 3               ;   the first instruction in any native code word loaded past
001558r 3               ;   'exit'.  Termination must be $00 $00 for each word, with "END"
001558r 3               ;   to designate the end of a 'library'.
001558r 3               ;
001558r 3               test00:
001558r 3  06           .byte 6
001559r 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest"

00155Fr 3  22                SKIPNEXT

00157Br 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
00157Er 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0

000014r 2  63 6F 6D 65  
000018r 2  20 74 6F 20  
000029r 2               
000029r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000029r 2               ; Serial driver: the file server for /dev/cons and /dev/ser (see IO_PLAN.md).  Runs in its own Resident
000029r 2               ; task (SERIAL_TASK_NUM, started by DRV_START at boot), so its state (ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS, ZP_SER_CAPTURE,
000029r 2               ; the SER_* task ZP in zero.s) and its RX and TX rings (SER_RX_BUF, SER_TX_BUF) live in that task.
000029r 2               ;   RX: the IRQ handler puts each received byte into the RX ring, and wakes the tasks waiting to read.
000029r 2               ;   TX: bytes go into the TX ring (or straight to the ACIA when it's idle); the IRQ handler sends the
000029r 2               ;       next one each time the ACIA's transmit register empties (Rockwell 65C51 TDRE interrupt).
000029r 2               ;   /dev/cons reads only for the foreground task (ZP_SER_CAPTURE: the shell to start with); others
000029r 2               ;   wait until they're brought to the foreground.  /dev/ser is the raw port.  The requests themselves
000029r 2               ;   are handled on ROM page 2 (ser_srv.s).
000029r 2               ;   READ_CHAR / WRITE_CHAR use the task's fd 0 / fd 1, or the rings directly if the task has none.
000029r 2               
000029r 2               SERIAL_DRIVER:
000029r 2  rr rr                        .word           SERIAL_INIT             ; DriverInfo::init
00002Br 2  rr rr                        .word           SERIAL_STOP             ; DriverInfo::stop
00002Dr 2  rr rr                        .word           SERIAL_NAME             ; DriverInfo::name
00002Fr 2  06 53 45 52  NamedHString SERIAL_NAME, "SERIAL"
000033r 2  49 41 4C     
000036r 2  63 6F 6E 73  CONS_NAME:      .byte           "cons", 0
00003Ar 2  00           
00003Br 2  73 65 72 00  SER_NAME:       .byte           "ser", 0
00003Fr 2               
00003Fr 2               ; Gate into the serial task: set the foreground task (the one /dev/cons reads for)
00003Fr 2               ; IN: .A = task
00003Fr 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE       SER_CALL_SET_CAPTURE, SERIAL_SET_CAPTURE, SERIAL_TASK_NUM
000043r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000047r 2  rr A9 0F 85  
000050r 2               
000050r 2               ; Serve routines (page 2, ser_srv.s)
000050r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE CONS_SERVE,     PAGE2::CONS_SERVE,      2
000054r 2  rr 02        
000056r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE SER_SERVE,      PAGE2::SER_SERVE,       2
00005Ar 2  rr 02        
00005Cr 2               
00005Cr 2               ; Driver init (runs in the serial task).  OUT: C = 0 on success, or C = 1 and .A = error
00005Cr 2               SERIAL_INIT:
00005Cr 2  08                           php                                     ; Save caller's I flag
00005Dr 2  78                           sei
00005Er 2  A9 1F                        lda             #$10 | SR_SELECT    ; 8-N-1
000060r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CTRL
000063r 2  A9 05                        lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLIE  ; No parity, no echo, tx & rx interrupts.
000065r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CMD
000068r 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
00006Ar 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
00006Cr 2  A9 01                        lda             #SHELL_TASK_NUM         ; The shell is in the foreground to start with
00006Er 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
000070r 2  A2 07                        ldx             #SER_RX_HEAD - SER_WR_WAIT
000072r 2               :
000072r 2  74 F8                        stz             SER_WR_WAIT,X           ; Empty rings, nobody waiting
000074r 2  CA                           dex
000075r 2  10 FB                        bpl             :-
000077r 2  A2 06                        ldx             #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SERIAL
000079r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
00007Br 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
00007Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IRQ_REGISTER            ; Handler runs in this (the serial) task
000080r 2  B0 22                        bcs             @done
000082r 2  A9 rr 85 rr                  LOAD_ADDR       CONS_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC   ; The files
000086r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00008Ar 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<CONS_NAME
00008Cr 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>CONS_NAME
00008Er 2  A2 0F                        ldx             #SERIAL_TASK_NUM
000090r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DEV_REGISTER
000093r 2  B0 0F                        bcs             @done
000095r 2  A9 rr 85 rr                  LOAD_ADDR       SER_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
000099r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00009Dr 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SER_NAME
00009Fr 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SER_NAME
0000A1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DEV_REGISTER
0000A4r 2               
0000A4r 2               @done:
0000A4r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             MM_RETURN               ; Restore caller's I flag, keep C
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               .assert         SER_RX_HEAD - SER_WR_WAIT = 7, error, "SERIAL_INIT clears the serial task ZP as one block"
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               SERIAL_STOP:
0000A7r 2  18                           clc
0000A8r 2  60                           rts
0000A9r 2               
0000A9r 2               ; Set the foreground task: /dev/cons reads for it (runs in the serial task; use SER_CALL_SET_CAPTURE,
0000A9r 2               ; or IO_CTL SER_CTL_FOREGROUND on a /dev/cons fd).  Wakes the tasks waiting to read, so the new
0000A9r 2               ; foreground task gets its input and the others go back to waiting.
0000A9r 2               ; IN: .A = task.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0000A9r 2               SERIAL_SET_CAPTURE:
0000A9r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
0000ABr 2  A2 FA                        ldx             #SER_RD_WAIT
0000ADr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_WAKE
0000B0r 2  18                           clc
0000B1r 2  60                           rts
0000B2r 2               
0000B2r 2               ; Input a character, if there is one: from fd 0 (stdin), without waiting.  (A task without an fd 0
0000B2r 2               ; gets nothing.)  The character is echoed.
0000B2r 2               ; On return, carry flag indicates whether a key was pressed
0000B2r 2               ; If a key was pressed, the key value will be in the A register
0000B2r 2               ;
0000B2r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0000B2r 2               READ_CHAR:
0000B2r 2               SERIAL_READ:
0000B2r 2  DA                           phx
0000B3r 2  AE A0 7D                     ldx             IO_FD_SERVER            ; fd 0 open?
0000B6r 2  E0 FF                        cpx             #IO_FD_CLOSED
0000B8r 2  F0 1A                        beq             @none
0000BAr 2  AD A2 7D                     lda             IO_FD_MODE              ; Just this once, don't wait
0000BDr 2  48                           pha
0000BEr 2  09 80                        ora             #IO_MODE_NONBLOCK
0000C0r 2  8D A2 7D                     sta             IO_FD_MODE
0000C3r 2  A2 00                        ldx             #0
0000C5r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_GETC                 ; C = 0: .A = byte
0000C8r 2  FA                           plx
0000C9r 2  8E A2 7D                     stx             IO_FD_MODE
0000CCr 2  B0 06                        bcs             @none
0000CEr 2  FA                           plx
0000CFr 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CHAR                                  ; echo
0000D2r 2  38                           sec
0000D3r 2  60                           rts
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               @none:
0000D4r 2  FA                           plx
0000D5r 2  18                           clc
0000D6r 2  60                           rts
0000D7r 2               
0000D7r 2               ; Input a character, waiting for it: from fd 0, so the task sleeps until one comes in (or until it's
0000D7r 2               ; brought to the foreground, for /dev/cons).  The character is echoed.  A task without an fd 0 (or with a
0000D7r 2               ; non-blocking one) polls READ_CHAR, yielding in between.
0000D7r 2               ; OUT: .A = the character, C = 1; or .A = error (e.g. ERR_IO_EOF), C = 0
0000D7r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0000D7r 2               GET_CHAR:
0000D7r 2  DA                           phx
0000D8r 2  AE A0 7D                     ldx             IO_FD_SERVER            ; fd 0 open?
0000DBr 2  E0 FF                        cpx             #IO_FD_CLOSED
0000DDr 2  F0 0B                        beq             @poll
0000DFr 2  A2 00                        ldx             #0
0000E1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_GETC                 ; Waits for it
0000E4r 2  90 0E                        bcc             @got
0000E6r 2  C9 73                        cmp             #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
0000E8r 2  D0 10                        bne             @error                  ; (A non-blocking fd 0: poll)
0000EAr 2               
0000EAr 2               @poll:
0000EAr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             READ_CHAR               ; (Echoes it)
0000EDr 2  B0 09                        bcs             @done
0000EFr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             YIELD
0000F2r 2  80 F6                        bra             @poll
0000F4r 2               
0000F4r 2               @got:
0000F4r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CHAR                              ; echo
0000F7r 2  38                           sec
0000F8r 2               
0000F8r 2               @done:
0000F8r 2  FA                           plx
0000F9r 2  60                           rts
0000FAr 2               
0000FAr 2               @error:
0000FAr 2  FA                           plx
0000FBr 2  18                           clc
0000FCr 2  60                           rts
0000FDr 2               
0000FDr 2               ; Write decimal value of .A to output
0000FDr 2               WRITE_DEC:
0000FDr 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
0000FFr 2  10 18                        bpl             WRITE_DEC_U
000101r 2  48                           pha
000102r 2  A9 2D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_MINUS
000106r 2  rr           
000107r 2  68                           pla
000108r 2  C9 80                        cmp             #$80                        ; special case for -128
00010Ar 2  D0 0A                        bne             :+
00010Cr 2  A9 31 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_1
000110r 2  rr           
000111r 2  A9 28 4C rr                  PRINT_BYTE_JMP  #$28
000115r 2  rr           
000116r 2               :
000116r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             NEGATE
000119r 2               
000119r 2               WRITE_DEC_U:
000119r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
00011Cr 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
00011Er 2  F0 1B                        beq             :++++
000120r 2  5A                           phy
000121r 2  A8                           tay
000122r 2  8A                           txa
000123r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
000126r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
000128r 2  D0 06                        bne             :+
00012Ar 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
00012Cr 2  D0 08                        bne             :++
00012Er 2  80 09                        bra             :+++
000130r 2               :
000130r 2  48                           pha
000131r 2  8A                           txa
000132r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
000135r 2  68                           pla
000136r 2               :
000136r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
000139r 2               :
000139r 2  98                           tya
00013Ar 2  7A                           ply
00013Br 2               :
00013Br 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             WRITE_HEX
00013Er 2               
00013Er 2               WRITE_BYTE_MIN:
00013Er 2  C9 10                        cmp             #$10
000140r 2  90 0B                        bcc             WRITE_HEX
000142r 2               
000142r 2               WRITE_BYTE:
000142r 2  48                           pha                                         ; Save A for LSD.
000143r 2  4A                           lsr
000144r 2  4A                           lsr
000145r 2  4A                           lsr
000146r 2  4A                           lsr                                         ; MSD to LSD position.
000147r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX                   ; Output hex digit.
00014Ar 2  68                           pla                                         ; Restore A.
00014Br 2               
00014Br 2               WRITE_HEX_MASK:
00014Br 2  29 0F                        and             #$0F                        ; Mask LSD for hex print.
00014Dr 2               
00014Dr 2               WRITE_HEX:
00014Dr 2  DA                           phx
00014Er 2  AA                           tax
00014Fr 2  BD rr rr                     lda             HEX_MAP,x
000152r 2  22                           SKIPNEXT
000153r 2               
000153r 2               ; Output a character (from the A register): to fd 1 (stdout), or straight to the serial port if the
000153r 2               ; task has no fd 1 (system and driver tasks).  Not with IRQs off: it may have to wait for the TX IRQ.
000153r 2               ;
000153r 2               ; Modifies: flags
000153r 2               WRITE_CHAR:
000153r 2               SERIAL_WRITE:
000153r 2  DA                           phx                                         ; Must stay 1 byte (WRITE_HEX SKIPNEXTs over it)
000154r 2  5A                           phy
000155r 2  AE A8 7D                     ldx             IO_FD_SERVER + IO_FD_SIZE   ; fd 1 open?
000158r 2  E0 FF                        cpx             #IO_FD_CLOSED
00015Ar 2  F0 09                        beq             @direct
00015Cr 2  48                           pha
00015Dr 2  A2 01                        ldx             #1
00015Fr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_PUTC
000162r 2  68                           pla
000163r 2  90 08                        bcc             @done
000165r 2               
000165r 2               @direct:
000165r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_TX_TRY
000168r 2  90 03                        bcc             @done
00016Ar 2  CB                           wai                                         ; The TX ring is full: wait for the TX IRQ
00016Br 2  80 F8                        bra             @direct
00016Dr 2               
00016Dr 2               @done:
00016Dr 2  7A                           ply
00016Er 2  FA                           plx
00016Fr 2  60                           rts
000170r 2               
000170r 2               ; Queue a byte for the serial port, from any task: into the TX ring, or straight to the ACIA when it's
000170r 2               ; idle (the ring is empty then).  IN: .A = byte.  OUT: C = 0, or C = 1 if the ring is full
000170r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X; modifies .Y
000170r 2               SER_TX_TRY:
000170r 2  08                           php                                         ; Save caller's I flag
000171r 2  78                           sei
000172r 2  DA                           phx
000173r 2  AE F0 FF                     ldx             T_REGISTER
000176r 2  A0 0F                        ldy             #SERIAL_TASK_NUM
000178r 2  8C F0 FF                     sty             T_REGISTER                  ; Quick switch to the serial task (no stack use!)
00017Br 2  A4 rr                        ldy             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
00017Dr 2  D0 07                        bne             @queue                      ; Busy: the TX IRQ sends it
00017Fr 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   ACIA_R_DATA
000182r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS          ; SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
000184r 2  80 0C                        bra             @ok
000186r 2               
000186r 2               @queue:
000186r 2  A4 FD                        ldy             SER_TX_HEAD
000188r 2  99 00 03                     sta             SER_TX_BUF,Y
00018Br 2  C8                           iny
00018Cr 2  C4 FC                        cpy             SER_TX_TAIL
00018Er 2  F0 09                        beq             @full                       ; (The byte stored isn't counted: head stays)
000190r 2  84 FD                        sty             SER_TX_HEAD
000192r 2               
000192r 2               @ok:
000192r 2  8E F0 FF                     stx             T_REGISTER                  ; Back to the calling task
000195r 2  FA                           plx
000196r 2  28                           plp
000197r 2  18                           clc
000198r 2  60                           rts
000199r 2               
000199r 2               @full:
000199r 2  8E F0 FF                     stx             T_REGISTER                  ; Back to the calling task
00019Cr 2  FA                           plx
00019Dr 2  28                           plp
00019Er 2  38                           sec
00019Fr 2  60                           rts
0001A0r 2               
0001A0r 2               ; Wake every task in a wait mask of the serial task (SER_RD_WAIT or SER_WR_WAIT), and clear the mask.
0001A0r 2               ; Runs in the serial task.  IN: .X = the mask's ZP address.  Modifies: .A, .Y
0001A0r 2               SER_WAKE:
0001A0r 2  08                           php
0001A1r 2  78                           sei
0001A2r 2  B5 00                        lda             0,X
0001A4r 2  15 01                        ora             1,X
0001A6r 2  F0 11                        beq             @done
0001A8r 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
0001AAr 2               
0001AAr 2               @loop:
0001AAr 2  56 01                        lsr             1,X
0001ACr 2  76 00                        ror             0,X
0001AEr 2  90 04                        bcc             :+
0001B0r 2  98                           tya
0001B1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_WAKE
0001B4r 2               :
0001B4r 2  C8                           iny
0001B5r 2  C0 10                        cpy             #16
0001B7r 2  D0 F1                        bne             @loop
0001B9r 2               
0001B9r 2               @done:
0001B9r 2  28                           plp
0001BAr 2  60                           rts
0001BBr 2               
0001BBr 2               ; Convenience method to write CR/LF to output stream
0001BBr 2               WRITE_CRLF:
0001BBr 2  A9 0D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #ASCII_CR, #ASCII_LF
0001BFr 2  rr A9 0A 4C  
0001C3r 2  rr rr        
0001C5r 2               
0001C5r 2               WRITE_PROMPT:
0001C5r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0001C8r 2  A9 54 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_T
0001CCr 2  rr           
0001CDr 2  AD F0 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF0
0001D1r 2  rr rr        
0001D3r 2  A9 20 20 rr                  PRINT_SPACE
0001D7r 2  rr           
0001D8r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      RAM_BANK_REG
0001DCr 2  rr           
0001DDr 2  C9 F0                        cmp             #$F0
0001DFr 2  90 10                        bcc             @not_shared
0001E1r 2  A9 28 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_LPAREN
0001E5r 2  rr           
0001E6r 2  AD F1 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF1                       ; Shared RAM sub-bank
0001EAr 2  rr rr        
0001ECr 2  A9 29 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_RPAREN
0001F0r 2  rr           
0001F1r 2               
0001F1r 2               @not_shared:
0001F1r 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_COLON
0001F5r 2  rr           
0001F6r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ROM_BANK_REG
0001FAr 2  rr           
0001FBr 2  A9 3E 4C rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #ASCII_GT
0001FFr 2  rr           
000200r 2               
000200r 2               ; .A, .Y hold the addr of HString to write
000200r 2               ; Clobbers .A, .Y; Preserves .X
000200r 2               WRITE_HSTRING:
000200r 2  DA                           phx
000201r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_HS_TEMP
000203r 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_HS_TEMP + 1
000205r 2  B2 rr                        lda             (ZP_HS_TEMP)                ; Length of HString
000207r 2  AA                           tax
000208r 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
00020Ar 2               @write_loop:
00020Ar 2  C8                           iny
00020Br 2  B1 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      {(ZP_HS_TEMP),Y}
00020Fr 2  rr           
000210r 2  CA                           dex
000211r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @write_loop
000213r 2  FA                           plx
000214r 2  60                           rts
000215r 2               
000215r 2               ; Escape sequences
000215r 2               CLEAR_SCR:
000215r 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ #ASCII_LBRACKET, #ASCII_2, #ASCII_J
000219r 2  rr A9 5B 20  
00021Dr 2  rr rr A9 32  
000229r 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ_JMP #ASCII_LBRACKET, #ASCII_0, #ASCII_SEMI, #ASCII_0, #ASCII_f
00022Dr 2  rr A9 5B 20  
000231r 2  rr rr A9 30  
000247r 2               
000247r 2               ; Serial IRQ handler (registered with IRQ_REGISTER; runs in the serial task)
000247r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if the ACIA was interrupting
000247r 2               SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER:
000247r 2  AD rr rr                     lda             ACIA_R_STATUS           ; Read once: it clears the IRQ flag, so a second
00024Ar 2  10 3C                        bpl             @not_mine 	            ;   read could lose a TDRE that came in between
00024Cr 2  48                           pha
00024Dr 2  29 10                        and             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_TDRE
00024Fr 2  F0 1C                        beq             @check_recv             ; Transmit register still full
000251r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000253r 2  F0 18                        beq             @check_recv             ; Idle: nothing to send
000255r 2  A4 FC                        ldy             SER_TX_TAIL             ; The next byte from the TX ring
000257r 2  C4 FD                        cpy             SER_TX_HEAD
000259r 2  D0 04                        bne             :+
00025Br 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS      ; SER_SEND_STATUS_READY: the ring is empty
00025Dr 2  80 0E                        bra             @check_recv
00025Fr 2               :
00025Fr 2  B9 00 03                     lda             SER_TX_BUF,Y
000262r 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   ACIA_R_DATA
000265r 2  C8                           iny
000266r 2  84 FC                        sty             SER_TX_TAIL
000268r 2  A2 F8                        ldx             #SER_WR_WAIT            ; There's room: wake the waiting writers
00026Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_WAKE
00026Dr 2               
00026Dr 2               @check_recv:
00026Dr 2  68                           pla
00026Er 2  29 08                        and             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_RDRF   ; is read register full?
000270r 2  F0 14                        beq             @int_done
000272r 2  AD rr rr                     IO_PORT_READ    ACIA_R_DATA
000275r 2  A4 FF                        ldy             SER_RX_HEAD             ; Into the RX ring
000277r 2  99 00 02                     sta             SER_RX_BUF,Y
00027Ar 2  C8                           iny
00027Br 2  C4 FE                        cpy             SER_RX_TAIL
00027Dr 2  F0 07                        beq             @int_done               ; The ring is full: the byte is dropped
00027Fr 2  84 FF                        sty             SER_RX_HEAD
000281r 2  A2 FA                        ldx             #SER_RD_WAIT            ; Wake the waiting readers
000283r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_WAKE
000286r 2               
000286r 2               @int_done:
000286r 2  38                           sec
000287r 2  60                           rts
000288r 2               
000288r 2               @not_mine:
000288r 2  18                           clc
000289r 2  60                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               ;; *****************************************************************
00028Ar 2               .if 0
00028Ar 2               ; I2C
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_SCL = $01
00028Ar 2               I2C_SDA = $02
00028Ar 2               I2C_CTRL_PORT = VIA_R_PORTA
00028Ar 2               I2C_DATA_PORT = VIA_R_DDRA
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro I2C_ON       val
00028Ar 2                           tay
00028Ar 2                           lda     #val
00028Ar 2                           ora     I2C_DATA_PORT
00028Ar 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
00028Ar 2                           tya
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro I2C_OFF      val
00028Ar 2                           tay
00028Ar 2                           lda     #~val
00028Ar 2                           and     I2C_DATA_PORT
00028Ar 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
00028Ar 2                           tya
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro SDA_LOW
00028Ar 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SDA
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro SCL_LOW
00028Ar 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SCL
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro SDA_HIGH
00028Ar 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro SCL_HIGH
00028Ar 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               .macro SCL_PULSE
00028Ar 2                           inc     I2C_DATA_PORT
00028Ar 2                           dec     I2C_DATA_PORT
00028Ar 2               .endmacro
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               ; A: Byte to send
00028Ar 2               ; Return (in A): 1 = SUCCESS, 0 = FAILURE
00028Ar 2               I2C_SEND:
00028Ar 2                           ldx     #$00
00028Ar 2                           stx     I2C_CTRL_PORT
00028Ar 2                           ldx     #$09
00028Ar 2               @loop:
00028Ar 2                           dex
00028Ar 2                           beq     @ack
00028Ar 2                           rol
00028Ar 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
00028Ar 2                           bra    @loop
00028Ar 2               @ack:
00028Ar 2                           jsr     I2C_RECV_BIT    ; ack in A, 0 = success
00028Ar 2                           eor     #$01            ; return 1 on success, 0 on fail
00028Ar 2               @end:
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_RECV:   lda     #$00
00028Ar 2                           sta     I2C_CTRL_PORT
00028Ar 2                           pha
00028Ar 2                           ldx     #$09
00028Ar 2               @loop:      dex
00028Ar 2                           beq     @end
00028Ar 2                           jsr     rec_bit
00028Ar 2                           ror
00028Ar 2                           pla
00028Ar 2                           rol
00028Ar 2                           pha
00028Ar 2                           jmp     @loop
00028Ar 2               @end:
00028Ar 2                           pla
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               ; A: Bit to send
00028Ar 2               I2C_SEND_BIT:
00028Ar 2                           bcc     @send_one
00028Ar 2                           SDA_LOW
00028Ar 2                           bra    @clock_out
00028Ar 2               @send_one:
00028Ar 2                           SDA_HIGH
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               @clock_out:
00028Ar 2                           SCL_PULSE
00028Ar 2                           SDA_LOW
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_RECV_BIT:
00028Ar 2                           SDA_HIGH
00028Ar 2                           SCL_HIGH
00028Ar 2                           lda     I2C_CTRL_PORT
00028Ar 2                           and     #I2C_SDA
00028Ar 2                           bne     @is_one
00028Ar 2                           lda     #$00
00028Ar 2                           jmp     @end
00028Ar 2               @is_one:
00028Ar 2                           lda     #$01
00028Ar 2               @end:
00028Ar 2                           SCL_LOW
00028Ar 2                           SDA_LOW
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_START:
00028Ar 2                           SDA_LOW
00028Ar 2                           SCL_LOW
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_STOP:
00028Ar 2                           SCL_HIGH
00028Ar 2                           SDA_HIGH
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_ACK:
00028Ar 2                           pha
00028Ar 2                           lda     #$00
00028Ar 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
00028Ar 2                           pla
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               I2C_NACK:
00028Ar 2                           pha
00028Ar 2                           lda     #$01
00028Ar 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
00028Ar 2                           pla
00028Ar 2                           rts
00028Ar 2               .endif
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               VIA_T2_INT_BIT = $20
00028Ar 2               VIA_T1_INT_BIT = $40
00028Ar 2               VIA_INT_ENABLE = $80
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               ; Must be called from the system task (the VIA handler runs there)
00028Ar 2               VIA_INIT:
00028Ar 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA <> 1 .AND ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
00028Ar 2                           pha
00028Ar 2                           lda     #0
00028Ar 2                           sta     VIA_R_AUX_CTRL
00028Ar 2                           pla
00028Ar 2               .endif
00028Ar 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
00028Dr 2  A2 07                    ldx     #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_VIA
00028Fr 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<VIA_IRQ_HANDLER
000291r 2  A0 rr                    ldy     #>VIA_IRQ_HANDLER
000293r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_REGISTER
000296r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
000299r 2  60                       rts
00029Ar 2               
00029Ar 2               VIA_ENABLE_T1_INT:
00029Ar 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
00029Cr 2  09 80                    ora     #VIA_INT_ENABLE
00029Er 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002A1r 2  60                       rts
0002A2r 2               
0002A2r 2               VIA_ENABLE_T2_INT:
0002A2r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
0002A4r 2  09 80                    ora     #VIA_INT_ENABLE
0002A6r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002A9r 2  60                       rts
0002AAr 2               
0002AAr 2               VIA_DISABLE_T1_INT:
0002AAr 2  48                       pha
0002ABr 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
0002ADr 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002B0r 2  68                       pla
0002B1r 2  60                       rts
0002B2r 2               
0002B2r 2               VIA_DISABLE_T2_INT:
0002B2r 2  48                       pha
0002B3r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
0002B5r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002B8r 2  68                       pla
0002B9r 2  60                       rts
0002BAr 2               
0002BAr 2               ; .A.Y = Timer value
0002BAr 2               VIA_START_T2:
0002BAr 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_T2C_L
0002BDr 2  8C rr rr                 sty     VIA_R_T2C_H
0002C0r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     VIA_ENABLE_T2_INT
0002C3r 2               
0002C3r 2               VIA_STOP_T2:
0002C3r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     VIA_DISABLE_T2_INT
0002C6r 2               
0002C6r 2               ; .A = Sub-component interrupt flag to test
0002C6r 2               VIA_IS_INT:
0002C6r 2  18                       clc
0002C7r 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
0002CAr 2  F0 01                    beq     :+
0002CCr 2  38                       sec
0002CDr 2               :
0002CDr 2  60                       rts
0002CEr 2               
0002CEr 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if T2 timer set the IRQ, 0 if not
0002CEr 2               VIA_IS_T1_INT:
0002CEr 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
0002D0r 2  80 F4                    bra     VIA_IS_INT
0002D2r 2               
0002D2r 2               VIA_IS_T2_INT:
0002D2r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
0002D4r 2  80 F0                    bra     VIA_IS_INT
0002D6r 2               
0002D6r 2               VIA_CLEAR_T1_INT:       ; READ LOB OF T1 Counter
0002D6r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L
0002D9r 2  60                       rts
0002DAr 2               
0002DAr 2               VIA_CLEAR_T2_INT:       ; READ LOB OF T2 Counter
0002DAr 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T2C_L
0002DDr 2  60                       rts
0002DEr 2               
0002DEr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0002DEr 2               
0002DEr 2               ; VIA IRQ handler (registered by VIA_INIT; runs in the system task).
0002DEr 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if T1 was interrupting
0002DEr 2               VIA_IRQ_HANDLER:
0002DEr 2               ; check which sub-device is triggering the IRQ
0002DEr 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
0002E0r 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
0002E3r 2  F0 09                    beq     :+
0002E5r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002E8r 2  A9 A5                    lda     #SCHED_RESCHED_A        ; T1 is the scheduler's tick: ask the dispatcher for a task switch
0002EAr 2  A0 5A                    ldy     #SCHED_RESCHED_Y
0002ECr 2  38                       sec
0002EDr 2  60                       rts
0002EEr 2               
0002EEr 2               :
0002EEr 2  18                       clc
0002EFr 2  60                       rts

0000FAr 2               .macro VECTORS
0000FAr 2                               .word   NMI_ENTRY       ; NMI vector (COMMON block, same address on every page)
0000FAr 2                               .word   RESET_ENTRY     ; RESET vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   IRQ_STUB_F      ; IRQ vector (COMMON block S/W IRQ stub; unused, see below)
0000FAr 2                               ;       will actually be pulled from the Vector RAM, depending on lowest priority IRQ currently triggered,
0000FAr 2                               ;       or vector for IRQ# set in V[0..3] if none are triggered.  Can trigger S/W IRQs by setting V and then
0000FAr 2                               ;       calling BRK.  S/W IRQ# is $F, so setting V[0..3] to $F and v[4..7] to a different number, you can
0000FAr 2                               ;       have up to 16 unique S/W IRQs.  You can invoke a hardware device IRQ handler in the same way
0000FAr 2               .endmacro
0000FAr 2

000354r 2               ; .A, .Y hold ADDR of LOB to increment, .X holds the length of the value in bytes
000354r 2               ; Incremets an x-byte value at addr, addr + 1 and sets Z, N properly based on HOB
000354r 2               .macro _M_ADD_BYTE_X           byteVal
000354r 2                           sta             ZP_MATH_TEMP
000354r 2                           sty             ZP_MATH_TEMP + 1
000354r 2               .ifblank    byteVal
000354r 2                           pha
000354r 2               .else

000354r 2               .endif

000354r 2                           dex
000354r 2                           phx
000354r 2                           bra             ADD_X_LOOP
000354r 2               .endmacro

000354r 2  71 rr                    adc             (ZP_MATH_TEMP),Y
000356r 2  91 rr                    sta             (ZP_MATH_TEMP),Y
000358r 2  CA                       dex
000359r 2  30 05                    bmi             :+
00035Br 2  A2 00                    ldx             #0
00035Dr 2  C8                       iny
00035Er 2  B0 F4                    bcs             ADD_X_LOOP
000360r 2               :
000360r 2  7A                       ply

000363r 2  90 02                    bcc             :+
000365r 2  09 01                    ora             #1                ; reset Z since LOB is not zero, but preserve other flags
000367r 2               :
000367r 2  60                       rts

0003AEr 2               .feature c_comments

0003AEr 1               .include "tasks.s"
0003AEr 2               .debuginfo
0003AEr 2               .segment "TASKS"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               TASK_0_VECTOR           = $E000
000000r 2               
000000r 2               TASK_BUSY_FLAG          = 1
000000r 2               TASK_PAUSED_FLAG        = 2
000000r 2               TASK_RESIDENT_FLAG      = 8
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; TASK STATUS REGISTER BITS
000000r 2               ;   0: 0 = Available, 1 = In Use
000000r 2               ;   1: 0 = Active, 1 = Paused
000000r 2               ;   2: 0 = Not Waiting, 1 = Awaiting I/O
000000r 2               ;   3: 0 = , 1 = Resident (driver task: runs only from IRQs / TASK_CALLs, never scheduled)
000000r 2               ;   4: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   5: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   6: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   7: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SELECT_TASK      task
000000r 2                           lda     T_REGISTER
000000r 2                           and     #$F0
000000r 2                           ora     #(task & $0F)
000000r 2                           sta     T_REGISTER
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SELECT_SHARED_BANK bank
000000r 2                           lda     T_REGISTER
000000r 2                           and     #$0F
000000r 2                           ora     #(bank << 4)
000000r 2                           sta     T_REGISTER
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Initialize the tasks, their stacks, etc.
000000r 2               TASKS_INIT:
000000r 2  78                       sei                                     ; Turn off interrupts
000001r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
000004r 2  D0 2A                    bne     @cleanup                        ; Only support tasks init when on task 0
000006r 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
000008r 2               
000008r 2               @loop:
000008r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to task X
00000Br 2  64 rr                    stz     RAM_BANK_REG
00000Dr 2  64 rr                    stz     ROM_BANK_REG
00000Fr 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_STATUS_REG
000011r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_D_PAGE                       ; Disassembler reads the BIOS page by default
000013r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_NO_PREEMPT                   ; Scheduler
000015r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
000017r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_TC_GUEST
000019r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
00001Br 2  A9 FF                    lda     #$FF
00001Dr 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_PARENT                     ; No parent
00001Fr 2  85 rr                    sta     STACK_SAVE_REG
000021r 2  A0 60                    ldy     #IO_MAX_FDS * IO_FD_SIZE        ; All fds closed
000023r 2               
000023r 2               @fds:
000023r 2  88                       dey
000024r 2  99 A0 7D                 sta     IO_FD_TABLE,Y
000027r 2  D0 FA                    bne     @fds
000029r 2  CA                       dex
00002Ar 2  10 DC                    bpl     @loop                           ; Loop back as long as X >= 0
00002Cr 2  A9 01                    lda     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
00002Er 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Mark task zero as "busy"
000030r 2               
000030r 2                           ; setup interrupt handler and interrupt timer
000030r 2               
000030r 2                           ; Will fall through when X = $FF, leaving us in Task 0, as required
000030r 2               
000030r 2               @cleanup:
000030r 2  58                       cli                                     ; Turn interrupts back on
000031r 2  60                       rts
000032r 2               
000032r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000032r 2               ; Scheduler (see IO_PLAN.md, Phase 1)
000032r 2               ;
000032r 2               ;   Every task that isn't running keeps the same frame on its own stack: the one IRQ_DISPATCH builds
000032r 2               ;   (interrupt frame, A, X, W, Y, the TASK_CALL scratch bytes), plus U.  Top of stack first:
000032r 2               ;       U, ZP_TC_TASK, ZP_TC_VEC + 1, ZP_TC_VEC, Y, W, X, A, P, PCL, PCH
000032r 2               ;   and STACK_SAVE_REG is the SP below it.  So a task switch is always the same: save SP, pick a task,
000032r 2               ;   load its SP, unwind its frame (SCHED_RESUME), whether the task stopped for the timer tick (IRQ),
000032r 2               ;   YIELD, or to wait for IO.  W and U are in the frame because they're global pseudo-registers.
000032r 2               ;
000032r 2               ;   Runnable: busy, and not paused, waiting or resident.  Round-robin over tasks 1-15; task 0 is the
000032r 2               ;   idle task and only runs when nothing else can.
000032r 2               
000032r 2               TASK_WAITING_FLAG       = 4                         ; Bit 2: awaiting IO (TASK_WAIT / IO_WAKE)
000032r 2               TASK_RUN_MASK           = TASK_BUSY_FLAG | TASK_PAUSED_FLAG | TASK_WAITING_FLAG | TASK_RESIDENT_FLAG
000032r 2               TASK_FRAME_SP           = $F4                       ; STACK_SAVE_REG of a new task (11-byte frame at $01F5)
000032r 2               SCHED_RESCHED_A         = $A5                       ; An IRQ handler returns C = 1, .A and .Y = these
000032r 2               SCHED_RESCHED_Y         = $5A                       ;   to ask for a task switch (the timer tick)
000032r 2               
000032r 2               ; Switch tasks.  IRQs off, and the current task's full frame (including U) on its stack.
000032r 2               SCHED_SWITCH:
000032r 2  BA                       tsx
000033r 2  86 rr                    stx     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; The current task's SP
000035r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     SCHED_PICK                      ; .A = next task (maybe the same one)
000038r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; Its ZP and stack
00003Br 2  A6 rr                    ldx     STACK_SAVE_REG
00003Dr 2  9A                       txs
00003Er 2               
00003Er 2               ; Unwind a task's frame and continue it
00003Er 2               SCHED_RESUME:
00003Er 2  68                       pla
00003Fr 2  8D F1 FF                 sta     U_REGISTER
000042r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     IRQ_RESTORE                     ; The rest is the IRQ frame (irq.s)
000045r 2               
000045r 2               ; Pick the next task to run: the next runnable task after the current one (1-15, round-robin); else
000045r 2               ; the current one if it's runnable; else task 0 (idle).  No stack use while looking at other tasks.
000045r 2               ; OUT: .A = task
000045r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000045r 2               SCHED_PICK:
000045r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
000048r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
00004Ar 2  A8                       tay                                     ; .Y = current task
00004Br 2  AA                       tax                                     ; .X = candidate
00004Cr 2  A9 0F                    lda     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00004Er 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_SCHED_CNT
000050r 2               
000050r 2               @next:
000050r 2  E8                       inx
000051r 2  8A                       txa
000052r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
000054r 2  AA                       tax
000055r 2  F0 0E                    beq     @skip                           ; Task 0 isn't in the rotation
000057r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick look at the candidate (no stack use!)
00005Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
00005Cr 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
00005Fr 2  29 0F                    and     #TASK_RUN_MASK
000061r 2  C9 01                    cmp     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
000063r 2  F0 11                    beq     @found
000065r 2               
000065r 2               @skip:
000065r 2  C6 rr                    dec     ZP_SCHED_CNT
000067r 2  D0 E7                    bne     @next
000069r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Nobody else: keep going if we can
00006Br 2  29 0F                    and     #TASK_RUN_MASK
00006Dr 2  C9 01                    cmp     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
00006Fr 2  F0 03                    beq     @stay
000071r 2  A9 00                    lda     #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM                ; Idle
000073r 2  60                       rts
000074r 2               
000074r 2               @stay:
000074r 2  98                       tya
000075r 2  60                       rts
000076r 2               
000076r 2               @found:
000076r 2  8A                       txa
000077r 2  60                       rts
000078r 2               
000078r 2               ; Can the interrupted (current) task be preempted?  Not if it's running a TASK_CALL routine for another
000078r 2               ; task (a guest), isn't runnable (e.g. a resident driver task), or holds NO_PREEMPT (then the switch is
000078r 2               ; noted, for PREEMPT).
000078r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 switch, C = 0 don't
000078r 2               SCHED_CAN_PREEMPT:
000078r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_GUEST
00007Ar 2  D0 10                    bne     @no
00007Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
00007Er 2  29 0F                    and     #TASK_RUN_MASK
000080r 2  C9 01                    cmp     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
000082r 2  D0 08                    bne     @no
000084r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
000086r 2  F0 06                    beq     @yes
000088r 2  A9 01                    lda     #1
00008Ar 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE                  ; Switch at PREEMPT
00008Cr 2               
00008Cr 2               @no:
00008Cr 2  18                       clc
00008Dr 2  60                       rts
00008Er 2               
00008Er 2               @yes:
00008Er 2  38                       sec
00008Fr 2  60                       rts
000090r 2               
000090r 2               ; Give up the CPU: switch to the next runnable task.  Returns when this task is picked again (at once
000090r 2               ; if nothing else can run).
000090r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y and the flags
000090r 2               YIELD:
000090r 2  08                       php
000091r 2  78                       sei
000092r 2  48                       pha                                     ; Build the frame an IRQ would: P is there already,
000093r 2  DA                       phx                                     ;   under it the return address (made RTI-style below)
000094r 2  BA                       tsx
000095r 2  FE 04 01                 inc     $0104,X                         ; Return address (S+4 = PCL, S+5 = PCH) + 1
000098r 2  D0 03                    bne     :+
00009Ar 2  FE 05 01                 inc     $0105,X
00009Dr 2               :
00009Dr 2  AD F3 FF                 lda     W_REGISTER
0000A0r 2  48                       pha
0000A1r 2  5A                       phy
0000A2r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_VEC
0000A4r 2  48                       pha
0000A5r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0000A7r 2  48                       pha
0000A8r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_TASK
0000AAr 2  48                       pha
0000ABr 2  AD F1 FF                 lda     U_REGISTER
0000AEr 2  48                       pha
0000AFr 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
0000B1r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     SCHED_SWITCH
0000B4r 2               
0000B4r 2               ; Hold the CPU: no task switches until the matching PREEMPT (nestable).  IRQs and their handlers keep
0000B4r 2               ; running.  For short sections, sei / cli also works (but holds off all IRQs).
0000B4r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000B4r 2               NO_PREEMPT:
0000B4r 2  E6 rr                    inc     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000B6r 2  60                       rts
0000B7r 2               
0000B7r 2               ; Undo NO_PREEMPT; if a task switch came due meanwhile (and this was the outermost), switch now.
0000B7r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000B7r 2               PREEMPT:
0000B7r 2  08                       php
0000B8r 2  78                       sei
0000B9r 2  48                       pha
0000BAr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000BCr 2  F0 0D                    beq     @done                           ; Not holding it
0000BEr 2  C6 rr                    dec     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000C0r 2  D0 09                    bne     @done                           ; Still nested
0000C2r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
0000C4r 2  F0 05                    beq     @done
0000C6r 2  68                       pla
0000C7r 2  28                       plp
0000C8r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     YIELD                           ; (YIELD clears ZP_PREEMPT_DUE)
0000CBr 2               
0000CBr 2               @done:
0000CBr 2  68                       pla
0000CCr 2  28                       plp
0000CDr 2  60                       rts
0000CEr 2               
0000CEr 2               ; Wait for IO: the task isn't run again until IO_WAKE.
0000CEr 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000CEr 2               TASK_WAIT:
0000CEr 2  08                       php
0000CFr 2  78                       sei
