000291r 2  B0 19                    bcs         @pages
000293r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_CHUNK_ALLOC                      ; ZP_M_CP + 1 = page, ZP_M_COFS = offset
000296r 2  B0 5D                    bcs         @done
000298r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #Handle::addr_l
00029Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_COFS
00029Cr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
00029Er 2  C8                       iny
00029Fr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_CP + 1
0002A1r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002A3r 2  C8                       iny
0002A4r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0002A6r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002A8r 2  A9 90                    lda         #AI_IN_USE                          ; No tier bit: a chunk
0002AAr 2  80 42                    bra         @set_status
0002ACr 2               
0002ACr 2               ; 65+ bytes: whole task RAM pages
0002ACr 2               @pages:
0002ACr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Pages = size high + (size low <> 0)
0002AEr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_M_SZ1
0002B0r 2  F0 01                    beq         :+
0002B2r 2  1A                       inc                                             ; ($FFxx wraps to 0: rejected as a bad size)
0002B3r 2               :
0002B3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_PAGE_ALLOC                       ; .A = first page
0002B6r 2  B0 3D                    bcs         @done
0002B8r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #Handle::addr_h
0002BAr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002BCr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0002BEr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #Handle::addr_l
0002C0r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002C2r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #Handle::bank
0002C4r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002C6r 2  A9 94                    lda         #AI_IN_USE | AI_BLOCK
0002C8r 2  80 24                    bra         @set_status
0002CAr 2               
0002CAr 2               ; AI_PAGED: whole 8K RAM banks
0002CAr 2               @banks:
0002CAr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SZ1                            ; Banks = ((size - 1) >> 13) + 1
0002CCr 2  C9 01                    cmp         #1
0002CEr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0002D0r 2  E9 00                    sbc         #0                                  ; High byte of size - 1
0002D2r 2  4A                       lsr
0002D3r 2  4A                       lsr
0002D4r 2  4A                       lsr
0002D5r 2  4A                       lsr
0002D6r 2  4A                       lsr
0002D7r 2  1A                       inc
0002D8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_BANK_ALLOC                       ; .A = first bank
0002DBr 2  B0 18                    bcs         @done
0002DDr 2  A0 02                    ldy         #Handle::bank
0002DFr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002E1r 2  A9 00                    lda         #<PAGED_RAM_BASE
0002E3r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #Handle::addr_l
0002E5r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002E7r 2  A9 80                    lda         #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
0002E9r 2  C8                       iny
0002EAr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002ECr 2  A9 91                    lda         #AI_IN_USE | AI_PAGED
0002EEr 2               
0002EEr 2               @set_status:
0002EEr 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status                     ; Status last: it makes the entry live
0002F0r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002F2r 2               
0002F2r 2               @ok:
0002F2r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
0002F4r 2  18                       clc
0002F5r 2               
0002F5r 2               @done:
0002F5r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0002F7r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0002FAr 2               
0002FAr 2               @bad_arg:
0002FAr 2  A9 43                    lda         #ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG
0002FCr 2  38                       sec
0002FDr 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
0002FFr 2               
0002FFr 2               ; Free an allocation and its handle.
0002FFr 2               ; IN: .A = handle
0002FFr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
0002FFr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID or ERR_MEM_LOCKED, C = 1
0002FFr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
0002FFr 2               MM_FREE:
0002FFr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000300r 2  78                       sei
000301r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000303r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_HANDLE_PTR                       ; .A = status
000306r 2  B0 3C                    bcs         @done
000308r 2  89 40                    bit         #AI_LOCKED
00030Ar 2  D0 3D                    bne         @locked
00030Cr 2  89 01                    bit         #AI_PAGED
00030Er 2  F0 0B                    beq         :+
000310r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #Handle::bank
000312r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000314r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_BANK_FREE
000317r 2  B0 2B                    bcs         @done
000319r 2  80 22                    bra         @free_entry
00031Br 2               :
00031Br 2  89 08                    bit         #AI_SMALL
00031Dr 2  D0 1E                    bne         @free_entry                         ; AI_SMALL: nothing else to free
00031Fr 2  89 04                    bit         #AI_BLOCK
000321r 2  F0 0B                    beq         @chunk
000323r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #Handle::addr_h
000325r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000327r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_PAGE_FREE
00032Ar 2  B0 18                    bcs         @done
00032Cr 2  80 0F                    bra         @free_entry
00032Er 2               
00032Er 2               @chunk:
00032Er 2  A0 01                    ldy         #Handle::addr_h
000330r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000332r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CP + 1                         ; Chunk page
000334r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #Handle::addr_l
000336r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y                         ; Chunk offset
000338r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_CHUNK_FREE
00033Br 2  B0 07                    bcs         @done
00033Dr 2               
00033Dr 2               @free_entry:
00033Dr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
00033Fr 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status
000341r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000343r 2  18                       clc
000344r 2               
000344r 2               @done:
000344r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000346r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000349r 2               
000349r 2               @locked:
000349r 2  A9 45                    lda         #ERR_MEM_LOCKED
00034Br 2  38                       sec
00034Cr 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
00034Er 2               
00034Er 2               ; Read a byte of an allocation.
00034Er 2               ; IN: .A = handle, .Y = offset (0-255; within the allocation's size for AI_SMALL)
00034Er 2               ; OUT (success): .A = byte, C = 0
00034Er 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID or ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG, C = 1
00034Er 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00034Er 2               MM_READ:
00034Er 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00034Fr 2  78                       sei
000350r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000352r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; Offset
000354r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_HANDLE_PTR                       ; .A = status
000357r 2  B0 0E                    bcs         @done
000359r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ACCESS_SETUP                     ; ZP_M_SP1 = data, bank selected
00035Cr 2  B0 09                    bcs         @done
00035Er 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_TEMP
000360r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1),Y
000362r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_SV
000364r 2  84 rr                    sty         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; Restore the RAM bank
000366r 2  18                       clc
000367r 2               
000367r 2               @done:
000367r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000369r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00036Cr 2               
00036Cr 2               ; Write a byte of an allocation.
00036Cr 2               ; IN: .A = handle, .Y = offset (0-255; within the allocation's size for AI_SMALL), .X = byte
00036Cr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00036Cr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID or ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG, C = 1
00036Cr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00036Cr 2               MM_WRITE:
00036Cr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00036Dr 2  78                       sei
00036Er 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000370r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; Offset
000372r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_HANDLE_PTR                       ; .A = status
000375r 2  B0 0F                    bcs         @done
000377r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ACCESS_SETUP                     ; ZP_M_SP1 = data, bank selected
00037Ar 2  B0 0A                    bcs         @done
00037Cr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_TEMP
00037Er 2  8A                       txa
00037Fr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y
000381r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_SV
000383r 2  84 rr                    sty         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; Restore the RAM bank
000385r 2  18                       clc
000386r 2               
000386r 2               @done:
000386r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000388r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00038Br 2               
00038Br 2               ; Get a raw pointer to an allocation, for speed.  For AI_PAGED allocations this also selects the
00038Br 2               ; allocation's first RAM bank; the old bank is returned for MM_UNLOCK.  A locked allocation can't be
00038Br 2               ; freed.  The pointer stays valid until MM_UNLOCK (allocations don't move).
00038Br 2               ; IN: .A = handle
00038Br 2               ; OUT (success): .A.Y = pointer (.A = low byte), .X = previous RAM bank (pass it to MM_UNLOCK), C = 0
00038Br 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1
00038Br 2               MM_LOCK:
00038Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00038Cr 2  78                       sei
00038Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_HANDLE_PTR                       ; .A = status
000390r 2  B0 12                    bcs         @done
000392r 2  09 40                    ora         #AI_LOCKED
000394r 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status
000396r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000398r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; Offset 0 (always valid)
00039Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ACCESS_SETUP                     ; ZP_M_SP1 = data, bank selected
00039Dr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_M_SV                             ; Previous RAM bank
00039Fr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1
0003A1r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
0003A3r 2  18                       clc
0003A4r 2               
0003A4r 2               @done:
0003A4r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0003A7r 2               
0003A7r 2               ; Release a pointer from MM_LOCK and restore the RAM bank.
0003A7r 2               ; IN: .A = handle, .X = RAM bank to restore (from MM_LOCK)
0003A7r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
0003A7r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1 (the RAM bank is restored anyway)
0003A7r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
0003A7r 2               MM_UNLOCK:
0003A7r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0003A8r 2  78                       sei
0003A9r 2  5A                       phy
0003AAr 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
0003ACr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_HANDLE_PTR                       ; .A = status
0003AFr 2  B0 07                    bcs         @done
0003B1r 2  29 BF                    and         #<~AI_LOCKED
0003B3r 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status
0003B5r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0003B7r 2  18                       clc
0003B8r 2               
0003B8r 2               @done:
0003B8r 2  7A                       ply
0003B9r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0003BCr 2               
0003BCr 2               ; Allocate a chunk from the current task's chunk pages, starting a new chunk page if the size class has
0003BCr 2               ; no free chunk.
0003BCr 2               ; IN: .A = bytes (MMU_CHUNK_MIN - MMU_CHUNK_MAX)
0003BCr 2               ; OUT (success): ZP_M_CP + 1 = chunk page, ZP_M_COFS = chunk offset, C = 0
0003BCr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, C = 1
0003BCr 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0003BCr 2               MM_CHUNK_ALLOC:
0003BCr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_CHUNK_CLASS                      ; ZP_M_CLS, ZP_M_CSZ
0003BFr 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_CP
0003C1r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_M_CLS
0003C3r 2  BD 2F 7E                 lda         MMU_CHUNK_HEADS,X                   ; First chunk page of the class
0003C6r 2               
0003C6r 2               @find_page:
0003C6r 2  F0 0E                    beq         @new_page                           ; No page with a free chunk
0003C8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CP + 1
0003CAr 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_count
0003CCr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y
0003CEr 2  D0 3B                    bne         @take
0003D0r 2  A0 03                    ldy         #ChunkPage::next_page
0003D2r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y
0003D4r 2  80 F0                    bra         @find_page
0003D6r 2               
0003D6r 2               @new_page:
0003D6r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0003D8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_PAGE_ALLOC                       ; .A = page
0003DBr 2  B0 43                    bcs         @done
0003DDr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CP + 1
0003DFr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_CSZ
0003E1r 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP)                           ; ChunkPage::size
0003E3r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_head
0003E5r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y                         ; The first chunk after the header
0003E7r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Free chunk count
0003E9r 2               
0003E9r 2               @link:                                                      ; .A = this chunk's offset
0003E9r 2  A8                       tay
0003EAr 2  18                       clc
0003EBr 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_M_CSZ                            ; Next chunk (C = 1 past the end of the page)
0003EDr 2  90 02                    bcc         :+
0003EFr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0                                  ; Last chunk: end of the list
0003F1r 2               :
0003F1r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
0003F3r 2  E8                       inx
0003F4r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0
0003F6r 2  D0 F1                    bne         @link
0003F8r 2  8A                       txa
0003F9r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_count
0003FBr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
0003FDr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_M_CLS                            ; Put the page at the head of the class list
0003FFr 2  BD 2F 7E                 lda         MMU_CHUNK_HEADS,X
000402r 2  A0 03                    ldy         #ChunkPage::next_page
000404r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
000406r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_CP + 1
000408r 2  9D 2F 7E                 sta         MMU_CHUNK_HEADS,X
00040Br 2               
00040Br 2               @take:
00040Br 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_head
00040Dr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y
00040Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_COFS                           ; The chunk
000411r 2  A8                       tay
000412r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y                         ; Its link: the next free chunk
000414r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_head
000416r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
000418r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_count
00041Ar 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y
00041Cr 2  3A                       dec
00041Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
00041Fr 2  18                       clc
000420r 2               
000420r 2               @done:
000420r 2  60                       rts
000421r 2               
000421r 2               ; Free a chunk.  A chunk page that becomes completely free goes back to the page allocator.
000421r 2               ; IN: ZP_M_CP + 1 = chunk page, .A = chunk offset
000421r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000421r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
000421r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000421r 2               MM_CHUNK_FREE:
000421r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_CP
000423r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_COFS
000425r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_head               ; Link the chunk in at the head of the free list
000427r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y
000429r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_COFS
00042Br 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
00042Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_COFS
00042Fr 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_head
000431r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
000433r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ChunkPage::free_count
000435r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y
000437r 2  1A                       inc
000438r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CP),Y
00043Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_COFS                           ; Free chunks now
00043Cr 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP)                           ; ChunkPage::size
00043Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CSZ
000440r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Chunks in a page (after the header)
000442r 2               
000442r 2               @count:
000442r 2  E8                       inx
000443r 2  18                       clc
000444r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_M_CSZ
000446r 2  90 FA                    bcc         @count
000448r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_M_COFS
00044Ar 2  F0 02                    beq         @release                            ; Every chunk is free
00044Cr 2  18                       clc
00044Dr 2  60                       rts
00044Er 2               
00044Er 2               ; Take the page off its class list and give it back
00044Er 2               @release:
00044Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_CSZ
000450r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_CHUNK_CLASS                      ; ZP_M_CLS
000453r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_M_CLS
000455r 2  A0 03                    ldy         #ChunkPage::next_page
000457r 2  BD 2F 7E                 lda         MMU_CHUNK_HEADS,X
00045Ar 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_CP + 1
00045Cr 2  D0 07                    bne         @find_prev
00045Er 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y                         ; It's the first page of the list
000460r 2  9D 2F 7E                 sta         MMU_CHUNK_HEADS,X
000463r 2  80 14                    bra         @give_back
000465r 2               
000465r 2               @find_prev:
000465r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_CPREV
000467r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0
000469r 2  F0 0E                    beq         @give_back                          ; Empty list (shouldn't happen)
00046Br 2               
00046Br 2               @next_prev:                                                 ; .A = a page on the list
00046Br 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CPREV + 1
00046Dr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CPREV),Y                      ; Its next page
00046Fr 2  F0 08                    beq         @give_back                          ; Not on the list (shouldn't happen)
000471r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_CP + 1
000473r 2  D0 F6                    bne         @next_prev
000475r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP),Y                         ; prev.next_page = page.next_page
000477r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_CPREV),Y
000479r 2               
000479r 2               @give_back:
000479r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_CP + 1
00047Br 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_PAGE_FREE
00047Er 2               
00047Er 2               ; Chunk size class for a size.
00047Er 2               ; IN: .A = bytes (MMU_CHUNK_MIN - MMU_CHUNK_MAX)
00047Er 2               ; OUT: ZP_M_CLS = class index, ZP_M_CSZ = chunk size
00047Er 2               ; Modifies: .X, .Y; preserves .A
00047Er 2               MM_CHUNK_CLASS:
00047Er 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
000480r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #MMU_CHUNK_MIN
000482r 2               
000482r 2               @loop:
000482r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_CSZ
000484r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_CSZ
000486r 2  F0 0A                    beq         @found
000488r 2  90 08                    bcc         @found
00048Ar 2  48                       pha
00048Br 2  98                       tya
00048Cr 2  0A                       asl
00048Dr 2  A8                       tay
00048Er 2  68                       pla
00048Fr 2  E8                       inx
000490r 2  80 F0                    bra         @loop
000492r 2               
000492r 2               @found:
000492r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_CLS
000494r 2  60                       rts
000495r 2               
000495r 2               ; Find the handle of the current task's chunk or page allocation that starts at an address.
000495r 2               ; (Not for AI_SMALL or AI_PAGED allocations: they have no unique address.)
000495r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = address (.A = low byte)
000495r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = handle, C = 0
000495r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1
000495r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000495r 2               MM_FIND:
000495r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000496r 2  78                       sei
000497r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000499r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1                            ; The address
00049Br 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
00049Dr 2  A9 7E 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MMU_HANDLE_TBL, ZP_M_HP
0004A1r 2  A9 65 85 rr  
0004A5r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0004A7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HANDLE
0004A9r 2               
0004A9r 2               @loop:
0004A9r 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status
0004ABr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0004ADr 2  29 99                    and         #AI_IN_USE | AI_SMALL | AI_PAGED
0004AFr 2  C9 90                    cmp         #AI_IN_USE                          ; In use, and a chunk or page run
0004B1r 2  D0 14                    bne         @next
0004B3r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #Handle::addr_l
0004B5r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0004B7r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_SP1
0004B9r 2  D0 0C                    bne         @next
0004BBr 2  C8                       iny
0004BCr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0004BEr 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
0004C0r 2  D0 05                    bne         @next
0004C2r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
0004C4r 2  18                       clc
0004C5r 2  80 16                    bra         @done
0004C7r 2               
0004C7r 2               @next:
0004C7r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HP                             ; Next entry
0004C9r 2  18                       clc
0004CAr 2  69 04                    adc         #.sizeof(Handle)
0004CCr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HP
0004CEr 2  90 02                    bcc         :+
0004D0r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_HP + 1
0004D2r 2               :
0004D2r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_HANDLE
0004D4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
0004D6r 2  C9 67                    cmp         #MMU_MAX_HANDLES + 1
0004D8r 2  D0 CF                    bne         @loop
0004DAr 2  A9 41                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID
0004DCr 2  38                       sec
0004DDr 2               
0004DDr 2               @done:
0004DDr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0004DFr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0004E2r 2               
0004E2r 2               ; Find a free handle table entry in the current task.
0004E2r 2               ; OUT (success): ZP_M_HANDLE = handle, ZP_M_HP = its entry, C = 0
0004E2r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES, C = 1
0004E2r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
0004E2r 2               MM_NEW_HANDLE:
0004E2r 2  A9 7E 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MMU_HANDLE_TBL, ZP_M_HP
0004E6r 2  A9 65 85 rr  
0004EAr 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0004ECr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HANDLE
0004EEr 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status
0004F0r 2               
0004F0r 2               @loop:
0004F0r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0004F2r 2  F0 17                    beq         @found
0004F4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HP                             ; Next entry
0004F6r 2  18                       clc
0004F7r 2  69 04                    adc         #.sizeof(Handle)
0004F9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HP
0004FBr 2  90 02                    bcc         :+
0004FDr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_HP + 1
0004FFr 2               :
0004FFr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_HANDLE
000501r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
000503r 2  C9 67                    cmp         #MMU_MAX_HANDLES + 1
000505r 2  D0 E9                    bne         @loop
000507r 2  A9 44                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES
000509r 2  38                       sec
00050Ar 2  60                       rts
00050Br 2               
00050Br 2               @found:
00050Br 2  18                       clc
00050Cr 2  60                       rts
00050Dr 2               
00050Dr 2               ; Point ZP_M_HP at a handle's entry, and check that it's in use.
00050Dr 2               ; IN: .A = handle
00050Dr 2               ; OUT (success): .A = entry status, ZP_M_HP = entry, ZP_M_HANDLE = handle, C = 0
00050Dr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1
00050Dr 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
00050Dr 2               MM_HANDLE_PTR:
00050Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HANDLE
00050Fr 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0
000511r 2  F0 26                    beq         @bad
000513r 2  C9 67                    cmp         #MMU_MAX_HANDLES + 1
000515r 2  B0 22                    bcs         @bad
000517r 2  3A                       dec                                             ; Entry = MMU_HANDLE_TBL + (handle - 1) * 4
000518r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_HP + 1
00051Ar 2  0A                       asl
00051Br 2  26 rr                    rol         ZP_M_HP + 1
00051Dr 2  0A                       asl
00051Er 2  26 rr                    rol         ZP_M_HP + 1
000520r 2  18                       clc
000521r 2  69 65                    adc         #<MMU_HANDLE_TBL
000523r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HP
000525r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HP + 1
000527r 2  69 7E                    adc         #>MMU_HANDLE_TBL
000529r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HP + 1
00052Br 2  A0 03                    ldy         #Handle::status
00052Dr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
00052Fr 2  29 90                    and         #AI_IN_USE
000531r 2  C9 90                    cmp         #AI_IN_USE
000533r 2  D0 04                    bne         @bad
000535r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000537r 2  18                       clc
000538r 2  60                       rts
000539r 2               
000539r 2               @bad:
000539r 2  A9 41                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID
00053Br 2  38                       sec
00053Cr 2  60                       rts
00053Dr 2               
00053Dr 2               ; Point ZP_M_SP1 at an allocation's data and select its RAM bank (AI_PAGED).  The current RAM bank is
00053Dr 2               ; saved in ZP_M_SV, for the caller to restore.
00053Dr 2               ; IN: .A = entry status, ZP_M_HP = entry, ZP_M_TEMP = offset to check (AI_SMALL: must be < length)
00053Dr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00053Dr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG, C = 1
00053Dr 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y; preserves .X
00053Dr 2               MM_ACCESS_SETUP:
00053Dr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         RAM_BANK_REG
00053Fr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_SV
000541r 2  89 08                    bit         #AI_SMALL
000543r 2  F0 12                    beq         @not_small
000545r 2  29 03                    and         #AI_SMALL_LEN
000547r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_TEMP
000549r 2  F0 39                    beq         @bad_arg                            ; Offset >= length
00054Br 2  90 37                    bcc         @bad_arg
00054Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HP                             ; The data is in the entry itself
00054Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1
000551r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HP + 1
000553r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
000555r 2  18                       clc
000556r 2  60                       rts
000557r 2               
000557r 2               @not_small:
000557r 2  89 01                    bit         #AI_PAGED
000559r 2  F0 06                    beq         :+
00055Br 2  A0 02                    ldy         #Handle::bank
00055Dr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
00055Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000561r 2               :
000561r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CLS                            ; Keep the status (.X must be preserved)
000563r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #Handle::addr_l
000565r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000567r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1
000569r 2  C8                       iny
00056Ar 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
00056Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
00056Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_CLS
000570r 2  29 05                    and         #AI_PAGED | AI_BLOCK
000572r 2  D0 0E                    bne         @ok                                 ; Pages and banks: any 8-bit offset is inside
000574r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1 + 1                        ; Chunk: offset must be < the chunk size
000576r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CP + 1
000578r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_CP
00057Ar 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_CP)                           ; ChunkPage::size
00057Cr 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_TEMP
00057Er 2  F0 04                    beq         @bad_arg
000580r 2  90 02                    bcc         @bad_arg
000582r 2               
000582r 2               @ok:
000582r 2  18                       clc
000583r 2  60                       rts
000584r 2               
000584r 2               @bad_arg:
000584r 2  A9 43                    lda         #ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG
000586r 2  38                       sec
000587r 2  60                       rts
000588r 2               
000588r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000588r 2               ; Reset a task's memory: everything it allocated is freed, without walking its handles.
000588r 2               ;   1. Its MMU area is re-initialized (all its task pages, chunks, banks and handles are freed)
000588r 2               ;   2. Its shared memory references are dropped (banks nobody else references are freed)
000588r 2               ;   3. The message rings it sends or receives on are emptied
000588r 2               ;   4. Its IRQ / S/W interrupt handlers are unregistered
000588r 2               ; Its fds are closed first.
000588r 2               ; Called when a task completes (TASK_START).  The task must not be running (or be the calling task).
000588r 2               ; IN: .A = task
000588r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000588r 2               MM_TASK_RESET:
000588r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000589r 2  78                       sei
00058Ar 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
00058Dr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
00058Fr 2  48                       pha
000590r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
000592r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   IO_CLOSE_ALL, ZP_TC_VEC
000596r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00059Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                           ; Close its fds, in the task
00059Dr 2  68                       pla                                             ; (Its requests to the servers change
00059Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK                          ;   ZP_TC_TASK when it's this task)
0005A0r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MM_TASK_INIT, ZP_TC_VEC
0005A4r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
0005A8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                           ; Re-initialize its MMU area, in the task
0005ABr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TC_TASK
0005ADr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_RESET_TASK
0005B0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MSG_RESET_TASK
0005B3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_UNREGISTER_TASK
0005B6r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0005B9r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
0005BAr 2  60                       rts
0005BBr 2               
0005BBr 2               ; Memory Copy
0005BBr 2               ; ZP_TEMP_VEC: From, ZP_TEMP_VEC2: To, .A.Y: Size
0005BBr 2               MEM_COPY:
0005BBr 2  DA                       phx
0005BCr 2  AA                       tax
0005BDr 2  F0 01                    beq         :+                                  ; Whole pages?  .Y is already the pass count
0005BFr 2  C8                       iny                                             ; Partial page counts as one more pass of .X
0005C0r 2               :
0005C0r 2  98                       tya
0005C1r 2  F0 3C                    beq         @done                               ; copy zero bytes?  Done!
0005C3r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0005C6r 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0005CAr 2  rr           
0005CBr 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC
0005CFr 2  rr           
0005D0r 2  A9 2D 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_DASH, #ASCII_GT
0005D4r 2  rr A9 3E 20  
0005D8r 2  rr rr        
0005DAr 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1
0005DEr 2  rr           
0005DFr 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC2
0005E3r 2  rr           
0005E4r 2               
0005E4r 2               @loop:
0005E4r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_TEMP_VEC)
0005E6r 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC2)
0005E8r 2  A9 2E 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_PERIOD
0005ECr 2  rr           
0005EDr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC
0005EFr 2  D0 02                    bne         :+
0005F1r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0005F3r 2               :
0005F3r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC2
0005F5r 2  D0 02                    bne         :+
0005F7r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1
0005F9r 2               :
0005F9r 2  CA                       dex
0005FAr 2  D0 E8                    bne         @loop
0005FCr 2  88                       dey
0005FDr 2  D0 E5                    bne         @loop
0005FFr 2               
0005FFr 2               @done:
0005FFr 2  FA                       plx
000600r 2  60                       rts
000601r 2               
000601r 2               ; TESTS
000601r 2               MEM_TEST:
000601r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000604r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000607r 2                           ;stz         T_REGISTER
000607r 2               
000607r 2               @task_num_loop:
000607r 2  64 rr                    stz         RAM_BANK_REG
000609r 2  A9 04                    lda         #$04
00060Br 2  A2 80                    ldx         #$80                                ; exclude the task serial buffers @ $0200 && $0300
00060Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TEST_PAGE_RANGE
000610r 2  A2 A0                    ldx         #$A0                                ; end of banked RAM
000612r 2               
000612r 2               @ram_bank_loop:
000612r 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  RAM_BANK_REG
000616r 2  rr           
000617r 2  A9 2E 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_PERIOD
00061Br 2  rr           
00061Cr 2  A9 80                    lda         #$80
00061Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TEST_PAGE_RANGE
000621r 2  E6 rr                    inc         RAM_BANK_REG
000623r 2  A9 F0                    lda         #NUM_RAM_BANKS
000625r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         RAM_BANK_REG
000627r 2  D0 E9                    bne         @ram_bank_loop
000629r 2                           ;inc         T_REGISTER
000629r 2                           ;lda         #$10
000629r 2                           ;cmp         T_REGISTER
000629r 2                           ;bne         @task_num_loop
000629r 2  9C F1 FF                 stz         U_REGISTER
00062Cr 2

00062Cr 2  A9 F0                    lda         #$F0
00062Er 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000630r 2

000630r 2  AD F1 FF 20              PRINT_BYTE  U_REGISTER
000634r 2  rr rr

00063Ar 2  rr           
00063Br 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  RAM_BANK_REG
00063Fr 2  rr           
000640r 2  A9 2E 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_PERIOD
000644r 2  rr

000647r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TEST_PAGE_RANGE
00064Ar 2  E6 rr                    inc         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; increment shared bank
00064Cr 2  D0 E2                    bne         @shared_bank_loop
00064Er 2  EE F1 FF                 inc         U_REGISTER

000654r 2  C9 10                    cmp         #$10
