000658r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
00065Br 2  60                       rts
00065Cr 2               
00065Cr 2               ; .A = HOB of first page to test, .X = HOB of last page + 1
00065Cr 2               TEST_PAGE_RANGE:
00065Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
00065Er 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
000661r 2  A9 00 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  #0
000665r 2  rr           
000666r 2  A9 2E 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_PERIOD
00066Ar 2  rr           
00066Br 2  8A                       txa
00066Cr 2  3A                       dec
00066Dr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
000670r 2  A9 FF 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  #$FF
000674r 2  rr           
000675r 2  A9 3A 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COLON
000679r 2  rr           
00067Ar 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC
00067Cr 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP
00067Er 2               
00067Er 2               @loop_init:
00067Er 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000680r 2               
000680r 2               @loop:
000680r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y                     ; save what is in memory (non-descructive)
000682r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV
000684r 2  A9 AA                    lda         #$AA
000686r 2               @test_it:
000686r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
000688r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
00068Ar 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
00068Cr 2  F0 04                    beq         @next
00068Er 2  A9 21                    lda         #ASCII_BANG
000690r 2  80 0D                    bra         @write                              ; always, no need to restore value since it isn't storing properly anyway
000692r 2               
000692r 2               @next:
000692r 2  C9 AA                    cmp         #$AA
000694r 2  D0 F0                    bne         @test_it
000696r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV                             ; restore saved value
000698r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
00069Ar 2  C8                       iny
00069Br 2  D0 E3                    bne         @loop
00069Dr 2  A9 2E                    lda         #ASCII_PERIOD
00069Fr 2               
00069Fr 2               @write:
00069Fr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
0006A2r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP
0006A4r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0006A6r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0006A8r 2  D0 D4                    bne         @loop_init
0006AAr 2  4C rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF_JMP
0006ADr 2               
0006ADr 2               ; test memory pages, start page in .A, end page in .X
0006ADr 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST_RANGE_AX:
0006ADr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_SP2_H
0006AFr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1_H
0006B1r 2  80 06                    bra         DEEP_PAGE_TEST_RANGE
0006B3r 2               
0006B3r 2               ; test a single page in .A
0006B3r 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST_A:
0006B3r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1_H
0006B5r 2               
0006B5r 2               ; test a single page in ZP_M_SP1_H
0006B5r 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST:
0006B5r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
0006B7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP2_H
0006B9r 2               
0006B9r 2               ; test a range of pages, start in ZP_M_SP1_H, end in ZP_M_SP2_H
0006B9r 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST_RANGE:
0006B9r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0006BBr 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_SP1_L
0006BDr 2               @next_page:
0006BDr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
0006BFr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
0006C2r 2  A9 00 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  #0
0006C6r 2  rr           
0006C7r 2  A9 3A 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COLON
0006CBr 2  rr           
0006CCr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0006CFr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0006D1r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0006D3r 2               @next_cell:
0006D3r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_TEMP
0006D5r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1),Y
0006D7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV                             ; save the old value
0006D9r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0006DBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP2
0006DDr 2               @loop_start:
0006DDr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0006DFr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; store it
0006E1r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; test it
0006E3r 2  D0 08                    bne         :+
0006E5r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF                                ; test the inverse
0006E7r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; store it
0006E9r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; test it
0006EBr 2  F0 06                    beq         @shift
0006EDr 2               :
0006EDr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0006EFr 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; add bit to set of bad bits found
0006F1r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
0006F3r 2               @shift:
0006F3r 2  06 rr                    asl         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Walk the bit forward
0006F5r 2  D0 E6                    bne         @loop_start
0006F7r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; get the set of bad bits we found
0006F9r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
0006FCr 2  E8                       inx
0006FDr 2  E0 10                    cpx         #$10
0006FFr 2  F0 07                    beq         :+
000701r 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_SPACE
000705r 2  rr           
000706r 2  80 05                    bra         :++
000708r 2               :
000708r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
00070Br 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
00070Dr 2               :
00070Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV
00070Fr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; restore the old value
000711r 2  C8                       iny
000712r 2  D0 BF                    bne         @next_cell
000714r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
000716r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_SP2_H
000718r 2  F0 05                    beq         @done
00071Ar 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_SP1_H
00071Cr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @next_page
00071Fr 2               @done:
00071Fr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000721r 2  60                       rts
000722r 2               
000722r 1               .include "irq.s"
000722r 2               .debuginfo
000722r 2               
000722r 2               .segment "IRQ"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; IRQ dispatch (see MMU_PLAN.md, IO subsystem)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   Every IRQ (and BRK) enters through a stub in the COMMON block, which saves W and switches to ROM
000000r 2               ;   page 0, then jumps to IRQ_DISPATCH.  The dispatcher looks the IRQ up in the registration table and
000000r 2               ;   runs each registered handler IN THE TASK THAT REGISTERED IT (via TASK_CALL), then switches back.
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   The registration tables live in the task system page and are replicated into every task, so the
000000r 2               ;   dispatcher can read them from whichever task was interrupted.  Registration writes all 16 copies.
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   Handler convention:
000000r 2               ;       IN:  .A = logical IRQ# (0-14), or S/W interrupt# (0-15) for S/W interrupts;  I flag set
000000r 2               ;       OUT: C = 1 if the interrupt was claimed (stops the chain), C = 0 if not
000000r 2               ;       May clobber .A, .X, .Y.  Must not re-enable interrupts, and must not use FAR_GATEs.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               IRQ_MAX_CHAIN       = 2                                     ; Handlers per hardware IRQ
000000r 2               IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE      = 3                                     ; {task, handler.w}
000000r 2               IRQ_LOGICAL_SW      = 15                                    ; Logical IRQ# of the S/W interrupt (IRQ_NUMBER_SW)
000000r 2               IRQ_NO_TASK         = $FF                                   ; Empty table entry
000000r 2               
000000r 2               IRQ_SYS_BASE        = MMU_SYS_PAGE * $100                   ; Task system page ($7D00)
000000r 2               IRQ_TABLE           = IRQ_SYS_BASE                          ; 16 IRQs x IRQ_MAX_CHAIN x {task, handler.w}
000000r 2               IRQ_SWI_OFFSET      = 16 * IRQ_MAX_CHAIN * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE   ; 16 S/W interrupts x {task, handler.w} follow
000000r 2               IRQ_TABLE_SIZE      = IRQ_SWI_OFFSET + (16 * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE)
000000r 2               IRQ_SPURIOUS        = IRQ_TABLE + IRQ_TABLE_SIZE            ; 16 counters, by logical IRQ# ($7D90-$7D9F in the interrupted
000000r 2                                                                           ; task): IRQs not claimed by their own handlers
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .assert     IRQ_TABLE_SIZE <= 256, error, "IRQ table must be indexable by .X"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Set up the IRQ tables in every task's system page, and point all 16 vectors at the IRQ stubs.
000000r 2               ; Must be called before interrupts are enabled.
000000r 2               IRQ_INIT:
000000r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000001r 2  78                       sei
000002r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000005r 2  AE F0 FF                 ldx         T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0 holds the home task # during the loop
000008r 2  9C F0 FF                 stz         T_REGISTER
00000Br 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
00000Dr 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00000Fr 2               
00000Fr 2               @task_loop:
00000Fr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Quick switch to task X (no stack use!)
000012r 2  A0 90                    ldy         #IRQ_TABLE_SIZE
000014r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #IRQ_NO_TASK
000016r 2               
000016r 2               @clear_table:
000016r 2  88                       dey
000017r 2  99 00 7D                 sta         IRQ_TABLE,Y
00001Ar 2  D0 FA                    bne         @clear_table
00001Cr 2  A0 10                    ldy         #16
00001Er 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000020r 2               
000020r 2               @clear_spurious:
000020r 2  88                       dey
000021r 2  99 90 7D                 sta         IRQ_SPURIOUS,Y
000024r 2  D0 FA                    bne         @clear_spurious
000026r 2  CA                       dex
000027r 2  10 E6                    bpl         @task_loop                          ; Ends in task 0
000029r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
00002Br 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Back to the home task
00002Er 2               
00002Er 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   IRQ_STUB_0, ZP_TEMP_VEC
000032r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000036r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Logical IRQ#
000038r 2               
000038r 2               @vector_loop:
000038r 2  DA                       phx
000039r 2  8A                       txa
00003Ar 2  49 07                    eor         #7                                  ; IRQ_NUMBER(): logical -> vector index
00003Cr 2  AA                       tax
00003Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
00003Fr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000041r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_SET_VECTOR
000044r 2  FA                       plx
000045r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Next stub (6 bytes each)
000047r 2  18                       clc
000048r 2  69 06                    adc         #6
00004Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC
00004Cr 2  90 02                    bcc         :+
00004Er 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000050r 2               :
000050r 2  E8                       inx
000051r 2  E0 10                    cpx         #16
000053r 2  D0 E3                    bne         @vector_loop
000055r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
000058r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000059r 2  18                       clc
00005Ar 2  60                       rts
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               ; X: IRQ# (vector index, i.e. IRQ_NUMBER(n)), .A.Y: Vector Addr
00005Br 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y, V and the caller's I flag
00005Br 2               IRQ_SET_VECTOR:
00005Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00005Cr 2  78                       sei
00005Dr 2  DA                       phx
00005Er 2  48                       pha
00005Fr 2  AD F2 FF                 lda         V_REGISTER                          ; Save V (shared pseudo-register)
000062r 2  8E F2 FF                 stx         V_REGISTER                          ; Select the vector for IRQ# .X
000065r 2  AA                       tax                                             ; .X = prior V
000066r 2  68                       pla
000067r 2  8D FE FF                 sta         $FFFE
00006Ar 2  8C FF FF                 sty         $FFFF
00006Dr 2  8E F2 FF                 stx         V_REGISTER                          ; Restore prior V
000070r 2  FA                       plx
000071r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000072r 2  60                       rts
000073r 2               
000073r 2               ; Entered from IRQ_ENTRY (COMMON block) on ROM page 0.
000073r 2               ; .A = logical IRQ#, .X = interrupted ROM page.  Stack: .X, .A, then the interrupt frame.
000073r 2               IRQ_DISPATCH:
000073r 2  DA                       phx                                             ; Interrupted ROM page, for IRQ_EXIT
000074r 2  5A                       phy
000075r 2  D8                       cld
000076r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TC_VEC                           ; The interrupted code may be setting up a TASK_CALL
000078r 2  DA                       phx
000079r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
00007Br 2  DA                       phx
00007Cr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TC_TASK
00007Er 2  DA                       phx
00007Fr 2  C9 0F                    cmp         #IRQ_LOGICAL_SW
000081r 2  F0 29                    beq         @swi
000083r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_NUM
000085r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x6 = IRQ_MAX_CHAIN * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
000086r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_NUM
000088r 2  0A                       asl
000089r 2  AA                       tax
00008Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
00008Dr 2  B0 2F                    bcs         @done
00008Fr 2  E8                       inx
000090r 2  E8                       inx
000091r 2  E8                       inx
000092r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
000095r 2  B0 27                    bcs         @done
000097r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_NUM                          ; Nobody on its own chain claimed it
000099r 2  FE 90 7D                 inc         IRQ_SPURIOUS,X
00009Cr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Offer it to every registered hardware handler: each
00009Er 2                                                                           ; checks its own device, so an IRQ that arrives on an
00009Er 2               @poll_all:                                                  ; unexpected vector still gets cleared (no IRQ storm)
00009Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
0000A1r 2  B0 1B                    bcs         @done
0000A3r 2  E8                       inx
0000A4r 2  E8                       inx
0000A5r 2  E8                       inx
0000A6r 2  E0 60                    cpx         #IRQ_SWI_OFFSET
0000A8r 2  D0 F4                    bne         @poll_all
0000AAr 2  80 12                    bra         @done
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               @swi:
0000ACr 2  AD F2 FF                 lda         V_REGISTER                          ; S/W interrupt# is in V[4..7]
0000AFr 2  4A                       lsr
0000B0r 2  4A                       lsr
0000B1r 2  4A                       lsr
0000B2r 2  4A                       lsr
0000B3r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_NUM
0000B5r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x3 = IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
0000B6r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_NUM
0000B8r 2  69 60                    adc         #IRQ_SWI_OFFSET
0000BAr 2  AA                       tax
0000BBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
0000BEr 2               
0000BEr 2               @done:
0000BEr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_RESCHED                      ; A handler asked for a task switch (timer tick)?
0000C0r 2  F0 0E                    beq         IRQ_RESTORE
0000C2r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
0000C4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SCHED_CAN_PREEMPT
0000C7r 2  90 07                    bcc         IRQ_RESTORE
0000C9r 2  AD F1 FF                 lda         U_REGISTER                          ; Complete the task's frame (tasks.s) and switch
0000CCr 2  48                       pha
0000CDr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SCHED_SWITCH
0000D0r 2               
0000D0r 2               ; Unwind the dispatcher's part of a task's frame and return to the interrupted code (also the end of
0000D0r 2               ; SCHED_RESUME)
0000D0r 2               IRQ_RESTORE:
0000D0r 2  68                       pla
0000D1r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
0000D3r 2  68                       pla
0000D4r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0000D6r 2  68                       pla
0000D7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC
0000D9r 2  7A                       ply
0000DAr 2  68                       pla                                             ; Interrupted ROM page
0000DBr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         IRQ_EXIT
0000DEr 2               
0000DEr 2               ; Run the handler in table entry .X (if any), in its task.
0000DEr 2               ; IN: .X = table offset.  OUT: C = 1 if claimed.  Preserves .X
0000DEr 2               IRQ_CALL_ENTRY:
0000DEr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
0000E0r 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
0000E3r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IRQ_NO_TASK
0000E5r 2  F0 23                    beq         @none
0000E7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
0000E9r 2  BD 01 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 1,X
0000ECr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC
0000EEr 2  BD 02 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 2,X
0000F1r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0000F3r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_NUM
0000F5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL
0000F8r 2  90 0D                    bcc         @done                               ; Not claimed
0000FAr 2  C9 A5                    cmp         #SCHED_RESCHED_A                    ; Claimed; a task switch asked for?
0000FCr 2  D0 08                    bne         @claimed
0000FEr 2  C0 5A                    cpy         #SCHED_RESCHED_Y
000100r 2  D0 04                    bne         @claimed
000102r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
000104r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
000106r 2               
000106r 2               @claimed:
000106r 2  38                       sec
000107r 2               
000107r 2               @done:
000107r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000109r 2  60                       rts
00010Ar 2               
00010Ar 2               @none:
00010Ar 2  18                       clc
00010Br 2  60                       rts
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00010Cr 2               ; Registration.  A handler always runs in the task that registered it (the current task), so drivers
00010Cr 2               ; register from their init routine, which DRV_START runs in the driver's task.
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2               ; Register a handler for a hardware IRQ.  Registering the same handler twice from the same task is OK.
00010Cr 2               ; IN: .X = IRQ# (IRQ_NUMBER(n), as used for V), .A.Y = handler
00010Cr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00010Cr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL, C = 1
00010Cr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00010Cr 2               IRQ_REGISTER:
00010Cr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00010Dr 2  78                       sei
00010Er 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000110r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_HW_SETUP
000113r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REG_AT
000116r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000118r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00011Br 2               
00011Br 2               ; Unregister a handler registered from the current task.
00011Br 2               ; IN: .X = IRQ# (IRQ_NUMBER(n)), .A.Y = handler
00011Br 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00011Br 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND, C = 1
00011Br 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00011Br 2               IRQ_UNREGISTER:
00011Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00011Cr 2  78                       sei
00011Dr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00011Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_HW_SETUP
000122r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_UNREG_AT
000125r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000127r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00012Ar 2               
00012Ar 2               ; Register a handler for a S/W interrupt (SW_INT).  One handler per S/W interrupt#.
00012Ar 2               ; IN: .X = S/W interrupt# ($0-$F), .A.Y = handler
00012Ar 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00012Ar 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL, C = 1
00012Ar 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00012Ar 2               SWI_REGISTER:
00012Ar 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00012Br 2  78                       sei
00012Cr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00012Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_SWI_SETUP
000131r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REG_AT
000134r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000136r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000139r 2               
000139r 2               ; Unregister a S/W interrupt handler registered from the current task.
000139r 2               ; IN: .X = S/W interrupt# ($0-$F), .A.Y = handler
000139r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000139r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND, C = 1
000139r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000139r 2               SWI_UNREGISTER:
000139r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00013Ar 2  78                       sei
00013Br 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00013Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_SWI_SETUP
000140r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_UNREG_AT
000143r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000145r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000148r 2               
000148r 2               ; IN: .X = IRQ_NUMBER(n), .A.Y = handler.  OUT: .X = first table offset, ZP_IRQ_CNT, ZP_IRQ_H set
000148r 2               IRQ_HW_SETUP:
000148r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_H
00014Ar 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
00014Cr 2  8A                       txa
00014Dr 2  49 07                    eor         #7                                  ; Vector index -> logical IRQ#
00014Fr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000151r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000153r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x6 = IRQ_MAX_CHAIN * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
000154r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000156r 2  0A                       asl
000157r 2  AA                       tax
000158r 2  A9 02                    lda         #IRQ_MAX_CHAIN
00015Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_CNT
00015Cr 2  60                       rts
00015Dr 2               
00015Dr 2               ; IN: .X = S/W interrupt#, .A.Y = handler.  OUT: .X = table offset, ZP_IRQ_CNT, ZP_IRQ_H set
00015Dr 2               IRQ_SWI_SETUP:
00015Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_H
00015Fr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
000161r 2  8A                       txa
000162r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000164r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000166r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x3 = IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
000167r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000169r 2  69 60                    adc         #IRQ_SWI_OFFSET
00016Br 2  AA                       tax
00016Cr 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
00016Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_CNT
000170r 2  60                       rts
000171r 2               
000171r 2               ; Add (current task, ZP_IRQ_H) to the ZP_IRQ_CNT entries starting at offset .X, unless already there.
000171r 2               IRQ_REG_AT:
000171r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000173r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IRQ_CNT
000175r 2               
000175r 2               @find_existing:
000175r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_MATCH
000178r 2  F0 31                    beq         @ok                                 ; Already registered
00017Ar 2  E8                       inx
00017Br 2  E8                       inx
00017Cr 2  E8                       inx
00017Dr 2  88                       dey
00017Er 2  D0 F5                    bne         @find_existing
000180r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000182r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IRQ_CNT
000184r 2               
000184r 2               @find_free:
000184r 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
000187r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IRQ_NO_TASK
000189r 2  F0 0A                    beq         @store
00018Br 2  E8                       inx
00018Cr 2  E8                       inx
00018Dr 2  E8                       inx
00018Er 2  88                       dey
00018Fr 2  D0 F3                    bne         @find_free
000191r 2  A9 50                    lda         #ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL
000193r 2  38                       sec
000194r 2  60                       rts
000195r 2               
000195r 2               @store:
000195r 2  8A                       txa
000196r 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = entry offset
000197r 2  C8                       iny
000198r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_H
00019Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
00019Dr 2  C8                       iny
00019Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
0001A0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001A3r 2  88                       dey
0001A4r 2  88                       dey
0001A5r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER                          ; Task byte last: it makes the entry live
0001A8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001ABr 2               
0001ABr 2               @ok:
0001ABr 2  18                       clc
0001ACr 2  60                       rts
0001ADr 2               
0001ADr 2               ; Remove (current task, ZP_IRQ_H) from the ZP_IRQ_CNT entries starting at offset .X.
0001ADr 2               IRQ_UNREG_AT:
0001ADr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IRQ_CNT
0001AFr 2               
0001AFr 2               @find:
0001AFr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_MATCH
0001B2r 2  F0 0A                    beq         @remove
0001B4r 2  E8                       inx
0001B5r 2  E8                       inx
0001B6r 2  E8                       inx
0001B7r 2  88                       dey
0001B8r 2  D0 F5                    bne         @find
0001BAr 2  A9 51                    lda         #ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND
0001BCr 2  38                       sec
0001BDr 2  60                       rts
0001BEr 2               
0001BEr 2               @remove:
0001BEr 2  8A                       txa
0001BFr 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = entry offset
0001C0r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #IRQ_NO_TASK
0001C2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001C5r 2  18                       clc
0001C6r 2  60                       rts
0001C7r 2               
0001C7r 2               ; Unregister every IRQ and S/W interrupt handler of a task (for MM_TASK_RESET).
0001C7r 2               ; IN: .A = task
0001C7r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0001C7r 2               IRQ_UNREGISTER_TASK:
0001C7r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0001C8r 2  78                       sei
0001C9r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0001CCr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
0001CEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_TMP
0001D0r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0001D2r 2               
0001D2r 2               @loop:
0001D2r 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
0001D5r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IRQ_TMP
0001D7r 2  D0 09                    bne         @next
0001D9r 2  DA                       phx
0001DAr 2  8A                       txa
0001DBr 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = entry offset
0001DCr 2  A9 FF                    lda         #IRQ_NO_TASK
0001DEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001E1r 2  FA                       plx
0001E2r 2               
0001E2r 2               @next:
0001E2r 2  E8                       inx
0001E3r 2  E8                       inx
0001E4r 2  E8                       inx
0001E5r 2  E0 90                    cpx         #IRQ_TABLE_SIZE
0001E7r 2  D0 E9                    bne         @loop
0001E9r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0001ECr 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
0001EDr 2  60                       rts
0001EEr 2               
0001EEr 2               ; Z = 1 if table entry .X is (current task, ZP_IRQ_H).  Preserves .X, .Y
0001EEr 2               IRQ_MATCH:
0001EEr 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
0001F1r 2  CD F0 FF                 cmp         T_REGISTER
0001F4r 2  D0 0C                    bne         @done
0001F6r 2  BD 01 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 1,X
0001F9r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IRQ_H
0001FBr 2  D0 05                    bne         @done
0001FDr 2  BD 02 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 2,X
000200r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
000202r 2               
000202r 2               @done:
000202r 2  60                       rts
000203r 2               
000203r 2               ; Write .A to IRQ_TABLE + .Y in every task's system page.  I flag must be set.
000203r 2               ; Preserves .A, .Y
000203r 2               IRQ_REPLICATE:
000203r 2  AE F0 FF                 ldx         T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0 holds the home task # during the loop
000206r 2  9C F0 FF                 stz         T_REGISTER
000209r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
00020Br 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00020Dr 2               
00020Dr 2               @loop:
00020Dr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Quick switch to task X (no stack use!)
000210r 2  99 00 7D                 sta         IRQ_TABLE,Y
000213r 2  CA                       dex
000214r 2  10 F7                    bpl         @loop                               ; Ends in task 0
000216r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
000218r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Back to the home task
00021Br 2  60                       rts
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 1               .include "msg.s"
00021Cr 2               .debuginfo
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 2               .segment "MSG"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; Message rings (see MMU_PLAN.md, Messaging)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   One 256-byte ring for each (receiver, sender) task pair, in shared RAM, so each ring has exactly one
000000r 2               ;   writer and one reader.  The sender is always the current task, and the receiver reads its own rings.
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   Shared bank ID $00 (U = 0, bank $F0):  ring pointers, indexed by receiver * 16 + sender
000000r 2               ;       $8000-$80FF  MSG_HEADS  write pointers (advanced by the sender)
000000r 2               ;       $8100-$81FF  MSG_TAILS  read pointers (advanced by the receiver)
000000r 2               ;   Shared bank IDs $01-$08 (U = 0, banks $F1-$F8):  the rings
000000r 2               ;       bank ID = 1 + (receiver >> 1), address = $8000 + (receiver & 1) * $1000 + sender * $100
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   This is a byte-stream layer; framed messages (type, length, payload) can be built on top of it.
000000r 2               ;   All routines run with IRQs off and save/restore RAM_BANK_REG and U, so they're safe to call from
000000r 2               ;   IRQ handlers (e.g. a driver delivering input to another task).
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MSG_SHARED_U        = 0                                     ; U (shared macro-page) for all message data
000000r 2               MSG_PTR_BANK        = $F0                                   ; Shared bank ID $00
000000r 2               MSG_RING_BANK       = $F1                                   ; Shared bank ID $01: rings for receivers 0 and 1
000000r 2               MSG_HEADS           = $8000
000000r 2               MSG_TAILS           = $8100
000000r 2               MSG_RING_BASE       = $8000
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Save RAM_BANK_REG and U on the stack and select the ring pointer bank.  Uses .Y
000000r 2               .macro _M_MSG_ENTER
000000r 2                           ldy         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           ldy         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           ldy         #MSG_SHARED_U
000000r 2                           sty         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           ldy         #MSG_PTR_BANK
000000r 2                           sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Restore U and RAM_BANK_REG.  Uses .Y; preserves .A and C
000000r 2               .macro _M_MSG_LEAVE
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           sty         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Clear all ring pointers.  Called by the system task at boot.
000000r 2               MSG_INIT:
000000r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000001r 2  78                       sei
000002r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000005r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER
000009r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00000Dr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000015r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000017r 2  98                       tya
000018r 2               
000018r 2               @loop:
000018r 2  99 00 80                 sta         MSG_HEADS,Y
00001Br 2  99 00 81                 sta         MSG_TAILS,Y
00001Er 2  C8                       iny
00001Fr 2  D0 F7                    bne         @loop
000021r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
000025r 2  7A 84 rr     
000028r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
00002Br 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
00002Cr 2  60                       rts
00002Dr 2               
00002Dr 2               ; Send a byte from the current task to another task.
00002Dr 2               ; IN: .A = byte, .X = receiving task
00002Dr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00002Dr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MSG_FULL or ERR_BAD_TASK, C = 1
00002Dr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00002Dr 2               MSG_SEND_BYTE:
00002Dr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00002Er 2  78                       sei
00002Fr 2  5A                       phy
000030r 2  DA                       phx
000031r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_BYTE
000033r 2  E0 10                    cpx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
000035r 2  B0 4D                    bcs         @bad_task
000037r 2  8A                       txa
000038r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; Receiver * 16
000039r 2  0A                       asl
00003Ar 2  0A                       asl
00003Br 2  0A                       asl
00003Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_IDX
00003Er 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER                          ; + sender
000041r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000043r 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_MSG_IDX
000045r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_IDX
000047r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER
00004Br 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00004Fr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000057r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_MSG_IDX
000059r 2  BD 00 80                 lda         MSG_HEADS,X
00005Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_PTR                          ; Ring offset = head
00005Er 2  1A                       inc
00005Fr 2  DD 00 81                 cmp         MSG_TAILS,X
000062r 2  F0 11                    beq         @full                               ; head + 1 == tail: the ring is full
000064r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MSG_RING_SELECT
000067r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_MSG_BYTE
000069r 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_MSG_PTR)
00006Br 2  A9 F0                    lda         #MSG_PTR_BANK
00006Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
00006Fr 2  FE 00 80                 inc         MSG_HEADS,X
000072r 2  18                       clc
000073r 2  80 03                    bra         @leave
000075r 2               
000075r 2               @full:
000075r 2  A9 60                    lda         #ERR_MSG_FULL
000077r 2  38                       sec
000078r 2               
000078r 2               @leave:
000078r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
00007Cr 2  7A 84 rr     
00007Fr 2               
00007Fr 2               @done:
00007Fr 2  FA                       plx
000080r 2  7A                       ply
000081r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000084r 2               
000084r 2               @bad_task:
000084r 2  A9 F3                    lda         #ERR_BAD_TASK
000086r 2  38                       sec
000087r 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
000089r 2               
000089r 2               ; Receive a byte sent to the current task.
000089r 2               ; IN: .X = sending task, or $FF for any sender (checked from task 0 up)
000089r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = byte, .X = sending task, C = 0
000089r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MSG_EMPTY or ERR_BAD_TASK, C = 1, .X unchanged
000089r 2               ; Preserves .Y
000089r 2               MSG_RECV_BYTE:
000089r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00008Ar 2  78                       sei
00008Br 2  5A                       phy
00008Cr 2  DA                       phx
00008Dr 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER                          ; Receiver * 16
000090r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000092r 2  0A                       asl
000093r 2  0A                       asl
000094r 2  0A                       asl
000095r 2  0A                       asl
000096r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_IDX
000098r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER
00009Cr 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
0000A0r 2  00 8C F1 FF  
0000A8r 2  E0 FF                    cpx         #$FF
0000AAr 2  F0 12                    beq         @any
0000ACr 2  E0 10                    cpx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
0000AEr 2  B0 2D                    bcs         @bad_task
0000B0r 2  8A                       txa                                             ; + sender
0000B1r 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_MSG_IDX
0000B3r 2  AA                       tax
0000B4r 2  BD 00 80                 lda         MSG_HEADS,X
0000B7r 2  DD 00 81                 cmp         MSG_TAILS,X
0000BAr 2  F0 12                    beq         @empty
0000BCr 2  80 23                    bra         @read
0000BEr 2               
0000BEr 2               @any:
0000BEr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_MSG_IDX                          ; Sender 0
0000C0r 2  A0 10                    ldy         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
0000C2r 2               
0000C2r 2               @scan:
0000C2r 2  BD 00 80                 lda         MSG_HEADS,X
0000C5r 2  DD 00 81                 cmp         MSG_TAILS,X
0000C8r 2  D0 17                    bne         @read
0000CAr 2  E8                       inx
0000CBr 2  88                       dey
0000CCr 2  D0 F4                    bne         @scan
0000CEr 2               
0000CEr 2               @empty:
0000CEr 2  A9 61                    lda         #ERR_MSG_EMPTY
0000D0r 2               
0000D0r 2               @fail:
0000D0r 2  38                       sec
0000D1r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
0000D5r 2  7A 84 rr     
0000D8r 2  FA                       plx
0000D9r 2  7A                       ply
0000DAr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0000DDr 2               
0000DDr 2               @bad_task:
0000DDr 2  A9 F3                    lda         #ERR_BAD_TASK
0000DFr 2  80 EF                    bra         @fail
0000E1r 2               
0000E1r 2               @read:                                                      ; .X = ring index
0000E1r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_MSG_IDX
0000E3r 2  BD 00 81                 lda         MSG_TAILS,X
0000E6r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_PTR                          ; Ring offset = tail
0000E8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MSG_RING_SELECT
0000EBr 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_MSG_PTR)
0000EDr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_BYTE
0000EFr 2  A9 F0                    lda         #MSG_PTR_BANK
0000F1r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
0000F3r 2  FE 00 81                 inc         MSG_TAILS,X
0000F6r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
0000FAr 2  7A 84 rr     
0000FDr 2  68                       pla                                             ; Drop the caller's .X
0000FEr 2  8A                       txa
0000FFr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000101r 2  AA                       tax                                             ; .X = sending task
000102r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_MSG_BYTE
000104r 2  7A                       ply
000105r 2  18                       clc
000106r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000109r 2               
000109r 2               ; Number of bytes waiting from a sender to the current task.
000109r 2               ; IN: .X = sending task
000109r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = bytes waiting, C = 0
000109r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_BAD_TASK, C = 1
000109r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000109r 2               MSG_PEEK:
000109r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00010Ar 2  78                       sei
00010Br 2  5A                       phy
00010Cr 2  DA                       phx
00010Dr 2  E0 10                    cpx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
00010Fr 2  B0 33                    bcs         @bad_task
000111r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER                          ; Receiver * 16 + sender
000114r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000116r 2  0A                       asl
000117r 2  0A                       asl
000118r 2  0A                       asl
000119r 2  0A                       asl
00011Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_IDX
00011Cr 2  8A                       txa
00011Dr 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_MSG_IDX
00011Fr 2  AA                       tax
000120r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER
000124r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
000128r 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000130r 2  BD 00 80                 lda         MSG_HEADS,X
000133r 2  38                       sec
000134r 2  FD 00 81                 sbc         MSG_TAILS,X
000137r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
00013Br 2  7A 84 rr     
00013Er 2  18                       clc
00013Fr 2               
00013Fr 2               @done:
00013Fr 2  FA                       plx
000140r 2  7A                       ply
000141r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000144r 2               
000144r 2               @bad_task:
000144r 2  A9 F3                    lda         #ERR_BAD_TASK
000146r 2  38                       sec
000147r 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
000149r 2               
000149r 2               ; Empty every ring the task sends or receives on (for task reset).
000149r 2               ; IN: .A = task
000149r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000149r 2               MSG_RESET_TASK:
000149r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00014Ar 2  78                       sei
00014Br 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
00014Er 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000150r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_BYTE
000152r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER
000156r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00015Ar 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000162r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; Rings where the task receives: task * 16 + (0..15)
000163r 2  0A                       asl
000164r 2  0A                       asl
000165r 2  0A                       asl
000166r 2  AA                       tax
000167r 2  A0 10                    ldy         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
000169r 2               
000169r 2               @receives:
000169r 2  BD 00 80                 lda         MSG_HEADS,X
00016Cr 2  9D 00 81                 sta         MSG_TAILS,X
00016Fr 2  E8                       inx
000170r 2  88                       dey
000171r 2  D0 F6                    bne         @receives
000173r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_MSG_BYTE                         ; Rings where the task sends: (0..15) * 16 + task
000175r 2  A0 10                    ldy         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
000177r 2               
000177r 2               @sends:
000177r 2  BD 00 80                 lda         MSG_HEADS,X
00017Ar 2  9D 00 81                 sta         MSG_TAILS,X
00017Dr 2  8A                       txa
00017Er 2  18                       clc
00017Fr 2  69 10                    adc         #16
000181r 2  AA                       tax
000182r 2  88                       dey
000183r 2  D0 F2                    bne         @sends
000185r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
000189r 2  7A 84 rr     
00018Cr 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
00018Fr 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000190r 2  60                       rts
000191r 2               
000191r 2               ; Point ZP_MSG_PTR + 1 and RAM_BANK_REG at ring ZP_MSG_IDX (U must already be MSG_SHARED_U).
000191r 2               ; Modifies: .A
000191r 2               MSG_RING_SELECT:
000191r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_MSG_IDX
000193r 2  29 1F                    and         #$1F                                ; (receiver & 1) * $10 + sender
000195r 2  09 80                    ora         #>MSG_RING_BASE
000197r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_MSG_PTR + 1
000199r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_MSG_IDX
00019Br 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; receiver >> 1
00019Cr 2  4A                       lsr
00019Dr 2  4A                       lsr
00019Er 2  4A                       lsr
00019Fr 2  4A                       lsr
0001A0r 2  18                       clc
0001A1r 2  69 F1                    adc         #MSG_RING_BANK
0001A3r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
0001A5r 2  60                       rts
0001A6r 2               
0001A6r 1               .include "shared.s"
0001A6r 2               .debuginfo
0001A6r 2               
0001A6r 2               .segment "SHARED"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; Shared memory (see MMU_PLAN.md)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   256 shared 8K banks: shared bank ID = U << 4 | (bank & $0F), seen at $8000-$9FFF with RAM_BANK_REG =
000000r 2               ;   $F0-$FF.  The system data lives in shared bank ID $00 (U = 0, bank $F0):
000000r 2               ;       $8000-$81FF  message ring pointers (msg.s)
000000r 2               ;       $8200-$821F  SH_MAP     shared bank bitmap (1 = in use)
000000r 2               ;       $8220-$823F  SH_ENDS    run-end bitmap (last bank of each allocation)
000000r 2               ;       $8400-$87FF  SH_HANDLES shared handle table: 255 entries of ShHandle
000000r 2               ;       $8800-$88FF  IO_DEV_TABLE the IO device table (io.s)
000000r 2               ;   Bank IDs $00 (system), $01-$08 (message rings) and $09 (IO transfer areas) are reserved, as are the
000000r 2               ;   banks of any U macro-page
000000r 2               ;   whose RAM isn't installed, and the banks of any RAM chip that failed the POST (ZP_M_BAD_SH: chip c
000000r 2               ;   holds bank IDs 4c - 4c+3 of every U).
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   A shared handle is a 1-byte index (1-255) that any task can use, so it can be sent in a message.
000000r 2               ;   Each task that uses it holds a reference (SH_ALLOC / SH_ATTACH); the banks are freed when the last
000000r 2               ;   reference goes (SH_DETACH, or MM_TASK_RESET of the task).  Only tasks holding a reference can read or
000000r 2               ;   write through the handle.  All calls: C = 0 on success, C = 1 with the error in .A.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SH_MAP              = $8200
000000r 2               SH_ENDS             = $8220
000000r 2               SH_HANDLES          = $8400
000000r 2               SH_MAX_HANDLES      = 255
000000r 2               SH_FIRST_FREE_ID    = $0A                                   ; Below: system data, message rings, IO transfers
000000r 2               SH_WINDOW           = PAGED_RAM_BASE                        ; Where SH_LOCK maps a shared allocation
000000r 2               SH_PROBE_ADDR       = $9FFF                                 ; Probe byte (bank $F0 of each U)
000000r 2               SH_PROBE_MARK       = $50                                   ; Probe marker: SH_PROBE_MARK + U
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     ShHandle
000000r 2                           bank        .byte                               ; First shared bank ID
000000r 2                           count       .byte                               ; Banks (0 = free entry)
000000r 2                           mask_lo     .byte                               ; Tasks $0-$7 holding a reference (bit = task)
000000r 2                           mask_hi     .byte                               ; Tasks $8-$F
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Set up the shared memory tables: find which U macro-pages have RAM, clear the bitmaps and handle
000000r 2               ; table, and reserve the system and message ring banks.  Called by MMU_INIT at boot.
000000r 2               SHARED_RAM_INIT:
000000r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000001r 2  78                       sei
000002r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000005r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER                                    ; Save RAM bank / U; select shared bank ID $00
000009r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00000Dr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000015r 2               
000015r 2               ; Probe: write a marker into every U's bank $F0, highest U first, so if U decoding aliases, the
000015r 2               ; lower (real) macro-page's marker wins and the alias reads back the wrong marker.
000015r 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #15
000017r 2               
000017r 2               @mark:
000017r 2  8E F1 FF                 stx         U_REGISTER
00001Ar 2  8A                       txa
00001Br 2  09 50                    ora         #SH_PROBE_MARK
00001Dr 2  8D FF 9F                 sta         SH_PROBE_ADDR
000020r 2  CA                       dex
000021r 2  10 F4                    bpl         @mark
000023r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Present macro-pages, bit = U
000025r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000027r 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #15
000029r 2               
000029r 2               @check:
000029r 2  8E F1 FF                 stx         U_REGISTER
00002Cr 2  8A                       txa
00002Dr 2  09 50                    ora         #SH_PROBE_MARK
00002Fr 2  CD FF 9F                 cmp         SH_PROBE_ADDR
000032r 2  D0 14                    bne         @next                               ; Missing (or an alias)
000034r 2  DA                       phx
000035r 2  8A                       txa
000036r 2  4A                       lsr
000037r 2  4A                       lsr
000038r 2  4A                       lsr
000039r 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = mask byte
00003Ar 2  8A                       txa
00003Br 2  29 07                    and         #7
00003Dr 2  AA                       tax
00003Er 2  BD rr rr                 lda         MMU_BIT_MASKS,X
000041r 2  19 rr rr                 ora         ZP_TEMP_VEC,Y
000044r 2  99 rr rr                 sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC,Y
000047r 2  FA                       plx
000048r 2               
000048r 2               @next:
000048r 2  CA                       dex
000049r 2  10 DE                    bpl         @check
00004Br 2  A0 00                    ldy         #MSG_SHARED_U
00004Dr 2  8C F1 FF                 sty         U_REGISTER                          ; Back to shared bank ID $00
000050r 2               
000050r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0                                  ; Clear the bitmaps and handle table
000052r 2  A8                       tay
000053r 2               
000053r 2               @clear:
000053r 2  99 00 82                 sta         SH_MAP,Y
000056r 2  99 00 84                 sta         SH_HANDLES,Y
000059r 2  99 00 85                 sta         SH_HANDLES + $100,Y
00005Cr 2  99 00 86                 sta         SH_HANDLES + $200,Y
00005Fr 2  99 00 87                 sta         SH_HANDLES + $300,Y
000062r 2  99 00 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE,Y                      ; The IO device table ($8800, io.s)
000065r 2  C8                       iny
000066r 2  D0 EB                    bne         @clear
000068r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_BAD_SH                         ; Chips that failed the POST (post_ram.s): chip c
00006Ar 2  29 03                    and         #$03                                ;   = bank IDs 4c - 4c+3 of every U, so a nibble
00006Cr 2  AA                       tax                                             ;   of each U's two SH_MAP bytes
00006Dr 2  BD rr rr                 lda         SH_CHIP_MASKS,X
000070r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC2                        ; Bank IDs $x0-$x7: chips 0, 1
000072r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_BAD_SH
000074r 2  4A                       lsr
000075r 2  4A                       lsr
000076r 2  29 03                    and         #$03
000078r 2  AA                       tax
000079r 2  BD rr rr                 lda         SH_CHIP_MASKS,X
00007Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1                    ; Bank IDs $x8-$xF: chips 2, 3
00007Er 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Reserve the banks of missing macro-pages and bad chips
000080r 2               
000080r 2               @macro_pages:
000080r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF
000082r 2  A8                       tay
000083r 2  46 rr                    lsr         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1                     ; C = this U's present bit
000085r 2  66 rr                    ror         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000087r 2  90 04                    bcc         @reserve                            ; Missing: all of its banks
000089r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC2                        ; Present: the banks on bad chips
00008Br 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1
00008Dr 2               
00008Dr 2               @reserve:
00008Dr 2  9D 00 82                 sta         SH_MAP,X
000090r 2  98                       tya
000091r 2  9D 01 82                 sta         SH_MAP + 1,X
000094r 2  E8                       inx
000095r 2  E8                       inx
000096r 2  E0 20                    cpx         #32
000098r 2  D0 E6                    bne         @macro_pages

0000BAr 2               
0000BAr 2               ; Allocate shared memory (whole 8K banks); the calling task holds the first reference.
0000BAr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = size in bytes (1 - $FFFF; .A = low byte)
0000BAr 2               ; OUT (success): .A = shared handle, C = 0
0000BAr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG, ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES or ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, C = 1
0000BAr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
0000BAr 2               SH_ALLOC:
0000BAr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0000BBr 2  78                       sei
0000BCr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0000BEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SZ1                            ; Size low
0000C0r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Size high
0000C2r 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP2

0000CAr 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
0000CEr 2  00 8C F1 FF

0000DBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SZ1                            ; Banks = ((size - 1) >> 13) + 1

0000DFr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0000E1r 2  E9 00                    sbc         #0
0000E3r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E4r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E5r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E6r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E7r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E8r 2  1A                       inc
0000E9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV
0000EBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_MAPS_SETUP

0000F5r 2  B0 1E                    bcs         @leave
0000F7r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
0000F9r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0000FBr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0000FDr 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ShHandle::mask_lo
0000FFr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000101r 2  C8                       iny
000102r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y

000126r 2               
000126r 2               ; Take a reference to a shared allocation for the calling task (e.g. a handle received in a message).
000126r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle
000126r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000126r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1

000154r 2               
000154r 2               ; Drop the calling task's reference; the banks are freed when no task holds a reference.
000154r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle

000181r 2               
000181r 2               ; Read a byte of a shared allocation (the calling task must hold a reference).
000181r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle, .Y = offset (0-255, in the first bank)
000181r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = byte, C = 0

0001DCr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0001DFr 2               
0001DFr 2               ; Map a shared allocation's first bank into the $8000-$9FFF window (SH_WINDOW), for speed.  The calling
0001DFr 2               ; task must hold a reference.  Undo with SH_UNLOCK.
0001DFr 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle
0001DFr 2               ; OUT (success): .X = previous RAM bank, .Y = previous U (pass both to SH_UNLOCK), C = 0
0001DFr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1 (.X, .Y preserved)
0001DFr 2               SH_LOCK:
0001DFr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0001E0r 2  78                       sei

0001FDr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
0001FFr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000201r 2  7A                       ply                                             ; Previous U        } Don't restore them:
000202r 2  FA                       plx                                             ; Previous RAM bank } they're returned
000203r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_SELECT_BANK                      ; Map the allocation
000206r 2  68                       pla                                             ; Drop the caller's .Y
000207r 2  68                       pla                                             ; Drop the caller's .X
000208r 2  18                       clc
000209r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00020Cr 2               
00020Cr 2               @fail:
00020Cr 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
000210r 2  7A 84 rr     
000213r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000215r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000218r 2

000218r 2               ; IN: .X = RAM bank, .Y = U (from SH_LOCK)

000218r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000218r 2               SH_UNLOCK:

00021Fr 2

0002ACr 2               
0002ACr 2               ; Task ZP_M_TEMP's bit in an entry's mask.  OUT: .Y = ShHandle::mask_lo or mask_hi, .A = bit.  Modifies: .X

0002BEr 2

0002C5r 2  60                       rts
0002C6r 2

0002D2r 2  18                       clc

0002D4r 2

0002D4r 2  A9 41                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID
0002D6r 2  38                       sec
0002D7r 2  60                       rts
0002D8r 2               
0002D8r 2               ; Drop task ZP_M_TEMP's reference to entry ZP_M_HP; free the banks if it was the last reference.
0002D8r 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1 if the task had no reference.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y

0002E7r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002E9r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ShHandle::mask_lo
0002EBr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002EDr 2  C8                       iny

0002F2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_MAPS_SETUP                       ; Last reference: free the banks
0002F5r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
0002F7r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y

0002FEr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0

000304r 2

00030Ar 2               
00030Ar 2               ; Check the calling task's reference to shared handle .A and map its first bank.
00030Ar 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y

000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; IO subsystem, the BIOS ROM page 0 part (see IO_PLAN.md; the IO layer itself is io.s, on page 2).
000000r 2               ; Drivers (on page 0) use these: DEV_REGISTER, and IO_SRV_MAP / IO_SRV_UNMAP in their serve routines.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; The IO layer's calls, for page 0 callers and the $F8xx thunks (compact gates into page 2)
000000r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_OPEN,        PAGE2::IO_OPEN,         2
000004r 2  rr 02        
000006r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_CLOSE,       PAGE2::IO_CLOSE,        2
00000Ar 2  rr 02        
00000Cr 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_READ,        PAGE2::IO_READ,         2
000010r 2  rr 02        
000012r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_WRITE,       PAGE2::IO_WRITE,        2
000016r 2  rr 02        
000018r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_GETC,        PAGE2::IO_GETC,         2
00001Cr 2  rr 02        
00001Er 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_PUTC,        PAGE2::IO_PUTC,         2
000022r 2  rr 02        
000024r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_SEEK,        PAGE2::IO_SEEK,         2
000028r 2  rr 02        
00002Ar 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_STAT,        PAGE2::IO_STAT,         2
00002Er 2  rr 02        
000030r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_CTL,         PAGE2::IO_CTL,          2
000034r 2  rr 02        
000036r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_TEST,        PAGE2::IO_TEST,         2
00003Ar 2  rr 02        
00003Cr 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_DUP2,        PAGE2::IO_DUP2,         2
000040r 2  rr 02        
000042r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_PIPE,        PAGE2::IO_PIPE,         2
000046r 2  rr 02        
000048r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_STD_OPEN,    PAGE2::IO_STD_OPEN,     2
00004Cr 2  rr 02        
00004Er 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_CLOSE_ALL,   PAGE2::IO_CLOSE_ALL,    2
000052r 2  rr 02        
000054r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_INHERIT,     PAGE2::IO_INHERIT,      2
000058r 2  rr 02        
00005Ar 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_ADOPT_FDS,   PAGE2::IO_ADOPT_FDS,    2  ; (Run in a new task by IO_INHERIT)
00005Er 2  rr 02        
000060r 2               
000060r 2               ; Serve routines must be page 0 addresses (TASK_CALL runs them on page 0): gates to the page 2 servers
000060r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     NULL_SERVE,     PAGE2::NULL_SERVE,      2
000064r 2  rr 02        
000066r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     ZERO_SERVE,     PAGE2::ZERO_SERVE,      2
00006Ar 2  rr 02        
00006Cr 2               
00006Cr 2  6E 75 6C 6C  NULL_NAME:  .byte   "null", 0
000070r 2  00           
000071r 2  7A 65 72 6F  ZERO_NAME:  .byte   "zero", 0
000075r 2  00           
000076r 2               
000076r 2               ; The pipe server (pipe_srv.s): a Resident task (PIPE_TASK_NUM), started at boot like the drivers
000076r 2               PIPE_DRIVER:
000076r 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_INIT                           ; DriverInfo::init
000078r 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_STOP                           ; DriverInfo::stop
00007Ar 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_DNAME                          ; DriverInfo::name
00007Cr 2  04 50 49 50  NamedHString PIPE_DNAME, "PIPE"
000080r 2  45           
000081r 2  70 69 70 65  PIPE_NAME:  .byte   "pipe", 0
000085r 2  00           
000086r 2               
000086r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     PIPE_SERVE,     PAGE2::PIPE_SERVE,      2
00008Ar 2  rr 02        
00008Cr 2               
00008Cr 2               ; Runs in the pipe task: no pipes yet, and register /dev/pipe
00008Cr 2               PIPE_INIT:
00008Cr 2  A2 3F                    ldx         #PIPE_MAX * PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE - 1
00008Er 2               :
00008Er 2  9E 00 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE,X
000091r 2  CA                       dex
000092r 2  10 FA                    bpl         :-
000094r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   PIPE_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
000098r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00009Cr 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<PIPE_NAME
00009Er 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>PIPE_NAME
0000A0r 2  A2 0D                    ldx         #PIPE_TASK_NUM
0000A2r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         DEV_REGISTER
0000A5r 2               
0000A5r 2               PIPE_STOP:
0000A5r 2  18                       clc
0000A6r 2  60                       rts
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               ; Register the IO layer's own devices.  Called at boot, after SHARED_RAM_INIT (which clears the table).
0000A7r 2               IO_INIT:
0000A7r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   NULL_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
0000ABr 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
0000AFr 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<NULL_NAME
0000B1r 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>NULL_NAME
0000B3r 2  A2 FF                    ldx         #IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK
0000B5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         DEV_REGISTER
0000B8r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   ZERO_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
0000BCr 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
0000C0r 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<ZERO_NAME
0000C2r 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>ZERO_NAME
0000C4r 2  A2 FF                    ldx         #IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK
0000C6r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         DEV_REGISTER
0000C9r 2               
0000C9r 2               ; Register a device (a file server): /dev/<name> is served by the serve routine, running in a task.
0000C9r 2               ; Drivers call it from their init (which runs in the driver's task).
0000C9r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = name (zero-terminated, 1-8 characters; in RAM or on ROM page 0), .X = task the serve routine
0000C9r 2               ;     runs in (IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK = the task making each request), ZP_TC_VEC = serve routine (page 0)
0000C9r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = device index, C = 0
0000C9r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IO_NAME (empty or too long) or ERR_IO_NO_DEVS, C = 1
0000C9r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
0000C9r 2               DEV_REGISTER:
0000C9r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0000CAr 2  78                       sei
0000CBr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0000CDr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF                           ; The name
0000CFr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
0000D1r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; The task
0000D3r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_MSG_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00 (device table)
0000D7r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
0000DBr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
0000E3r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF)
0000E5r 2  F0 4E                    beq         @empty_name
0000E7r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Device table offset
0000E9r 2               
0000E9r 2               @find:
0000E9r 2  BD 00 88                 lda         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X
0000ECr 2  F0 0B                    beq         @free                               ; Free entry
0000EEr 2  8A                       txa
0000EFr 2  18                       clc
0000F0r 2  69 10                    adc         #IO_DEV_SIZE
0000F2r 2  AA                       tax
0000F3r 2  90 F4                    bcc         @find                               ; (16 entries x 16 bytes: ends at 256)
0000F5r 2  A9 76                    lda         #ERR_IO_NO_DEVS
0000F7r 2  80 3E                    bra         @fail
0000F9r 2               
0000F9r 2               @free:
0000F9r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CNT                           ; The entry's offset
0000FBr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0                                  ; Copy the name, zero-padded
0000FDr 2               
0000FDr 2               @name:
0000FDr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y
0000FFr 2  F0 0B                    beq         @pad
000101r 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_DEV_NAME_LEN
000103r 2  B0 2B                    bcs         @bad_name                           ; Too long
000105r 2  9D 00 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X
000108r 2  E8                       inx
000109r 2  C8                       iny
00010Ar 2  80 F1                    bra         @name
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2               @pad:
00010Cr 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_DEV_NAME_LEN
00010Er 2  B0 07                    bcs         @padded
000110r 2  9E 00 88                 stz         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X
000113r 2  E8                       inx
000114r 2  C8                       iny
000115r 2  80 F5                    bra         @pad
000117r 2               
000117r 2               @padded:
000117r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CNT                           ; Back to the entry's start
000119r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP
00011Br 2  9D 08 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_TASK,X
00011Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TC_VEC
000120r 2  9D 09 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_SERVE,X
000123r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
000125r 2  9D 0A 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_SERVE + 1,X
000128r 2  8A                       txa                                             ; Device index = offset / 16
000129r 2  4A                       lsr
00012Ar 2  4A                       lsr
00012Br 2  4A                       lsr
00012Cr 2  4A                       lsr
00012Dr 2  18                       clc
00012Er 2  80 08                    bra         @done
000130r 2               
000130r 2               @bad_name:
000130r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CNT
000132r 2  9E 00 88                 stz         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X        ; Leave the entry free
000135r 2               
000135r 2               @empty_name:
000135r 2  A9 77                    lda         #ERR_IO_NAME
000137r 2               
000137r 2               @fail:
000137r 2  38                       sec
000138r 2               
000138r 2               @done:
000138r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_MSG_LEAVE
00013Cr 2  7A 84 rr     
00013Fr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000141r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000144r 2               
000144r 2               ; Server side: map the client's request block (in its IO transfer area) into $8000-$9FFF, in the
000144r 2               ; server's task.  Undo with IO_SRV_UNMAP before returning from the serve routine.
000144r 2               ; IN: .X = client task.  OUT: ZP_IO_REQ = the request block (data at ZP_IO_REQ + IO_BLK_DATA)
000144r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000144r 2               IO_SRV_MAP:
000144r 2  48                       pha
000145r 2  A5 rr                    lda         RAM_BANK_REG
000147r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_SAVEB
000149r 2  AD F1 FF                 lda         U_REGISTER
00014Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_SAVEU
00014Er 2  9C F1 FF                 stz         U_REGISTER
000151r 2  A9 F9                    lda         #IO_XFER_BANK
000153r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000155r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_REQ
000157r 2  8A                       txa
000158r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
00015Ar 2  0A                       asl                                             ; $8000 + task * $200
00015Br 2  09 80                    ora         #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
00015Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
00015Fr 2  68                       pla
000160r 2  60                       rts
000161r 2               
000161r 2               ; Undo IO_SRV_MAP.  Preserves .A, .X, .Y and C
000161r 2               IO_SRV_UNMAP:
000161r 2  48                       pha
000162r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_SAVEU
000164r 2  8D F1 FF                 sta         U_REGISTER
000167r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_SAVEB
000169r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
00016Br 2  68                       pla
00016Cr 2  60                       rts
00016Dr 2

00016Dr 2               .debuginfo
00016Dr 2               .segment "SHELL"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SHELL_MAIN:
000000r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IO_STD_OPEN         ; fds 0-2 on /dev/cons (inherited by the tasks the shell starts)
000003r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     COPYTORAM
