00000Fr 1               .include "sound.s"
00000Fr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00000Fr 2               ; Sound driver.  Runs in its own Resident task (SOUND_TASK_NUM, started by DRV_START at boot), so its
00000Fr 2               ; ZP is task ZP.  Other tasks call it through the SND_CALL_* gates, which run the routine in the
00000Fr 2               ; sound task with TASK_CALL, or through its file, /dev/snd (snd_srv.s, on ROM page 2).
00000Fr 2               
00000Fr 2               SOUND_DRIVER:
00000Fr 2  rr rr                        .word       SOUND_DRV_INIT              ; DriverInfo::init
000011r 2  rr rr                        .word       SOUND_STOP                  ; DriverInfo::stop
000013r 2  rr rr                        .word       SOUND_NAME                  ; DriverInfo::name
000015r 2  05 53 4F 55  NamedHString SOUND_NAME, "SOUND"
000019r 2  4E 44        
00001Br 2  73 6E 64 00  SND_NAME:       .byte       "snd", 0
00001Fr 2               
00001Fr 2               ; Gates into the sound task: .A/.X/.Y/C pass through to the routine and back
00001Fr 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE           SND_CALL_INIT, SOUND_INIT, SOUND_TASK_NUM
000023r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000027r 2  rr A9 0E 85  
000030r 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE           SND_CALL_TEST, SOUND_TEST, SOUND_TASK_NUM
000034r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000038r 2  rr A9 0E 85  
000041r 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE           SND_CALL_YM_WRITE, YM_WRITE, SOUND_TASK_NUM
000045r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000049r 2  rr A9 0E 85  
000052r 2               
000052r 2               ; The serve routine (page 2, snd_srv.s)
000052r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     SND_SERVE,      PAGE2::SND_SERVE,       2
000056r 2  rr 02        
000058r 2               
000058r 2               ; Driver init (runs in the sound task): the chip, then the file.  OUT: C = 0, or C = 1 and .A = error
000058r 2               SOUND_DRV_INIT:
000058r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         SOUND_INIT
00005Br 2  A9 rr 85 rr                  LOAD_ADDR   SND_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
00005Fr 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000063r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SND_NAME
000065r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SND_NAME
000067r 2  A2 0E                        ldx         #SOUND_TASK_NUM
000069r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp         DEV_REGISTER
00006Cr 2               
00006Cr 2               ; zero out all YM-2151 registers $28-$FF
00006Cr 2               SOUND_INIT:
00006Cr 2  48                           pha
00006Dr 2  DA                           phx
00006Er 2  A9 00                        lda         #0
000070r 2  A2 14                        ldx         #$14        ; turn off the clocks
000072r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000075r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28
000077r 2               
000077r 2               @write_z:
000077r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00007Ar 2  B0 0C                        bcs         @error
00007Cr 2  E8                           inx
00007Dr 2  D0 F8                        bne         @write_z
00007Fr 2  A2 03                        ldx         #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SOUND
000081r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000083r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000085r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         IRQ_REGISTER            ; Handler runs in this (the sound) task
000088r 2               
000088r 2               @error:
000088r 2  FA                           plx
000089r 2  68                           pla
00008Ar 2  60                           rts
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2               SOUND_STOP:
00008Br 2  18                           clc
00008Cr 2  60                           rts
00008Dr 2               
00008Dr 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if the interrupt was claimed
00008Dr 2               SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER:
00008Dr 2                               ; check which
00008Dr 2  18                           clc
00008Er 2  60                           rts
00008Fr 2               
00008Fr 2               TASK_ZP_BEGIN

0000F7r 2               SCOM_NOTES_0:   ;            D4,  D4,  E4,  E4,  G4,  G4,  D4,  D4
0000F7r 2                               ;.byte       $41, $41, $44, $44, $48, $48, $41, $41
0000F7r 2  40 40 42 42                  .byte       $40, $40, $42, $42, $46, $46, $40, $40 ; half-note flat, just like Slash

0000FFr 2

0000FFr 2                               ;            D5,  A4,  G4,  G5,  A4, F#5,  A4

0000FFr 2  50 49 46 56                  .byte       $50, $49, $46, $56, $49, $55, $49   ; half-note flat
000103r 2  49 55 49     
000106r 2               
000106r 2               SOUND_TEST:
000106r 2                               ; Set basic FM patch (simple sine-like sound)
000106r 2  A2 20                        ldx         #$20
000108r 2  A9 C7                        lda         #$C7
00010Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00010Dr 2               
00010Dr 2  A2 80                        ldx         #$80
00010Fr 2  A9 1F                        lda         #$1F
000111r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000114r 2               
000114r 2  A2 E0                        ldx         #$E0
000116r 2  A9 0F                        lda         #$0F
000118r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00011Br 2               
00011Br 2  A2 21                        ldx         #$21
00011Dr 2  A9 C7                        lda         #$C7
00011Fr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000122r 2               
000122r 2  A2 81                        ldx         #$81
000124r 2  A9 1F                        lda         #$1F
000126r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000129r 2               
000129r 2  A2 E1                        ldx         #$E1
00012Br 2  A9 0F                        lda         #$0F
00012Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000130r 2               
000130r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Channel 0
000132r 2  A2 rr                        ldx         #<GUITAR_PATCH
000134r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>GUITAR_PATCH
000136r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_LOADPATCH
000139r 2               
000139r 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; Channel 1
00013Br 2  A2 rr                        ldx         #<GUITAR_PATCH
00013Dr 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>GUITAR_PATCH
00013Fr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_LOADPATCH
000142r 2               
000142r 2  A2 60                        ldx         #$60    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 0
000144r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
000146r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000149r 2               
000149r 2  A2 61                        ldx         #$61    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 1
00014Br 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
00014Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000150r 2               
000150r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_0
000152r 2  85 FC                        sta         YMN0L
000154r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_0
000156r 2  85 FD                        sta         YMN0H
000158r 2               
000158r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_1
00015Ar 2  85 FE                        sta         YMN1L
00015Cr 2  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_1
00015Er 2  85 FF                        sta         YMN1H
000160r 2               
000160r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; counter for channel 0
000162r 2               @play_loop:
000162r 2                               ; 0. set outer counter to 0
000162r 2                               ; 1. key off channel 0
000162r 2                               ; 1a.  key off on channel 1
000162r 2                               ; 2. set note to next note in channel 0 list
000162r 2                               ; 3. key on on channel 0
000162r 2                               ; 4. one delay
000162r 2                               ; 5. notes for rest of sequence on channel 1
000162r 2                               ; 5a.  reset counter to 0
000162r 2                               ; 5b.  key off on channel 1
000162r 2                               ; 5c.  set note on channel 1
000162r 2                               ; 5d.  key on on channel 1
000162r 2                               ; 5e.  one delay
000162r 2                               ; 5f.  increment counter
000162r 2                               ; 5g.  loop until 7 notes have been played on channel 1
000162r 2                               ; 6.  increment outer counter to play next start note on channel 0
000162r 2                               ; 7.  loop back to 1 until outer count reaches 8 (8 sequences have played)
000162r 2                               ; Key Off (silence previous note)
000162r 2               
000162r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
000164r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; CH0 off
000166r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000169r 2               
000169r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00016Br 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
00016Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000170r 2               
000170r 2                               ; Set frequency (KC)
000170r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28    ; KC register for CH0
000172r 2  B1 FC                        lda         (YMN0L),y
000174r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000177r 2               
000177r 2                               ; Key On
000177r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
000179r 2  A9 78                        lda         #$78    ; CH0 on
00017Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00017Er 2               
00017Er 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
00017Er 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
000180r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
000183r 2               
000183r 2  5A                           phy
000184r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0
000186r 2  80 07                        bra         @skip_ch1_off   ; already off at the start of the loop
000188r 2               
000188r 2               @next_ch1:
000188r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00018Ar 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
00018Cr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00018Fr 2               
00018Fr 2               @skip_ch1_off:
00018Fr 2                               ; Set frequency (KC)
00018Fr 2  A2 29                        ldx         #$29    ; KC register for CH1
000191r 2  B1 FE                        lda         (YMN1L),y
000193r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000196r 2               
000196r 2                               ; Key On
000196r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
000198r 2  A9 79                        lda         #$79    ; CH1 on
00019Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00019Dr 2               
00019Dr 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
00019Dr 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
00019Fr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
0001A2r 2               
0001A2r 2  C8                           iny
0001A3r 2  C0 07                        cpy         #7
0001A5r 2  D0 E1                        bne         @next_ch1
0001A7r 2               
0001A7r 2                               ; Next note on CH0
0001A7r 2  7A                           ply                 ; pull CH0 count
0001A8r 2  C8                           iny
0001A9r 2  C0 08                        cpy         #8      ; 8 bars
0001ABr 2  D0 B5                        bne         @play_loop
0001ADr 2               
0001ADr 2                               ; Stop sound
0001ADr 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
0001AFr 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00
0001B1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0001B4r 2               
0001B4r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
0001B6r 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01
0001B8r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp         YM_WRITE
0001BBr 2               
0001BBr 2               YM_TIMEOUT = 64 * CPU_CLOCK_MULT    ; Busy-wait loop count (<= 128: the loop ends when .Y goes negative)
0001BBr 2               
0001BBr 2               ; Write value in A to YM-2151 register in X
0001BBr 2               YM_WRITE:
0001BBr 2  78                           sei
0001BCr 2  5A                           phy
0001BDr 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
0001BFr 2               
0001BFr 2               @ym_wait1:
0001BFr 2  88                           dey
0001C0r 2  30 18                        bmi         @timeout
0001C2r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
0001C5r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait1
0001C7r 2  8E rr rr                     stx         YM_REG
0001CAr 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
0001CCr 2               
0001CCr 2               @ym_wait2:
0001CCr 2  88                           dey
0001CDr 2  30 0B                        bmi         @timeout
0001CFr 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
0001D2r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait2
0001D4r 2  8D rr rr                     sta         YM_DATA
0001D7r 2  18                           clc
0001D8r 2  80 01                        bra         @cleanup
0001DAr 2               
0001DAr 2               @timeout:
0001DAr 2  38                           sec
0001DBr 2               
0001DBr 2               @cleanup:
0001DBr 2  7A                           ply
0001DCr 2  58                           cli
0001DDr 2  60                           rts
0001DEr 2               
0001DEr 2               YM_LOADPATCH:
0001DEr 2                               ; Make re-entrant safe by protecting tmp and pointer variables from interrupt
0001DEr 2  08                           php
0001DFr 2  78                           sei
0001E0r 2               
0001E0r 2                               ; and #$07 ; mask channel to range 0..7
0001E0r 2  86 F8                        stx AZP0L
0001E2r 2  84 F9                        sty AZP0H
0001E4r 2  18                           clc
0001E5r 2  69 20                        adc #$20 ; first byte of patch goes to YM:$20+channel
0001E7r 2  AA                           tax
0001E8r 2               
0001E8r 2  B2 F8                        lda (AZP0L)
0001EAr 2  29 3F                        and #$3F
0001ECr 2               
0001ECr 2                               ;sta ymtmp1
0001ECr 2                               ;lda ymshadow,x
0001ECr 2                               ;and #$C0 ; L+R bits for YM channel
0001ECr 2                               ;ora ymtmp1 ; Add the patch byte without L+R
0001ECr 2  09 C0                        ora #$C0;
0001EEr 2               
0001EEr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
0001F1r 2  B0 16                        bcs @fail
0001F3r 2  A0 00                        ldy #0
0001F5r 2  8A                           txa         ; YM_WRITE preserves X (YM register)
0001F6r 2                               ; Now skip over $28 and $30 by adding $10 to the register address.
0001F6r 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful ym_write
0001F6r 2  69 10                        adc #$10
0001F8r 2  AA                           tax         ; set up for loop
0001F9r 2               @next:
0001F9r 2  8A                           txa
0001FAr 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful YM_WRITE
0001FAr 2  69 08                        adc #$08
0001FCr 2  B0 0E                        bcs @success
0001FEr 2  C8                           iny
0001FFr 2  AA                           tax
000200r 2  B1 F8                        lda (AZP0L),y
000202r 2  5A                           phy         ; YM_WRITE clobbers .Y
000203r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000206r 2  7A                           ply
000207r 2  90 F0                        bcc @next
000209r 2               @fail:
000209r 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
00020Ar 2  38                           sec
00020Br 2  60                           rts         ; return C set as failed patch write.
00020Cr 2               @success:
00020Cr 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
00020Dr 2  18                           clc
00020Er 2  60                           rts
00020Fr 2               
00020Fr 2               ; YM_DELAY_64 - Delay subroutine for musical note lengths
00020Fr 2               ; A: number of 64th-note delays to wait (a 64th note @120 BPM = ~ 111,861 clock cycles @ 3.57955 MHz)
00020Fr 2               ; The loop below is tuned for 3.58 MHz; at a faster CPU clock it runs CPU_CLOCK_MULT times.
00020Fr 2               YM_DELAY_64:
00020Fr 2               .if CPU_CLOCK_MULT > 1
00020Fr 2                               pha
00020Fr 2                               jsr         YM_DELAY_64_1
00020Fr 2                               pla
00020Fr 2               .endif
00020Fr 2               
00020Fr 2               YM_DELAY_64_1:
00020Fr 2  DA 5A                        PUSH_XY
000211r 2  85 FA                        sta         YMTMP1
000213r 2               
000213r 2               @far_outer:
000213r 2  A9 01                        lda         #1          ; 2 cycles
000215r 2  A2 4B                        ldx         #75
000217r 2  A0 8E                        ldy         #142
000219r 2  80 04                        bra         @inner
00021Br 2               
00021Br 2               @outer:
00021Br 2  A2 00                        ldx         #0          ; 2 cycles
00021Dr 2               
00021Dr 2               @mid:
00021Dr 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; 2 cycles
00021Fr 2               
00021Fr 2               @inner:
00021Fr 2  88                           dey                     ; 2 cycles
000220r 2  D0 FD                        bne         @inner      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000222r 2  CA                           dex                     ; 2 cycles
000223r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @mid        ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000225r 2               
000225r 2                               ; Fine-tuning at end of inner loop
000225r 2  3A                           dec                     ; 2 cycles
000226r 2  D0 F3                        bne         @outer      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000228r 2  C6 FA                        dec         YMTMP1      ; 3 cycles
00022Ar 2  D0 E7                        bne         @far_outer  ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
00022Cr 2  7A FA                        PULL_YX
00022Er 2  60                           rts                     ; 6 cycles
00022Fr 2

000000r 2               RESET_VECTOR_START:
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              ZERO_W
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2  D8                       cld                                                     ; We don't like decimal mode
000006r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta                 T_REGISTER                          ; Make sure task 0 is selected

00000Br 2  85 01                    sta                 $01                                 ; Init ROM Bank selector

00000Fr 2  9A                       txs
000010r 2

000016r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 TASKS_INIT                          ; Must be called before the drivers and MMU_INIT
000019r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 MMU_INIT

000043r 2               ; hand the CPU over

000055r 2               
000055r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000055r 2               ; Power-on self test.  Checks the memory mapping the task system depends on, using only polled serial
000055r 2               ; output (no IRQs, no drivers) and task switches that don't touch the stack.  Prints e.g.:
000055r 2               ;       POST ZP:T ST:T LO:T 7D:T SH:S P1:4C
000055r 2               ;   ZP/ST/LO/7D: $0080 / $0180 / $0280 / $7D80 are per-Task (T) or common to all tasks (C)
000055r 2               ;   SH: shared RAM bank $F0 (U = 0) is Shared between tasks (S) or not (X)
000055r 2               ;   P1: byte at $EA00 on ROM page 1 (4C expected: the jmp at forth_main)
000055r 2               ; and a second line of paged RAM line tests (hex masks of bad lines, all 0 when good):
000055r 2               ;       RAM U:0 F0:0/00/0000 F4:0/00/0000 F8:0/00/0000 FC:0/00/0000 00:0/00/0000 10:0/00/0000 ...
000055r 2               ;   U:x      U lines U0-U3 (bit n = Un), tested on shared bank $F0
000055r 2               ;   bb:x/dd/aaaa  bank bb at $8000-$9FFF: bank register lines 0-3 (bb ^ 1/2/4/8), data lines D0-D7 and
000055r 2               ;            address lines A0-A12.  Tested: the first bank of each shared RAM chip ($F0, $F4, $F8, $FC, with
000055r 2               ;            U = 0; a missing chip shows as bad lines) and of each installed task RAM module.
000055r 2               ;   Destructive: run before anything is kept in paged RAM.
000055r 2               POST_ACIA_CMD   = ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLID | ACIA_CMD_BIT_RID    ; No IRQs
000055r 2               
000055r 2               .macro _M_POST_TASK_TEST    addr, label
000055r 2                           ldx                 #label - POST_STRINGS
000055r 2                           jsr                 POST_PUTS
000055r 2                           lda                 #$A5
000055r 2                           stz                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0 (no stack use until back in task 0)
000055r 2                           sta                 addr
000055r 2                           ldx                 #1
000055r 2                           stx                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 1
000055r 2                           lda                 #$5A
000055r 2                           sta                 addr
000055r 2                           stz                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0
000055r 2                           lda                 addr
000055r 2                           ldx                 #'T'
000055r 2                           cmp                 #$A5
000055r 2                           beq                 :+
000055r 2                           ldx                 #'C'
000055r 2               :
000055r 2                           txa
000055r 2                           jsr                 POST_PUTC
000055r 2               .endmacro
000055r 2               
000055r 2               POST:
000055r 2  08                       php
000056r 2  78                       sei
000057r 2  A9 1F                    lda                 #$10 | SR_SELECT                    ; 8-N-1
000059r 2  8D rr rr                 sta                 ACIA_R_CTRL
00005Cr 2  A9 0B                    lda                 #POST_ACIA_CMD
00005Er 2  8D rr rr                 sta                 ACIA_R_CMD

000065r 2  rr A9 A5 9C  
000069r 2  F0 FF 85 80

000102r 2

000104r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTS

00010Fr 2  86 rr                    stx                 RAM_BANK_REG
000111r 2  A9 C3                    lda                 #$C3

00012Dr 2  A2 58                    ldx                 #'X'

000135r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTS
000138r 2  A9 EA 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR           $EA00, ZP_D_XAM
00013Cr 2  A9 00 85 rr  
000140r 2  A9 01                    lda                 #1
000142r 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_D_PAGE
000144r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 PEEK_D_XAM                          ; Byte at $EA00 on ROM page 1

000149r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
00014Cr 2

00014Fr 2  A2 24                    ldx                 #POST_S_CRLF - POST_STRINGS

0001F4r 2               
0001F4r 2               ; A: S/W interrupt number
0001F4r 2               ; Preserves .X and V
0001F4r 2               SW_INT:
0001F4r 2  08                       php                                                     ; No task switch while V is ours (BRK runs
0001F5r 2  78                       sei                                                     ;   even with IRQs off)
0001F6r 2  DA                       phx
0001F7r 2  AE F2 FF                 ldx                 V_REGISTER                          ; Save V (shared pseudo-register)
0001FAr 2  0A                       asl                                                     ; move int# to V[4..7]
0001FBr 2  0A                       asl
0001FCr 2  0A                       asl
0001FDr 2  0A                       asl
0001FEr 2  09 08                    ora                 #IRQ_NUMBER_SW                      ; S/W IRQ vector in V[0..3]
000200r 2  8D F2 FF                 sta                 V_REGISTER
000203r 2  00                       brk                                                     ; force an interrupt
000204r 2  00                       .byte               $00                                 ; BRK signature byte (RTI returns past it)
000205r 2  8E F2 FF                 stx                 V_REGISTER                          ; Restore prior V
000208r 2  FA                       plx
000209r 2  28                       plp
00020Ar 2  60                       rts
00020Br 2               
00020Br 2               ; Start of every other BIOS page: the RESET entry at $E000 zeroes W, and execution continues on page 0
00020Br 2               ; right after RESET_VECTOR_START's ZERO_W.  The rest of each page is filled with NOPs by the linker
00020Br 2               ; (fillval), except for the COMMON block and the vectors.
00020Br 2               .macro OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
00020Br 2                           ZERO_W
00020Br 2               .endmacro
00020Br 2               
00020Br 2               .segment "BIOS_P1"                                                  ; HyForth and the disassembler follow (page1.s)
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF

000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P3"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P4"
